### [1] [Having Dog Ears "for Real": Effects of Active and Passive Haptics on Embodying Non-Human Body Parts in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26364)
*Omar A. Khan,Bao Han Trinh,Lee Lisle,Tiffany D. Do*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过问卷调查和2x2被试内实验，发现被动触觉（物理头带）比主动触觉（振动手套）更能增强对VR中非人类身体部位（狗耳朵）的拥有感，且组合两种触觉模态反而降低了被动触觉的益处。非人类身体部位的拥有感与整个虚拟化身拥有感正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR中非人类身体部位的具身化在某些亚文化中很普遍，但真实与虚拟身体形态差异会降低拥有感。触觉反馈可补偿这种降低，但不同触觉模态及其组合对拥有感的影响尚缺乏比较研究。

Method: 1) 向社交VR社区发放在线问卷(n=63)，确定动物耳朵是常见且生态效度高的非人类身体部位；2) 开展2x2被试内用户研究(n=28)，自变量为主动触觉（振动触觉手套）和被动触觉（物理头带），因变量为拥有感及相关指标。

Result: 1) 被动触觉产生最强的整体拥有感；2) 组合两种触觉模态反而削弱了被动触觉的益处；3) 非人类身体部位的拥有感与整个虚拟化身的拥有感正相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果对VR化身设计、触觉反馈技术应用及拥有感理论具有启示意义。

Abstract: Embodying non-human body parts in VR is a prevalent practice among certain subcultures and is a personally important creative outlet to many individuals. However, the discrepant morphology between real and virtual bodies can decrease Sense of Embodiment (SoE). Haptic feedback can compensate by increasing SoE felt towards non-human body parts, but there is a literature gap in comparing the effects of different haptic modalities, and their combinations, on SoE. Through an online survey sent out to social VR communities (n = 63), we determined that animal ears are a commonly embodied and ecologically valid non-human body part to study. We then ran a 2x2 within-subjects user study (n = 28) with two independent variables: active haptics, delivered through vibrotactile gloves, and passive haptics, delivered through a physical headband, for when participants reach up to touch virtual dog ears appended to their avatar in VR. Our findings show that (1) passive haptics produced the strongest overall embodiment outcomes, (2) combining modalities reduced the benefits of passive haptics, and (3) SoE towards non-human body parts positively correlates with SoE towards the entire avatar. We discuss implications of our findings in various domains, and on embodiment literature.

</details>


### [2] [Assistive Visual Cues for Visual Neglect Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26407)
*Per Bjerre,Andreas Køllund Pedersen,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了内源性和外源性线索对视觉忽视患者的影响，发现内源性线索能更好地帮助患者关注被忽视区域，在某些情况下组合线索效果更佳，但患者间表现差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注正常视力人群的线索引导注意机制，而视觉忽视患者难以注意部分视野，现有治疗方法需更强视觉线索，因此需要专门研究不同线索类型对该群体的效果。

Method: 通过对比实验，分别测试内源性线索（目标驱动的自上而下注意）和外源性线索（刺激驱动的自下而上注意）对视觉忽视患者注视被忽视区域的影响，并考察两者组合的效果。

Result: 视觉忽视患者在内源性线索条件下表现更好，目标在被忽视区域时效果尤佳；部分患者中组合线索可进一步提升表现；但个体差异显著，示例患者的最佳内源性线索平均采集时间3.5秒，最佳外源性6.5秒，组合后提升至2.8秒。

Conclusion: 内源性线索对视觉忽视患者更有效，组合线索有潜力进一步改善，但需考虑患者个体差异性，个性化治疗方案至关重要。

Abstract: Previous research on exogenous and endogenous cues has shown how they direct attention and improve interaction speed and error rate in applications. However, most studies focus on people with normal sight. People suffering from visual neglect have difficulties attending to parts of the visual field. One treatment method calls for the use of strong visual cues to remind patients of their neglected area and help guide their attention to it. Therefore, we examine the effects of endogenous and exogenous cues on visual neglect patients. Our results showed that visual neglect patients perform better with endogenous cues, when targets are within their neglected area. In some cases, combining exogenous and endogenous cues improve performance further. However, the performance varies greatly between patients. Using one neglect patient as an example, we saw that the best endogenous cue had an average acquisition time of 3.5 seconds compared to 6.5 for the best exogenous. Combining exogenous and endogenous cues further improved acquisition time to 2.8 seconds.

</details>


### [3] [TinyCNNDeep: Lightweight Attention-Based CNN for EEG Classification of Eye States and Sleep Deprivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26506)
*Thien Nhan Vo,Yen Nhi Tran Thi,Ngan Nguyen Xuan Phuong,Xuan-The Tran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyCNNDeep，一种结合残差学习和SE注意力机制的轻量级CNN模型，将多通道EEG短片段转换为224×224灰度图像，用于四分类（睡眠状态×眼状态）任务。在35名受试者数据集上达到83.69%的平均准确率，远超基线方法（RF，47.66%），而EEGNet等经典架构接近随机水平。混淆矩阵显示残差误分类集中在闭眼状态跨睡眠条件之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠剥夺会损害警觉性和认知功能，但联合识别睡眠条件（正常vs剥夺）和眼状态（睁眼vs闭眼）在EEG研究中尚不充分，现有方法缺乏对这一四分类问题的有效建模。

Method: 使用TinyCNNDeep轻量级CNN，结合残差学习和Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)通道注意力模块；将Fp1、Fp2、O1、Oz、O2五个通道的短EEG片段经过Z-score归一化、min-max缩放和中心填充，转换为224×224灰度图像，利用2D卷积联合建模通道间和时间结构。

Result: 在35名受试者的正常睡眠与睡眠剥夺数据集上，TinyCNNDeep达到83.69%的受试者-wise平均准确率，比最强基线（随机森林结合时频特征，47.66%）高出36.03个百分点；EEGNet、ShallowConvNet、DeepConvNet等经典架构表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 基于图像的EEG表示结合残差特征提取和通道注意力机制，在最小电极配置下为多类睡眠相关EEG分类提供了准确且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Sleep deprivation impairs vigilance and cognitive function, yet jointly identifying the sleep condition (normal vs deprived) and the eye state (open vs closed) from electroencephalography (EEG) remains underexplored. We address this four-class problem with TinyCNNDeep, a lightweight convolutional neural network that combines residual learning with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) attention module. We convert short multi-channel EEG segments from five physiologically relevant channels (Fp1, Fp2, O1, Oz, O2) into 224x224 grayscale images through per-channel Z-score normalization, min-max scaling, and center padding, enabling 2D convolutions to jointly model inter-channel and temporal structure. On a 35-subject dataset recorded under normal-sleep and sleep-deprivation sessions, TinyCNNDeep attains a subject-wise mean accuracy of 83.69%, outperforming the strongest baseline (Random Forest with combined time-frequency features, 47.66%) by 36.03 percentage points, while three established EEG architectures (EEGNet, ShallowConvNet, DeepConvNet) operate near chance. Per-subject analysis quantifies inter-subject variability, and confusion-matrix inspection shows that residual misclassifications concentrate between eyes-closed states across sleep conditions. These results indicate that an image-based EEG representation paired with residual feature extraction and channel attention provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for multiclass sleep-related EEG classification under a minimal electrode configuration.

</details>


### [4] [DanceDuo: Bridging Human Movement and AI Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26507)
*Gia-Cat Bui-Le,Tuong-Vy Truong-Thuy,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DanceDuo是一个基于扩散模型的音乐驱动舞蹈生成平台，支持用户选择音乐、人形模型、导入个人舞蹈视频进行对比，并通过人体姿态估计提供AI生成与用户表演的对比分析。用户研究显示界面直观，舞蹈对比功能备受好评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用深度学习和生成模型的最新进展，开发一个能够根据多种音乐风格生成AI编舞序列的平台，鼓励舞蹈练习，并为用户提供丰富的交互体验。

Method: 采用扩散模型生成与音乐同步的舞蹈序列；集成人体姿态估计模型实现用户表演与AI生成序列的对比分析；设计可交互界面支持音乐选择、人形模型选择和用户个人视频导入。

Result: 用户研究表明界面直观易用，舞蹈对比功能获得用户高度好评。

Conclusion: DanceDuo显著推动了AI在舞蹈编排领域的应用，为休闲和专业场景提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: In recent years, advancements in deep learning and generative models have revolutionized music-driven dance generation. This paper introduces a novel platform, namely DanceDuo, leveraging diffusion models to generate AI-choreographed dance sequences synchronized with a variety of music genres, to encourage dancing practice. The system allows users to interact with AI by selecting music tracks, humanoid models, and importing personal dance videos for comparison, fostering a rich and engaging user experience. DanceDuo not only offers dance generation but also integrates human pose estimation models to provide users with insightful comparisons of their own performances with AI-generated sequences. We conducted a comprehensive user study, revealing that users found the interface intuitive, with particular praise for the dance comparison feature. Our DanceDuo contributes significantly to the integration of AI in dance choreography, offering novel avenues for both recreational and professional applications.

</details>


### [5] [Budget-Aware Keyboardless Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26508)
*Quang-Thang Nguyen,Gia-Phuc Song-Dong,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅使用标准摄像头和打印键盘布局纸张的虚拟键盘系统，结合现代计算机视觉技术与传统图像处理算法，通过指甲颜色分析实现按键检测，无需复杂校准或特殊光照条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输入设备限制了用户的移动性，而现有虚拟键盘方案通常需要复杂设置或昂贵设备，因此需要一种简单、低成本且易于部署的虚拟键盘解决方案。

Method: 使用标准摄像头捕捉包含打印键盘布局纸张的图像；结合现代分割与检测模型以及传统图像处理算法识别键盘区域；通过分析用户指甲颜色变化实现触摸检测与按键定位。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出的键盘和按键检测方案在实际应用中具有良好效果；参与用户研究的受试者对该系统表现出兴趣。

Conclusion: 该系统作为一种低成本、易部署的虚拟键盘方案，在实用性和用户体验方面均展现出潜力。

Abstract: Interacting with computers typically relies on traditional input devices such as keyboards, mice, and monitors, which can be cumbersome for users seeking greater mobility. Virtual keyboards have been explored to address these limitations, but they often involve complex setups or expensive equipment. This paper proposes a novel virtual keyboard system that leverages only a standard camera and a paper with a printed keyboard layout. Unlike previous methods requiring complex calibration or special lighting conditions, our approach can work on standard environment using modern computer vision technologies. Combining modern segmentation and detection models with traditional image processing algorithms, we efficiently identify the keyboard region. Touch detection is performed using an algorithm analyzing the color of the user's fingernail. Experiments demonstrated a promising results our proposed solution of keyboard and keystroke detection for practical applications. Participants attended our user study also found the proposed system interesting.

</details>


### [6] [Co-Designing Community-Centered AI Education for Adults: A Midwestern Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26565)
*Yao Lyu,Leonymae Aumentado,Holden Winton,Jared Lee Katzman,Sparkle Berry,Zachary Rowe,Kimberly Sanders,Tawanna R. Dillahunt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究针对美国中西部城市东区非裔美国人社区的54名成人（48人线下，6人线上）开展AI教育案例研究，发现参与者的AI担忧从泛化转向具体、基于本地的问题，并提出应构建扎根于社区情境、增强社区能力的AI素养框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育日益重要，但针对高等教育之外成年人的AI教育关注不足，尤其缺乏在社区层面理解AI素养的实证研究。

Method: 与社区伙伴共同设计AI教育课程，面向54名成人（48人线下+6人线上）开展案例研究，采用参与式方法收集数据。

Result: 参与者对AI的担忧并未消失，但转变为更具体、与本地情境紧密相关的问题，关注AI的设计与部署如何影响其社区。

Conclusion: 应从社区能力视角理解AI素养，AI素养框架需要扎根于地方社区情境，以增强社区应对AI变革的能力。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) education is increasingly important, yet adults outside higher education receive less attention. We report a case study of an AI education session with 54 adults (48 in-person and 6 virtual) in a predominantly African American community on the east side of a major Midwestern city. We ask: "What does AI education for adults outside formal educational systems look like in practice?" and "What does this AI education session reveal about AI literacy at the community level?" Through a co-designed session developed with community partners, we found that concerns about AI persisted but shifted to specific, locally grounded questions about AI design and deployment. We also discuss AI literacy from a community capacity perspective and argue for AI literacy frameworks grounded in local community contexts that strengthen community capacity.

</details>


### [7] [An exploratory behavioral and electroencephalographic study of artificial intelligence-assisted learning modes in high school students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26579)
*Kashika Khurana,Ally Liew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过48名青少年的脑电图和行为数据分析，发现AI交互模式（导师、协作者、解决者）在行为指标上存在显著差异，但脑电图数据未达统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将AI接触视为二元条件（有AI vs. 无AI），缺乏对交互模式差异和暴露后效应的区分，需要更细粒度地探究不同AI交互模式对认知参与和问题解决行为的影响。

Method: 48名参与者（14-18岁，男女各半）在组内设计中完成两次平衡测验，记录额叶脑电图频谱活动和量化行为指标（启动、处理、压力），使用Friedman检验、重复测量方差分析、配对t检验和效应量计算进行统计分析。

Result: 每种AI交互模式在三个行为指标上均表现出显著差异；脑电图模式呈现描述性差异但未达统计显著性；初步发现了AI的短期遗留效应可能性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步的行为证据和电生理观察结果，表明不同AI交互模式可能影响神经活动和行为，并建立了未来人机交互研究的可复制框架。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly integrating into education, concerns have emerged regarding its potential implications on cognitive engagement and problem-solving behavior. However, existing research largely treats AI exposure as a binary condition (AI vs. no-AI), with limited differentiation between interaction modalities and post-exposure effects. This study investigates whether distinct AI interaction modes (Tutor, Collaborator, Solver) influence frontal EEG spectral activity. Electroencephalography (EEG) data and quantified behavioral metrics were recorded from 48 study participants (24 males, 24 females; ages 14-18) across two counterbalanced quizzes in a within-subject design. Statistical analyses included Friedman tests, repeated-measures ANOVA, paired t-tests, and effect size calculations. Behavioral changes were mathematically analyzed in an observation matrix of three characteristics -Initiation, Processing, and Stress-measured on an ordinal scale. Each mode showed significant differences in all three behavioral measures. Descriptive EEG patterns in AI interaction mode were observed, and the possibility of short-term carryover effects of AI was explored. Although the EEG data did not reach statistical significance, the patterns observed across the three AI interaction modes warrant further investigation. This study provides preliminary behavioral evidence and investigative electrophysiological observations, exploring possible AI-interaction-mode-based differences in neural activity and behavior, while establishing a replicable framework for future human-AI interaction studies.

</details>


### [8] [HiLSVA: Design and Evaluation of a Human-in-the-Loop Agentic System for Scientific Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26614)
*Kuangshi Ai,Patrick Phuoc Do,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HiLSVA是一个支持人机协同的科学可视化代理系统，通过计划优先的多智能体架构、人工监督、逐步溯源和测试时学习，实现了混合主动交互。实验表明该设计能提升任务完成度、用户控制和工作流透明度，但在执行效率与人工监督之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在科学可视化中过度强调自主性，缺乏人类分析控制、透明度和人工监督，导致用户难以有效参与和干预分析过程。

Method: 提出了HiLSVA系统，包含：(1) 计划优先的多智能体架构；(2) 人工监督和逐步溯源追踪；(3) 测试时从用户反馈中学习适应；(4) 自然语言与直接操作混合的人机交接；(5) 沙箱执行确保安全可复现。

Result: 通过案例研究和12名不同专业水平参与者的控制用户实验，证明混合主动交互在不同专业水平下均能提升任务完成度、用户控制和工作流透明度，但存在执行效率与人工监督之间的权衡。

Conclusion: HiLSVA将代理驱动的科学可视化重新定义为协同过程，增强而非替代人类分析推理，强调了以人为中心的设计在智能科学可视化中的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents enable natural language interaction for scientific visualization (SciVis). Still, prior systems have essentially prioritized autonomy over human analytical control, thereby limiting transparency and human oversight. We present HiLSVA, a human-in-the-loop agentic system that supports mixed-initiative SciVis workflows. HiLSVA integrates a plan-first multi-agent architecture with explicit human oversight, stepwise provenance tracking, and learn-at-test-time adaptation from user feedback. The system supports fluid handoff between humans and agents through both natural language and direct manipulation of visualizations, while sandboxed execution ensures safe, reproducible workflows. In doing so, HiLSVA reframes agentic SciVis as a collaborative process that augments, rather than replaces, human analytical reasoning. We evaluate HiLSVA through representative case studies and a controlled user study with twelve participants of varying expertise across multiple autonomy settings. Results show that mixed-initiative interaction improves task completion, user control, and workflow transparency across different levels of user expertise, while revealing a tradeoff between execution efficiency and human oversight. These findings highlight the importance of human-centered design in agentic SciVis and guide the development of future collaborative visualization systems. We encourage readers to explore our demo video, case studies, and source code at https://hilsva.github.io/.

</details>


### [9] [Reviving Reflection-in-Action: Instilling Designerly Thinking in AI-Supported Ideation through Multimodal Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26626)
*Samangi Wadinambiarachchi,Jenny Waycott,Greg Wadley*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态提示（文本、草图、草图+标签）在AI辅助设计工具中的影响，发现草图模态能提升设计流畅性，但用户仍偏好文本提示，并探讨了如何在AI工具中重新引入反思性草图绘制以支持设计技能培养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI创意支持工具主要依赖文本提示生成解决方案导向的输出，但多模态提示（尤其是引入"生产性摩擦"以促进迭代和反思）在设计师-AI交互中的潜在价值尚未被充分探索。

Method: 开发了SketchifAI原型AI-CST，通过混合方法、被试内设计进行实验，比较不同输入模态（文本、草图、草图+标签）对设计专业学生的影响，测量指标包括意图表达能力、创意支持感知和发散思维表现。

Result: 草图模态倾向于提升流畅性，但在多样性、原创性或质量方面与文本模态相比无显著差异。然而，参与者对文本提示表现出强烈的偏好。

Conclusion: 需要设计能够重新引入反思性草图绘制的AI工具，确保设计师-AI交互能够支持而非侵蚀学生的基本设计技能。

Abstract: Current AI-powered creativity support tools (AI-CSTs) primarily use text prompting to generate solution-oriented outputs. However, the potential value of multimodal prompting in designer-AI interaction, specifically the introduction of productive friction to encourage iteration and reflection, has not been fully explored. To address this, we developed SketchifAI, a prototype AI-CST, and evaluated it with design students. In a mixed-methods, within-participants study, we examined how different input modalities (text, sketch, and sketch-plus-tags) affected design students' perceived ability to express their intent, their perception of creativity support, and their divergent thinking performance. Our preliminary findings suggest that the sketch modality tended to enhance fluency, with inconclusive evidence for differences in variety, originality, or quality compared to text modality. Yet, paradoxically, participants showed a strong preference for text prompting. We discuss how AI tools might be designed to reintroduce reflection-through-sketching, ensuring that designer-AI interaction supports, rather than erodes, essential design skills in students.

</details>


### [10] [Invisible Impact of Empathy on Behavioral Change: Isolating the Effect of Empathy in Long-term Physical Activity Coaching Chatbot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26641)
*Li Siyan,Kai-Hui Liang,Shopnil Shahriar,Yilin Ye,Shiyoh Goetsu,Wei-Wei Du,Masahiro Yoshida,Tsunayuki Ohwa,Xuhai Xu,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过构建三个仅 empathy 程度不同的 WhatsApp 身体活动教练聊天机器人，在六周内测实验（N=13）中发现：参与者难以区分不同共情条件，非共情版本反而被认为更具吸引力和实用性；但高共情版本与更高的步数增长和更快的意图改善相关。这表明共情的作用是微妙的——用户难以明确识别，但仍能影响动机和信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话系统（由大语言模型驱动）常将共情视为当然，但未评估其真实影响，尤其是在行为改变领域，动机和依从性往往依赖微妙的用户-聊天机器人互动。本文旨在检验这一假设。

Method: 构建三个仅 empathy 水平不同的 WhatsApp 身体活动（PA）教练聊天机器人，进行为期六周的 within-subject 研究（N=13），评估参与者对共情条件的区分能力及各条件对步数、意图等指标的影响。

Result: 参与者难以区分不同共情条件；非共情版本常被认为更吸引人、更有用；但高共情版本与更大的平均步数增长和更快的意图改善相关。

Conclusion: 共情的作用是 nuanced 的：外行用户难以明确识别，但仍能塑造支持持续改变的动机和信任。通过精细加工可能性模型的边缘路径解释这一模式，并为构建平衡有效性与人性化连接的下一代 PA 教练聊天机器人提出设计启示。

Abstract: Current dialogue systems, powered by large language models, often treat empathy as essential without assessing its true impact, especially in behavior change, where motivation and adherence often depend on subtle user-chatbot dynamics. We examine this assumption by building three WhatsApp physical-activity (PA) coaching chatbots that differ only in empathy level and evaluating them in a six-week within-subject study (N = 13). Participants struggled to distinguish between the empathy conditions, and the non-empathetic version was often rated as more engaging and useful. However, higher-empathy variants were still associated with a larger overall average increase in step counts and faster improvement in intention to follow advice. These results suggest empathy's role is nuanced: it may be hard for lay users to identify explicitly, but it can still shape motivation and trust that support sustained change. We interpret this pattern through the Elaboration Likelihood Model's peripheral route. We highlight design implications for building next-generation PA coaching chatbots that balance effectiveness with human-like connection.

</details>


### [11] [From Content to Strategy: Understanding the Motivations, Processes, and Impacts of AI-Guided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26672)
*Chang Wan,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过26次深度访谈，探讨了AI引导沟通（AI-guided communication）在亲密关系中的应用。参与者使用AI分析挑战性场景，获得了自我反思、情绪缓解、冲突预防、多视角和安全的自我表露空间。AI增强了共情和沟通技能，但也引发自我怀疑和对失去独特性的担忧。对长期关系影响的看法因短期问题解决的有用性而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的进步已将其应用从生成内容扩展到引导沟通策略，但对这一新兴使用模式及其后果的理论和实证理解仍然有限。

Method: 对26位使用AI制定沟通策略的个体进行了深度访谈。

Result: 参与者强烈偏好将AI用于分析亲密关系中的挑战性场景，因为它促进了自我反思、情绪缓解、冲突预防、提供多视角和安全无评判的自我表露空间。AI增强了共情和沟通技能，但也引发自我怀疑和对失去独特性的担忧。对长期关系影响的看法因短期问题解决的有用性而存在分歧。

Conclusion: AI引导沟通在亲密关系中具有促进自我反思和情绪调节等积极效用，但存在自我怀疑和独特性丧失的隐忧，长期关系影响取决于短期实用性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-mediated communication (AI-MC) is conceptualized as applying AI to augment or generate message content (Hancock et al., 2020). However, advances in generative AI have expanded its use beyond generating content to guiding individuals' communication strategies, that is, AI-guided communication, yet theoretical and empirical understandings of this emerging use pattern and its consequences remains limited. To address this gap, this study conducted 26 in-depth interviews with individuals who have used AI to develop their communication strategies. Findings suggest participants strongly preferred using AI to analyze challenging scenarios in close relationships, because it fostered self-reflection, eased emotions, prevented conflict escalation, offered multiple perspectives, and provided a safe, nonjudgmental space for self-disclosure. Participants also stated that AI-guided communication enhanced their empathy and communication skills, though some voiced self-doubt and worried about losing their uniqueness. Views on long-term relational impact were mixed, depending on perceived usefulness of AI for resolving short-term interpersonal challenges.

</details>


### [12] [Modeling Adaptive Visual Search in Semantically Hierarchical Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26725)
*Saku Sourulahti,Jussi P. P. Jokinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算认知模型，研究信息分组如何影响视觉搜索，并通过计算理性视角将用户行为视为对认知和任务约束的适应。模型利用分层任务表示，结合语义和视觉结构提升搜索效率，并通过人类实验验证语义分组与空间分组一致时可改善搜索表现。模型复现了任务时长和眼动模式，并展示了在UI线框快速原型评估中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解信息分组（尤其是语义分组）如何影响视觉搜索效率，为UI设计提供理论依据，并填补现有视觉搜索模型在层次化记忆结构利用方面的不足。

Method: 基于计算理性（computational rationality）构建认知模型，将用户行为视为对认知与任务约束的适应；模型利用层次化任务表示，结合语义与视觉结构；通过两项人类行为实验（视觉搜索与语义分类）进行验证，并与模型输出的任务时长和眼动模式进行对比。

Result: 模型成功复现了人类在视觉搜索中的任务时长和眼动模式；实验表明，当语义分组与空间分组一致时，搜索性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 该模型扩展了现有视觉搜索模型，能够解释人类如何利用层次化记忆结构提升搜索效率，并展示了在UI线框快速原型中评估语义视觉分组的实际应用价值，为复杂真实界面设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces a computational cognitive model to investigate how information grouping impacts visual search, a key consideration in user interface design. The model uses computational rationality to view user behavior as an adaptation to cognitive and task constraints. Our work highlights that humans use hierarchical task representations, exploiting semantic and visual structures to improve search efficiency within the constraints of the visual system. We validate this model with data from two human studies focused on visual search and semantic categorization, demonstrating that semantic grouping improves search performance when it aligns with spatial grouping. Our model replicates task durations and eye movement patterns. By improving understanding of how hierarchical memory structures are utilized in human cognition, the model extends previous visual search models. We showcase our model in the rapid prototyping and evaluation of semantic visual groupings within user interface wireframes, suggesting a pathway toward applications in more complex, real-world interface design.

</details>


### [13] ['A bit of chaos and madness': The AI Assessment Scale and the work of assessment reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26729)
*Mike Perkins,Darius Postma,Jasper Roe,Susan Sisay,Craig Holdcroft*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过越南和英国两所大学的焦点小组访谈，探讨了AI评估量表(AIAS)在高等教育中的实施情况。研究发现AIAS能作为共同语言促进生成式AI的合法使用，但实施效果受治理、工具获取、教师信心、工作量、诚信担忧、学科背景和学习目标一致性等因素影响。AIAS可能推动真实评估设计，也可能沦为合规形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI给高校评估设计带来压力，需要平衡诚信、公平和有效性。虽然AIAS等框架已被提出，但关于教师如何体验其实施的实证证据仍然有限。

Method: 质性研究设计，在越南一所私立国际大学和英国一所公立大学进行5个焦点小组访谈，共30名学术人员参与。采用混合主题分析，以批判性AI素养作为敏化概念。

Result: 提炼出六个主题：认识与整合AI、促进条件、能力建设、采纳路径、实践伦理、重构教学法。教师认为AIAS提供了共享语言，但实施受到治理、工具可及性、教师信心、工作量、诚信问题、学科背景和学习目标一致性等因素影响。

Conclusion: AIAS可推动真实评估设计和学生参与，但当与学习目标、学科背景和教师能力脱节时，可能沦为合规层。研究提供了关于GenAI评估框架从政策采纳走向教学实施的制度条件的实证证据。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has intensified pressure on universities to redesign assessment while maintaining integrity, equity, and validity. Structured frameworks such as the Artificial Intelligence Assessment Scale (AIAS) offer one response, but evidence of how staff experience their implementation remains limited. This qualitative study examines AIAS implementation at a private international university in Vietnam and a public university in the United Kingdom. Data from five focus groups with 30 academic staff were analysed using hybrid thematic analysis, with Critical AI Literacy used as a sensitising concept. Six themes were developed: recognising and integrating AI, facilitating conditions, building capacity, pathways to adoption, ethics in practice, and reframing pedagogy.
  Staff valued the AIAS as a shared language for legitimising GenAI use, clarifying boundaries, and prompting reflection on assessment design. However, implementation was shaped by governance, tool access, staff confidence, workload, integrity concerns, disciplinary context, and alignment with learning outcomes. The findings show that the AIAS could prompt authentic assessment design and student engagement, but may become a compliance layer when disconnected from learning outcomes, disciplinary context, and staff capacity. This study contributes empirical evidence on the institutional conditions through which GenAI assessment frameworks move from policy adoption to pedagogical enactment.

</details>


### [14] [MedSWFlow: An Open-Source LLM Workflow for Drafting Medical Social Work Case Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26884)
*Yulin Mao,Shiyu Li,Shuping Song,Yuling Zhang,Yajun Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MedSWFlow是一个开源、模型无关的LLM工作流，用于起草医疗社会工作个案计划，将专业个案规划任务拆解为六个阶段，通过分阶段提示生成可审阅的评估表格和服务计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗社会工作个案计划的撰写专业性强、流程复杂，现有LLM应用缺乏针对该领域的结构化、可复现的工作流框架，需要一种标准化、开源且模型无关的方案来辅助个案规划。

Method: 将个案规划任务分解为评估、问题分析、目标设定、干预规划、风险预判、预期效果评估六个阶段；标准化个案输入，构建结构化个案档案；通过分阶段提示（staged prompting）生成可审阅的评估表格和服务计划。

Result: 开发了MedSWFlow开源框架，支持跨LLM提供商的可复现个案计划生成，输出作为从业者审阅的草案。

Conclusion: MedSWFlow提供了一种标准化、可复现的医疗社会工作计划生成方法，输出需经从业者审阅后方可应用，源码已开源。

Abstract: We present MedSWFlow, an open-source, model-agnostic LLM workflow for drafting medical social work case plans. The framework translates professional case-planning tasks into six stages: assessment, problem analysis, goal setting, intervention planning, risk anticipation, and planned effect evaluation. Drawing on established social work and behavioral frameworks, MedSWFlow standardizes case inputs, builds structured case profiles, and generates reviewable assessment forms and service plans through staged prompting. The system is released as an open-source research framework for reproducible case-plan generation across LLM providers. Outputs are intended as practitioner-reviewed drafts rather than final service decisions. Source code: https://github.com/santhiyacw-droid/MedSWFlow/tree/main.

</details>


### [15] [Optimizing Human-Machine Interface for Real-Time AI Support in the Operating Room: the CVS Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26886)
*Lorenzo Arboit,Nicolas Chanel,Aditya Murali,Pietro Mascagni,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过17位外科医生的混合方法用户中心设计研究，探索了腹腔镜胆囊切除术中AI辅助关键安全视野评估的人机界面优化方案，最终设计了CVS Copilot——一种外科医生控制、角色自适应、提供最小默认可视化和可选覆盖层的人机界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统用于自动CVS评估即将进入临床转化，但临床安全性和有效性不仅取决于算法性能，还取决于人机界面(HMI)的质量。目前缺乏对术中AI预测结果应如何呈现和控制的系统性研究。

Method: 17位外科医生（包括住院医师、主治医师和教授）参与混合方法、用户中心设计研究。通过访谈探索交互模式、辅助时机、可视化策略和控制机制，采用反思性主题分析和人因启发式方法进行分析。

Result: 16/17支持AI术中决策辅助但拒绝自主决策；主治医师偏好关键时刻的最小AI反馈(13/14)，住院医师偏好带有置信度指示器和按需解剖覆盖层的可选指导(3/3)；最支持最小覆盖层(16/17)和按需解剖分割(13/17)；对持续性覆盖层、触觉反馈和数字置信度显示存在担忧。

Conclusion: 设计了CVS Copilot——一种外科医生控制、角色自适应的人机界面，提供基于AI的CVS评估，采用最小默认可视化和可选覆盖层，满足不同经验水平外科医生的差异化需求。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems for automated Critical View of Safety (CVS) assessment in laparoscopic cholecystectomy are nearing clinical translation. Beyond algorithmic performance, clinical safety and effectiveness depend on the quality of the human-machine interface (HMI). This work examines how AI-generated predictions should be presented and controlled intraoperatively. Seventeen surgeons, including residents, attending surgeons, and professors, took part in a mixed-methods, user-centered design study to optimize an intraoperative HMI for AI-assisted safe laparoscopic cholecystectomy. Interviews explored interaction modalities, timing of assistance, visualization strategies, and control mechanisms across surgical roles, and were analyzed using reflexive thematic analysis and human-factors heuristics. Most surgeons (16/17) supported the use of AI for intraoperative decision support while rejecting autonomous decision-making. Attendings preferred minimal AI feedback at decisive moments (13/14), whereas residents favored optional guidance (3/3) with confidence indicators and on-demand anatomical overlays. Across interviews, surgeons consistently prioritized visual, surgeon-controlled, minimally intrusive displays, with the strongest support for a minimal overlay (16/17) and on-demand anatomical segmentation (13/17). Recurrent concerns included persistent overlays, haptic feedback, and numeric confidence displays, although these were not uniformly raised across the cohort. These findings informed the design of CVS Copilot, a surgeon-controlled, role-adaptive HMI that provides AI-based CVS assessment with minimal default visualization and optional overlays.

</details>


### [16] [Game Changers: Designing and Measuring Dynamic Feedback To Help Users Self-Regulate in a VR Pointing Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26925)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Scott Bateman,Iris Brunner,Lars Evald,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过VR指向任务系统研究三种性能指标（短完成时间、直线运动、高峰值速度）和三种反馈时机（连续、动作结束、任务结束）对玩家表现和感知的影响，发现动态反馈平均上帮助玩家更直更快地指向，但个体差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏通过反馈动态传递信息对玩家表现、学习和提升至关重要，但性能指标如何影响玩家在指向、操控等核心游戏任务中的表现和感知尚不清晰。

Method: 在VR指向任务中，系统性地探索三种性能指标（奖励短完成时间、直线移动或高峰值速度）驱动的反馈在三种时机（连续、动作结束时、任务结束时）对玩家的影响，并进行定量比较。

Result: 平均而言，动态反馈帮助玩家指向更直更快，但对部分玩家效果微弱甚至相反。研究对三种反馈形式与驱动指标进行了定量比较。

Conclusion: 该工作为游戏设计师提供了动态反馈设计的依据，帮助其了解何时采用与理想游戏性能目标一致的反馈方案。

Abstract: The way games dynamically convey information through feedback is critical to players' ability to perform, learn, and improve. However, it is poorly understood how performance metrics impact player performance and perception in core game tasks like pointing or steering. With a virtual reality pointing task we systematically explored how three performance metrics driving the feedback affected players when rewarding short completion times, straight movements, or high peak speed. across different points in time - continuously, at end-of-action, or at end-of-task. On average the dynamic feedback helped people point more straight and faster, while for others it had small or opposite effect. The study quantitatively compared dynamic feedback across three forms with the metrics driving the form as the intended locus of quantitative comparison. Our work improves game designers basis for crafting dynamic feedback by helping them know when to employ feedback schemes that align with desirable game performance objectives.

</details>


### [17] [Continuous Behavioral Synthesis for Adaptive Health Dashboards: An LLM-Mediated Architecture Integrating Explicit Preference, Spatial Reorganization, and Attention Allocation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26937)
*Tiziano Santilli,Mina Alipour,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型作为行为合成引擎的技术架构，通过整合三种交互信号（显式微反馈、空间优先级、注意力投入），在无需大量历史数据的情况下实现自适应用户界面的即时个性化。系统通过分层提示工程框架将低层交互模式转化为高层设计决策，并在个人健康监控领域进行了实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应用户界面工程依赖基于规则的编码设计者直觉或需要大量历史数据的机器学习方法，难以从稀疏、异构的用户信号中实现即时个性化。

Method: 提出LLM作为行为合成引擎的架构，集成三种行为通道（显式微反馈、通过拖拽交互推断的空间优先级、悬停驻留时间反映的注意力投入），采用分层提示构建方法（分离时间上下文确定、行为信号提取、显式偏好执行、用户画像合成），结合人工指定的行为解释和时间启发式与LLM介导的合成。

Result: 在个人健康监控领域实现了管理14项健康指标、7种可视化模态的系统，进行了多场景适应行为分析评估，展示了配置文件驱动初始化和多信号行为适应的结果界面。

Conclusion: 该方法能够调和多个同时出现的信号，处理难以通过显式规则编码的适应逻辑，实现从稀疏信号到即时界面适应的转变。

Abstract: The engineering of adaptive user interfaces has traditionally relied on either rule-based systems encoding designer intuitions about user needs or machine learning approaches requiring substantial historical data before achieving effective personalization. We present a technical architecture that leverages Large Language Models as behavioral synthesis engines to enable immediate adaptation from sparse, heterogeneous user signals. Our system integrates three distinct behavioral channels, i) explicit micro-feedback on individual interface elements, ii) spatial priority inferred from manual widget reorganization through drag-and-drop interaction, iii) and attentional investment measured through dwell time during hover events, within a structured prompt engineering framework that continuously regenerates dashboard layouts while maintaining explanatory coherence. The architecture addresses the technical challenge of translating low-level interaction patterns into high-level design decisions through a layered prompt construction methodology that separates temporal context determination, behavioral signal extraction, explicit preference enforcement, and user profile synthesis. The approach combines manually specified behavioral interpretations and temporal heuristics with LLM-mediated synthesis, enabling the reconciliation of multiple simultaneous signals that would be difficult to encode through explicit rules alone. We demonstrate the system through an instantiation in the personal health monitoring domain, including an analytical evaluation of adaptation behavior across multiple scenarios and a working implementation managing fourteen distinct health metrics across seven widget visualization modalities. The evaluation compares profile-driven initialization, multi-signal behavioral adaptation, and presents the resulting interfaces through representative post-adaptation screenshots.

</details>


### [18] [What Holds Back Brain-Computer Interfaces? Uncovering Challenges and Opportunities in BCI-controlled Games for Cerebral Palsy Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26951)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Kirstine Schultz Dalgaard,Kirstine Johanne Stougaard Klebæk,Hendrik Knoche,Mads Jochumsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了脑机接口（BCI）在脑瘫（CP）康复中的设计约束，通过临床治疗师和10名脑瘫患者的用户体验研究，发现BCI游戏中的间歇性帮助虽能缓解单调性，但会引发用户对控制感的怀疑；治疗师认为BCI康复可作为传统训练的补充，帮助患者从游戏化练习过渡到自主训练。文章总结了关键挑战与机遇，为未来BCI康复设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCI-based CP康复的设计约束尚不明确，尤其缺乏对患者如何体验BCI控制感以及计算机介导的游戏辅助如何影响用户体验的理解。

Method: 结合临床治疗师的专业见解与10名脑瘫患者参与BCI游戏原型的体验报告，进行临床和用户视角的初步分析。

Result: 间歇性帮助在缓解游戏单调性的同时，引发了用户对自我控制感的怀疑；治疗师视角下BCI康复是传统训练的补充，有助于从游戏化练习过渡到自主训练。

Conclusion: 明确了BCI脑瘫康复设计中的关键挑战与机遇，为未来设计和研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) offer promising avenues for cerebral palsy (CP) rehabilitation at home and in the clinic, using games that promote engagement and sustained training effort. Nonetheless, the design constraints of BCI-based CP rehabilitation remain unclear, especially how individuals with CP experience a sense of control through BCI, and how they experience computer-mediated game assistance. To address this gap, we present preliminary clinical and user perspectives on BCI-based CP rehabilitation, drawing on in-clinic insights from a CP therapist and experiential accounts from ten individuals with CP engaging with BCI game prototypes. Sporadic help in BCI games eased monotony, but also fostered doubts regarding agency. The therapist saw BCI rehabilitation as complementary to traditional training, facilitating the transition from playful exercises to autonomous, self-managed training. We outline key challenges and opportunities to inform and empower further design and research of BCI training for CP.

</details>


### [19] [Floor Raiser or Ceiling Limiter? Differential Storytelling Outcomes with a Child-Centric GenAI System Across Individual Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27067)
*Min Fan,Wanqing Ma,Xinyue Cui,Xiaolu Dai,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过40名7-12岁儿童的混合方法实验，发现GenAI辅助故事创作能缩小83.5%的质量差距（提升低水平儿童、约束高水平儿童），但仅改善创意和丰富性维度，且年龄影响关键词选择偏好，图像再生行为与结构质量正相关但受基线水平调节。提出机制权变支架设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI有望普及创意素养，但尚不清楚是否所有儿童都能平等受益，需要实证研究其差异化影响。

Method: 采用混合方法被试内实验设计，40名2-6年级（7-12岁）儿童在GenAI辅助与传统故事板两种条件下进行故事创作，对比分析质量差异。

Result: 1) GenAI辅助导致"抬升地板"式趋同模式，质量差距缩小83.5%，其机制是低端支持与高端约束；该趋同具有维度选择性（改善创意与丰富性，不改变连贯性与叙事结构）。2) 年幼儿童更倾向选择语义距离大的关键词，年长儿童反之，但参与取向存在个体差异。3) 图像再生与结构质量正相关，但基线控制后关联减弱。

Conclusion: 提出机制权变支架（mechanism-contingent scaffolding）作为面向多样化儿童的自适应GenAI故事创作系统设计原则。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) holds promise for democratizing creative literacy, yet whether it benefits all children equally remains unclear. Using a child-centric GenAI storytelling system for children aged 7-12, we conducted a mixed-methods within-subjects experiment (N = 40, Grades 2-6) comparing GenAI-assisted and traditional storyboard conditions. Three findings emerged. First, the GenAI-assisted condition was associated with a floor-raising convergence pattern, with the quality gap narrowing by 83.5%, driven by lower-end support and upper-end constraint mechanisms. This convergence was dimension-selective, improving creativity and richness while leaving coherence and narrative structure tied to baseline performance. Second, younger children more often selected semantically distant keywords while older children preferred semantically closer ones, although engagement orientation varied across individuals regardless of age. Third, image regeneration was positively associated with structural quality dimensions, though this association was attenuated after baseline control. We propose mechanism-contingent scaffolding as a design principle for adaptive GenAI storytelling systems serving diverse children.

</details>


### [20] [Urban Context and Travel Experience Events: An Exploratory Comparison of Two German Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27077)
*Marie Güntert,Esther Bosch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过移动应用追踪德国城市（汉堡）和乡村（图特林根）共91名参与者的日常出行，运用多层回归分析发现：图特林根地区公共交通运力充足对体验有最强正面影响，而准点性差和低幸福感负面影响最大；汉堡地区准点性差和负面信息事件影响最大。这些发现为改善地方公共交通提供了决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究影响城市与乡村地区公共交通出行体验的关键事件，以及这些事件如何影响出行体验和出行行为，为改善公共交通服务提供实证基础。

Method: 使用移动应用追踪91名参与者（图特林根21人，汉堡70人）的日常行程，收集实时出行体验评价和情境数据，采用多层回归分析评估准点性、运力、信息等事件对途中体验的影响。

Result: 图特林根：运力充足正面影响最大，准点性差和低幸福感负面影响最大；汉堡：准点性差和负面信息事件影响最大。城乡影响因素的权重存在差异。

Conclusion: 识别出的关键影响因素为地方公共交通改善措施的决策提供了科学依据，有助于针对性地优化不同区域的公共交通服务。

Abstract: The presented study investigates events influencing public transportation experience in both urban (Hamburg) and rural (Tuttlingen) areas in Germany, with the aim of identifying events that affect travel experience and as a result travel behavior. Using a mobile application, 21 participants in Tuttlingen and 70 participants in Hamburg tracked everyday trips, providing real-time evaluations of travel experiences along with situational data. Multi-level regression analyses were applied to assess the impact of events such as punctuality, capacity offer, information about public transportation and others on the ontrip experience. Results indicate that a sufficient public transportation capacity offer has the strongest positive effect in Tuttlingen, whereas a lack of punctuality and low personal well-being have the strongest negative effects. In Hamburg, a lack of punctuality and a negative information event have the largest impacts. These identified effects provide a foundation for decision-making and measures to improve local public transportation.

</details>


### [21] [Behind the Mask: A Taxonomic Analysis of Activities in Online Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27111)
*Debora F De Souza,Gabriela Beltrao,Berta Chulvi,Sergio Dantonio,Mehmet Gokay Ozerim,Javier Torregrosa,Adrian Giron,Angel Panizo,Pablo Miralles Gonzalez,Helena Liz,Javier Huertas Tato,Sonia Sousa,Alejandro Martin,Monika Maciuliene,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过专家协作和文献综述，设计了一个用于识别在线社交网络中传播虚假信息的恶意行为者的分类法，并通过反移民话语案例研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中虚假信息的传播日益严重，现有研究缺乏系统性的恶意行为者分类方法，需要一种能够区分其特征、活动和策略的分类框架。

Method: 与领域专家协作并结合学术文献综述，设计恶意行为者分类法；通过分析反移民话语社交媒体渠道的案例研究，应用并验证该分类法。

Result: 提出了一套区分恶意行为者特征、活动和策略的分类法，并在反移民话语案例中成功应用，展示了其在识别虚假信息传播者方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 该分类法有助于研究人员和实践者负责任地设计网络系统，以应对虚假信息传播问题。

Abstract: The broadcast of disinformation in online social networks (OSN) is a growing concern examined across several disciplines, including human-computer interaction (HCI). The pervasive issue has been prompting novel approaches to identify the malicious actors behind the dissemination of deceptive and fabricated content. Analyzing the characteristics and activities of these actors, we designed a taxonomy informed by collaboration with subject matter experts (SMEs) and a review of the academic literature. Our study explores how to distinguish the characteristics, activities, and strategies of malicious actors on OSN and examines how they contribute to the spread of disinformation. We describe the design process and the application of the taxonomy in a case study analyzing anti-migration discourse in social media channels, and reflect on its potential to aid researchers and practitioners in the responsible design of network systems.

</details>


### [22] ["Everyone Says Them": Deception Typologies, Probabilistic Trust, and Grassroots Safety Knowledge Among Gay Dating App Users in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27284)
*Yibo Meng,Lyumanshan Ye,Yingfangzhong Sun,Bingyi Liu,Huidi Lu,Xiaolan Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过22位中国男同性恋用户的半结构化访谈，揭示了约会应用中的欺骗类型（从身份造假扩展到情感、财务、商业欺骗）、用户的多层概率验证策略（信任评估是一个多信号、暂定过程而非二元判断），以及风险识别的社区协作机制（经验流通、策略抽象、规则编码化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HCI和CSCW研究中，性少数男性在约会应用中遭遇欺骗互动这一现象尚未得到充分探索，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对22位使用Blued、Aloha、Fanka、Soul等平台的中国男同性恋用户进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 识别出超越档案虚假陈述的欺骗类型学（关系、情感、财务、商业欺骗）；发现用户通过长期平台使用形成了分层概率验证策略（信任评估是多信号、暂定过程）；揭示风险识别是受经验流通、战术抽象和社区共享规则编码影响的协作实践。

Conclusion: 约会应用中的欺骗远不止虚假档案，用户发展出复杂、非二元的信任评估策略，且风险识别本质上是一种社区协作实践。

Abstract: Gay dating applications have become critical platforms for sexual minority men to seek relationships and community, yet they also expose users to deceptive interactions that remain underexplored in HCI and CSCW research. This study examines how gay male users in China experience, identify, and respond to deception on dating applications. Through semi-structured interviews with 22 participants across platforms including Blued, Aloha, Fanka, and Soul, we make three contributions. First, we identify a typology of deceptive practices extending beyond profile misrepresentation to encompass relational, emotional, financial, and commercial forms of deception. Second, we document the layered, probabilistic verification strategies users develop through long-term platform use, showing that trust assessment operates as a multi-signal, provisional process rather than a binary judgment. Third, we demonstrate that risk recognition is a collaborative practice shaped by the circulation of experience, the abstraction of recurrent tactics, and the codification of shared rules within the community.

</details>


### [23] [Reading the Same Data Differently: Interpretive Labor Across System Boundaries in Electronic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27301)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Zhiqi Gao,Shuai Ma,Hongyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文通过对中国社区矫正系统中26名受监督者和12名管理人员的半结构化访谈，揭示了电子监控（EM）作为双面传感系统中存在的"解释性错位"现象：受监督者只能从结果推断系统逻辑，而管理者则依赖语境知识重建行为。这种结构性分歧源于数据、语境和推理过程的不对称访问，并塑造了试探、策略适应、过度服从、脱离和抗争等行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将电子监控视为刑事司法工具或合规机制，忽略了传感数据在日常实践中如何被赋予意义的过程。作者试图揭示EM作为双面传感系统中不同主体（受监督者与管理者）如何从不同位置理解同一数据流。

Method: 基于对中国社区矫正系统中26名受监督者和12名管理人员的半结构化访谈，采用定性分析方法。

Result: 发现了"解释性错位"（interpretive misalignment）这一核心现象：受监督者对系统逻辑的可见性有限，只能从结果反向推断；管理者则利用语境知识、专业经验和制度程序从模糊痕迹中重建行为。这种不对称导致了试探、策略适应、过度服从、脱离和抗争等行为模式。

Conclusion: 为CSCW领域提供了连续传感作为分布式解释工作的理论视角，并提出了使数据到决策的过程对系统双方更可理解、可争议和可问责的设计机会。

Abstract: Electronic monitoring (EM) systems are increasingly used in community corrections to enforce spatial, temporal, and behavioral rules through continuous sensing. While prior work has examined EM as a criminal justice tool or as a mechanism for compliance, less is known about how sensed data become meaningful in everyday practice. This poster examines EM as a dual-sided sensing system in which supervised individuals and authorities reason about the same data stream from different positions. Based on semi-structured interviews with 26 supervised individuals and 12 authorities in China's community corrections system, we show that supervised individuals infer system logic from outcomes with limited visibility into how data are interpreted, while authorities reconstruct behavior from ambiguous traces using contextual knowledge, professional experience, and institutional procedures. We call this structural divergence interpretive misalignment. It emerges from asymmetric access to data, context, and reasoning processes, and it shapes behavior through probing, strategic adaptation, over-compliance, disengagement, and contestation. We contribute a CSCW account of continuous sensing as distributed interpretive work and identify design opportunities for making data-to-decision processes more legible, contestable, and accountable across system sides.

</details>


### [24] [AI Healthcare Chatbots as Information Infrastructure: A Large-Scale Study of User-Reported Breakdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27302)
*Muhammad Hassan,Ramazan Yener,Ece Gumusel,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究分析了59款AI医疗健康聊天机器人的15,000+用户评论，识别出三大类故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量问题、计费与客户支持问题。其中隐私与安全问题与最负面的体验相关。论文将AI医疗聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，指出接入、可用性和信任方面的失败如何影响用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI医疗健康聊天机器人被越来越多地用于支持健康信息寻求和自我管理，但其性能和用户影响尚待研究。作者希望通过大规模用户评论分析，系统性地理解这些系统在日常信息与情感场景中的实际表现和问题。

Method: 收集59款AI医疗健康聊天机器人应用的15,000+条用户评论，采用主题建模（Topic Modeling）和解释性分析（Interpretive Analysis）识别用户反馈中的重复性故障模式。

Result: 识别出三大重复性故障：(1) 访问障碍与服务不可靠；(2) 用户体验与交互质量；(3) 计费与客户支持问题。其中隐私和安全问题与最负面的用户体验显著相关。

Conclusion: 将AI医疗聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，揭示了接入、可用性和信任方面的失败对用户的影响，为设计师、政策制定者和信息专业人士改进数字健康系统提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: AI healthcare chatbots are increasingly used to support health information seeking and self-management, yet their performance and impact on users remains to be studied. This study examines over 15,000 user reviews from 59 AI healthcare chatbot apps to explore how these systems function in everyday informational and emotional contexts. Topic modeling and interpretive analysis identify three recurring breakdowns: access barriers and service unreliability, user experience and interaction quality, and billing and customer support issues. Privacy and security concerns are associated with the most negative experiences. By framing AI healthcare chatbots as information infrastructures, our findings highlight how failures in access, usability, and trust affect users, offering actionable insights for designers, policymakers, and information professionals aiming to improve digital health systems.

</details>

### [25] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VMTAD——一种基于Transformer和记忆模块的全无监督视频异常检测方法，用于农业机器人在动态场景下的实时障碍物检测，在油菜数据集上达到SOTA性能（检测AUROC 0.973，分割AUROC 0.997），轻量版本推理仅需14ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业机器人安全感知方法存在两大局限：LiDAR无法检测植物冠层下方的障碍物；基于监督学习的视觉方法对训练集未见过的新障碍物泛化能力差。全无监督异常检测虽能学习正常视觉模式，但在移动机器人捕获的动态场景中表现不佳。

Method: VMTAD采用Transformer架构并集成专用记忆模块，该模块通过处理前序帧的编码表示来利用时序上下文信息，从而应对机器人运动引起的动态场景变化。模型仅使用正常操作图像进行训练，无需任何数据标注。

Result: 在'Grillion'农业机器人的挑战性油菜数据集上，检测AUROC达0.973，分割AUROC达0.997，均为当时最先进水平。轻量变体在保持高精度的同时实现14ms实时推理，经机器人总制动距离分析验证满足安全要求。

Conclusion: VMTAD通过Transformer与记忆模块的时序建模能力，有效解决了移动机器人在动态农业场景下的无监督异常检测难题，在精度和实时性之间取得了理想平衡，为农业机器人安全运行提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [26] [Racing a Wheeled Quadruped: Active Load Transfer Mitigation via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26313)
*Marla Eisman,Brian Lam,Samuel Sonnino,Francesco Borrelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模型预测控制和强化学习的分层控制框架，用于轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的主动侧倾控制，通过腿部执行器作为主动悬架产生抗侧倾力矩，实验证明该方法将横向载荷转移率降低44%，最快圈速提升8.7%，峰值横向加速度提升21.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主竞速中轮式四足机器人高速过弯时面临严重的横向载荷转移问题，影响稳定性和极限性能，现有方法缺乏有效的主动侧倾控制手段。

Method: 提出分层控制框架：1) 离线生成时间最优赛道线；2) 基于车辆动力学自行车模型的在线MPC规划器，主动最小化横向载荷转移率；3) 低层全身强化学习策略直接部署于16个执行器，利用腿部膝关节产生抗侧倾力矩实现主动悬架效果。

Result: 在Unitree Go2-W平台上的物理赛道实验表明：平均LTR降低44%，最快圈速提升8.7%，峰值横向加速度提升21.3%达到1.98 m/s²，高速稳定性优于非倾斜基线控制器。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPC+RL分层主动侧倾控制框架能显著提升轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的横向稳定性和极限性能，验证了利用腿部执行器作为主动悬架的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical control framework using model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) for active roll control to manage lateral load transfer during autonomous racing of a wheeled quadruped. The framework integrates offline time-optimal raceline generation, an online MPC planner that actively minimizes the lateral Load Transfer Ratio (LTR), and a low-level, whole-body RL policy deployed directly onto the robot's 16 actuators. The MPC is based on a vehicle dynamics bicycle model of the Unitree Go2-W platform. The robot's leg actuators act as active suspension where knee joints generate anti-roll torque to bank into turns. Physical track experiments demonstrate that active roll control reduces mean LTR by up to 44%, improves the fastest lap time by 8.7%, and boosts peak lateral acceleration capability by 21.3% to 1.98 $m/s^2$, maintaining robust high-speed stability beyond the range of a non-tilting baseline controller. Supplementary code and video can be found at https://github.com/meisman-ucb/go2w-roll-control-mpc

</details>


### [27] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
*Jannik Drotleff,Samuel Tovey,Paul Hohenberger,Christoph Lohrmann,Julian Hoßbach,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于物理的血毛细管网络仿真，结合真实流体力学流场、红细胞动力学和解剖学分岔几何结构，训练深度强化学习智能体通过趋化性导航。系统绘制了机器人尺寸和游泳速度的物理极限，揭示了布朗运动和流场克服推进力的禁用区域。成功智能体独立发现多种通用策略类型，包括旋转-旋转和节能搜索-等待策略，无需重新训练即可实现毛细管流的靶向阻塞与解阻塞，将通量恢复至健康基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在微机器人导航研究中局限于理想化几何结构，忽略了体内复杂的流体力学流场、受限分岔结构和密集细胞障碍，缺乏对真实生物环境中导航策略的有效训练框架。

Method: 构建了包含真实流体力学流场、显式红细胞动力学和解剖学来源分岔几何结构的血毛细管网络物理仿真，利用深度强化学习训练智能体通过趋化性信号导航，系统扫描机器人尺寸和游泳速度参数空间以绘制导航物理极限。

Result: 绘制了导航物理极限图，发现布朗运动和流场主导的禁用区域；成功智能体独立涌现出旋转-旋转和节能搜索-等待等多种通用策略；训练后的智能体无需重新调参即可实现毛细管流的靶向阻塞与解阻塞，恢复通量至健康基线水平。

Conclusion: 强化学习是开发复杂生物环境中自主微机器人干预策略的可行框架，为靶向药物递送和溶栓治疗提供了新途径。

Abstract: Autonomous microrobots navigating biological vasculature could enable targeted drug delivery and thrombolysis, yet training control policies for realistic environments remains an open challenge. Prior reinforcement learning (RL) studies of microrobotic navigation have been limited to idealized geometries that omit complex hydrodynamic flow fields, confined branching structures, and dense cellular obstacles found in vivo. Here, we develop a physically grounded simulation of a blood capillary network, incorporating realistic hydrodynamic flow fields, explicit red blood cell dynamics, and anatomically derived branching geometry, and train deep RL agents to navigate it via chemotaxis. We systematically map the physical limits of navigation across robot size and swimming speed, revealing a forbidden regime where Brownian motion and flow overcome propulsion. Successful agents independently discover multiple universal strategy types, including run-and-rotate and energy-efficient search-and-sit policies, regardless of robot parameters. Without retraining, these agents perform targeted blocking and unblocking of capillary flow, restoring throughput to healthy baseline levels. These results establish RL as a viable framework for developing autonomous microrobotic intervention strategies in complex biological environments.

</details>


### [28] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş,Orhan Kahraman,Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出强化微任务学习（RMTL），将操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，每个微任务使用独立的VLM奖励提示，结合多视角观测和逆向课程学习，实现比单提示VLM奖励更高效的机器人操作强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单提示VLM奖励在长程操作任务中奖励信号过于粗糙，在轨迹大部分区域接近平坦，导致智能体难以检测早期进展；而手动设计密集奖励函数难以调优且脆弱，人类演示/偏好学习则成本高昂。

Method: 1) 将操作任务分解为少量语言描述的微任务，每个微任务有独立的VLM奖励提示；2) 多视角VLM奖励平均以减少特定视角遮挡影响；3) 逆向课程学习逐步暴露更难初始条件；4) 先使用固定距离规则选择活跃微任务训练PPO worker，再替换为学习的层次化管理器实现完全学习策略。

Result: 在Fetch操作环境中，RMTL相比单提示VLM奖励提供了更丰富的奖励信号，实现了更快的强化学习收敛速度。

Conclusion: 将VLM奖励分解为微任务特定的语言提示可以显著提升语言引导的机器人操作强化学习的可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [29] [LiMoDE: Rethinking Lifelong Robot Manipulation from a Mixture-of-Dynamic-Experts Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26183)
*Zhihao Gu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LiMoDE，一种基于动态混合专家架构的两阶段终身机器人操作学习方案，通过多任务预训练学习先验知识，并在任务适应阶段结合终身专家与冻结专家实现知识迁移，在模拟和真实任务中展现了优越的终身适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在单任务适应中使用参数高效微调缓解灾难性遗忘，但无法提取可复用技能并有效建模技能间交互，且缺乏持续任务适应的能力。

Method: 提出两阶段学习方案：1) 多任务预训练阶段构建动态MoE结构，根据运动信息激活不同数量的异构专家处理短期操作；2) 任务适应阶段设计终身MoE适应机制，学习终身专家并与冻结专家动态组合，促进知识迁移。

Result: 在模拟终身学习基准和真实世界任务上验证，引入适度的额外可训练参数和推理开销下，实现了优越的性能和强大的终身适应能力。

Conclusion: LiMoDE通过动态MoE架构从架构视角有效解决了机器人终身操作中的知识复用与技能交互问题，为持续任务适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: Building a generalist robot that can leverage prior knowledge for continuous task adaptation remains a significant challenge. Previous works alleviate the catastrophic forgetting problem by parameter-efficient fine-tuning for single-task adaptation. However, they fail to extract reusable skills and model the interaction with other skills effectively. Recent works try to address these issues by learning prompts. Differently, this paper presents an architectural perspective on the Lifelong Mixture of Dynamic Experts (\textit{LiMoDE}), a novel two-stage learning scheme for lifelong robot manipulation. Specifically, a dynamic MoE structure is first proposed in the multi-task pre-training stage to learn prior knowledge, where a varied number of heterogeneous experts are activated based on the motion information to address different short-term manipulations. Subsequently, in the task adaptation stage, we design a lifelong MoE adaptation mechanism % (LiMoEAM) that learns lifelong experts and dynamically combines them with frozen ones for new tasks, facilitating the knowledge transfer during adaptation. The proposed \textit{LiMoDE} is evaluated on both the simulated lifelong learning benchmark and real-world tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving superior performance and strong lifelong adaptation by introducing a moderate number of additional trainable parameters and inference overhead.

</details>


### [30] [Morphology-Specific Closed-Loop Control of Logarithmic-Spiral Continuum Arms via Online Jacobian Error Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26188)
*Partha Datta,Yi Jin,Wei Lin,C. Chase Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了针对对数螺旋连续体机械臂的形态特异性闭环任务空间控制框架。该框架基于分段肌腱驱动模型，推导了分析性任务空间雅可比矩阵，并结合Broyden更新和卡尔曼滤波进行在线误差补偿。仿真验证表明，该方法在轨迹跟踪、姿态调节、干扰抑制等方面优于传统PCC基线方法，并成功应用于障碍辅助抓取、自适应臂抓取及多臂协作操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数螺旋形态在生物附属结构中普遍存在，为连续体机械臂提供了理想的缠绕、抓取形态，但现有对数螺旋机器人缺乏逆运动学和闭环控制能力，亟需建立专用控制框架以发挥其形态优势。

Method: 采用分段肌腱驱动模型（中心线骨架+等边肌腱布局）在MuJoCo中建模；推导基于对数螺旋运动学的分析任务空间雅可比矩阵；结合Broyden割线更新和卡尔曼滤波进行在线雅可比误差补偿，持续修正非线性变形、接触和几何失配引起的建模误差。

Result: 在平面与空间仿真中验证了轨迹跟踪、姿态调节、干扰抑制、三维位置跟踪及位置-姿态联合控制性能；与PCC基线相比，该方法持续降低跟踪误差、抑制姿态漂移并保持有界雅可比估计误差；成功实现障碍辅助绕-伸-放运动、自适应整臂抓取及多臂协作操作。

Conclusion: 将对数螺旋形态与在线雅可比补偿相结合，能够实现对高度欠驱动连续体机械臂的精确、鲁棒且可扩展控制；该框架为后续硬件实现和学习增强型软体机器人控制建立了物理驱动的基线。

Abstract: Logarithmic spirals are ubiquitous in biological appendages and provide an attractive morphology for continuum manipulators capable of reaching, wrapping, and grasping. Recently reported logarithmic-spiral robots demonstrated scalable fabrication and versatile grasping but lacked inverse kinematics and closed-loop control. This work presents the first morphology-specific closed-loop task-space control framework for logarithmic-spiral continuum arms. A segmented tendon-driven model with a centerline backbone and equilateral tendon routing is developed in MuJoCo to capture tapered compliance and contact dynamics. An analytical task-space Jacobian is derived directly from the logarithmic-spiral kinematics and combined with online Jacobian error compensation using a Broyden secant update and Kalman-filter estimation. The resulting controller continuously corrects modeling errors arising from nonlinear deformation, contact, and geometric mismatch. The framework is validated through planar and spatial simulations, including trajectory tracking, attitude regulation, disturbance rejection, three-dimensional position tracking, and simultaneous position-orientation control. Compared with a piecewise-constant-curvature (PCC) baseline, the proposed method consistently reduces tracking errors, suppresses attitude drift, and maintains a bounded Jacobian estimation error. The controller is further applied to morphology-enabled manipulation tasks, including obstacle-assisted reach-wrap-release motions, adaptive whole-arm grasping, and cooperative multi-arm object handling. Results demonstrate that combining logarithmic-spiral morphology with online Jacobian compensation enables accurate, robust, and scalable control of highly underactuated continuum manipulators. The proposed framework establishes a physics-grounded baseline for future hardware implementation and learning-augmented soft robotic control.

</details>


### [31] [OmniContact: Chaining Meta-Skills via Contact Flow for Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26201)
*Runyi Yu,Xiaoyi Lin,Ji Ma,Yinhuai Wang,Koukou Luo,Jiahao Ji,Huayi Wang,Wenjia Wang,Runhan Zhang,Ping Tan,Ting Wu,Ruoli Dai,Qifeng Chen,Lei Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于接触流(Contact Flow)的分层框架OmniContact，包含CF-Track底层技能库和CF-Gen高层规划器，用于人形机器人长时程移动操作任务的鲁棒执行与自动恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程人形移动操作面临双重挑战：既需要元技能的鲁棒执行，又需要闭环链接与自主恢复。现有方法中，显式的人-物交互表示虽然精确但难以用于高层规划，隐式技能嵌入虽紧凑但缺乏可解释性，无法可靠组合。

Method: 提出OmniContact分层框架：1) 以接触流(CF)——关键身体轨迹+时序二值接触信号——作为共享接口；2) 底层策略CF-Track学习统一移动操作技能库；3) 高层模块CF-Gen启发式合成未来接触流序列；4) 收集基于动作捕捉的OmniContact数据集。

Result: Carry Box任务成功率98.7%，Push-Stack Boxes任务成功率76.5%，在元技能和技能链接上分别超越基线平均40.9%和66.5%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了鲁棒执行、自主故障恢复和元技能的灵活组合，并能与VLM集成实现语义级任务分解（如将散落盒子排列成心形）。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation poses a dual challenge: it requires not only the robust execution of meta-skills but also their seamless, closed-loop chaining equipped with autonomous recovery. Existing approaches remain limited: explicit humanoid-object interaction representations offer precision but are notoriously difficult for high-level planning, whereas implicit skill embeddings are compact but lack the interpretability required for reliable composition. We propose \ours, a hierarchical framework centered on \textbf{contact flow (CF)}, a compact representation consisting of key body trajectories and time-series binary contact signals. Leveraging this shared interface, our low-level policy \textbf{CF-Track} learns a unified library of loco-manipulation skills, while our high-level module \textbf{CF-Gen} heuristically synthesizes future contact-flow sequences. To support this setting, we additionally collect the OmniContact dataset, a MoCap-based HOI corpus for humanoid loco-manipulation (Appendix~\ref{sec:dataset}). Together, they enable robust execution, autonomous failure recovery, and flexible composition of meta-skills for long-horizon tasks. Experiments show that OmniContact achieves \(98.7\%\) success on \textit{Carry Box} and \(76.5\%\) on \textit{Push-Stack Boxes}, outperforming prior baselines by average margins of \(40.9\%\) in meta-skill and \(66.5\%\) in skill chaining. Besides, our framework naturally integrates with VLMs for semantic task decomposition, enabling complex, semantically grounded loco-manipulation behaviors, such as arranging scattered boxes into a heart shape.

</details>


### [32] [RoboTales: ROBOTic Anthropomorphic LEarning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26213)
*Andrew Chen,Ju-Hung Chen,Phurinat Pinyomit,Alexis E. Block*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTales是一个低成本机器人故事讲述系统，通过在Baxter机器人上实现自主木偶表演，同步叙述、手势和嘴部动作，研究表明木偶模式相比纯手势模式能显著提升用户参与度和故事记忆效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种低成本的、屏幕无关的机器人故事讲述方式，通过具身木偶表演来增强儿童的叙事理解和参与度，替代被动的媒体消费模式。

Method: 在Baxter机器人上实现RoboTales系统，将叙述、手势和嘴部动作同步，并通过木偶表演的形式进行故事讲述；在初步研究中将木偶模式与纯手势模式进行对比评估。

Result: 木偶故事讲述模式在HRIES评分和故事回忆方面均优于纯手势模式，表明具身木偶表演能提升参与度和叙事理解能力。

Conclusion: RoboTales作为一种模块化、平台无关的低成本系统，可适配其他机械臂，为儿童学习环境提供无屏幕的互动式故事讲述方案。

Abstract: RoboTales is a low-cost robotic storytelling system that animates narratives using expressive sock puppetry. Implemented autonomously on a Baxter robot as a test case, RoboTales synchronizes narration, gestures, and mouth movements to perform character-driven stories. In a pilot study, puppet-based storytelling outperformed a gesture-only mode, producing higher HRIES ratings and improved story recall, suggesting that embodied puppetry enhances engagement and narrative comprehension. Designed to be modular and platform-agnostic, RoboTales can be adapted to other manipulators and offers a screen-free alternative to passive media, supporting future deployment in child-centered learning environments.

</details>


### [33] [TaskNPoint: How to Teach Your Humanoid to Hit a Backhand in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26215)
*Blake Werner,Ilona Demler,Pietro Perona,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskNPoint训练协议，通过人类教练提供少量示范和交互窗口定义，让机器人在物理仿真中学习动态技能（如网球击球、踢球、抓取），并能在单GPU上不到一小时训练后零样本泛化到新目标位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态技能学习方法通常需要大量数据（如观看数千小时比赛视频），但人类学习此类技能（如网球反手）是通过教练指导和针对性练习，而非被动观看大量演示。作者认为机器人学习也应采用类似的"教练-学习者"分工范式，以提高样本效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出TaskNPoint训练协议，将教练与学习者的分工显式化：人类教练提供离散技能集（如正手/反手）、每个技能一个示范、交互窗口识别以及目标定义；学习者在物理仿真环境中通过随机目标采样训练，填充完整动作轨迹，实现零样本泛化到未见过的目标位置。无需每个任务单独设计奖励函数。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了该方法，能够成功执行正手/反手击球、踢球和拾取放置等任务。仅需短时间人类视频示范，在单GPU上不到一小时完成训练，无需逐任务奖励调参。

Conclusion: 动态技能学习的关键在于掌握少量关键动作（交互窗口），而不是大量数据驱动的端到端学习。TaskNPoint通过模仿人类"教练指导+练习"的学习模式，实现了高效、可泛化的技能获取。

Abstract: How do we learn to hit a tennis backhand? Not from a thousand hours of tennis tournaments on TV - we work with a coach and practice. We argue this is also the right recipe for teaching dynamic skills to humanoid robots. This follows from a structural property of dynamic skills: the outcome is decided by a short, crucial portion of the trajectory - for a backhand, the ~20cm of racket travel around ball contact. Getting this interaction window right requires coordinating the whole motion, so that control, physics, and morphology act in concert. Learning thus reduces to mastering a handful of distinct actions and, for each, practicing until the window comes out right. To this end, we introduce TaskNPoint, a training protocol which makes the coach-learner division of labor explicit. The human coach contributes four inputs: a discrete set of skills (e.g. different shots), one demonstration per skill, identification of the interaction window, and the goal. Learning in a physically realistic simulation environment fills in each action trajectory and provides robustness to unmodeled events. Crucially, randomized target sampling during training lets a single demonstration generalize zero-shot to unseen goal locations. We test this approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid that hits forehands and backhands against balls thrown by a human, kicks incoming soccer balls, and picks and places boxes from novel locations. We find that learning is successful from short human video demonstrations and under an hour of training on a single GPU, with no per-task reward tuning.

</details>


### [34] [NavIsaacLab: Generating Realistic Crowd via Parallel Robot Learning for Benchmarking Human-aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26265)
*Bingyi Xia,Han Bao,Jingyu Zhu,Hanjing Ye,Yuhan Pang,Guangcheng Chen,Liang Lin,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavIsaacLab框架，基于Isaac Lab构建物理逼真、支持GPU并行仿真的行人感知导航平台，通过数据驱动的轨迹扩散模型和对抗运动控制器实现可控的行人模拟，并集成跨尺度场景为行人感知导航算法提供基准测试与训练环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前行人感知导航研究面临高质量场景数据稀缺、现有仿真平台依赖手工规则模拟行人行为、缺乏真实传感器信号（假设完美观测）等问题，亟需一个具备物理真实性和丰富感知信号的仿真框架。

Method: 基于Isaac Lab构建框架，利用其照片级场景渲染和GPU并行仿真能力；采用数据驱动的轨迹扩散模型生成行人轨迹，结合对抗运动学习控制器实现物理可控的行人模拟；集成跨尺度多样化场景作为基准。

Result: 实现了实时准确的3D视觉反馈、物理逼真的行人行为模拟，以及面向行人感知导航算法的鲁棒基准测试平台。

Conclusion: NavIsaacLab通过物理仿真和数据驱动的行人建模，有效解决了现有平台在场景多样性和传感器真实性上的不足，为训练和评估行人感知导航策略提供了高效、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Robot autonomous navigation that accounts for surrounding human activities is crucial for ensuring both safety and natural human-robot interaction in real-world environments shared by humans and robots. Simulation of complex and diverse navigation scenarios serves as the foundation for training reliable robot navigation policies and accurately evaluating the performance of algorithms, offering an efficient alternative to manual supervision of real data. However, current human-aware navigation research faces significant challenges due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality scene data. Existing simulation platforms often rely on handcrafted rules to approximate pedestrian behavior and lack the capability to provide extensive sensor signals, typically assuming perfect observations. To address these limitations, this paper presents NavIsaacLab, a comprehensive framework for benchmarking and training human-aware navigation policies through physics-based and photo-realistic simulations of pedestrians and scenes. Based on Isaac Lab, the proposed framework employs photo-realistic scene rendering capabilities and supports parallel simulation on GPU, delivering real-time and accurate 3D visual feedback to robots. To enhance the realism of human behavior, a data-driven approach is employed that incorporates a trajectory diffusion model and an adversarial motion learning controller, enabling controllable, physics-based pedestrian simulation. Furthermore, the integration of diverse cross-scale scenes provides a robust benchmark for state-of-the-art human-aware navigation methods.

</details>


### [35] [Layered Outer-Loop Control for Disturbance-Robust Multi-Waypoint UAV Arrival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26315)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多航点无人机位置调节的分层终端控制架构，并在PyBullet、PX4/Gazebo和实际硬件上进行了分阶段评估。第一阶段通过PyBullet基准测试识别出分离平滑逼近、持续偏置补偿和监督近目标终端调节的控制器核心；第二阶段将该架构迁移至PX4/Gazebo闭环环境中，暴露了基准增益在部署类环境中的迁移问题。最终硬件验证在Vicon追踪的Tello平台上完成。结果表明，将主控制器设计与基准特定优化分离，并在更严苛的闭环评估下仍保持稳健，能使基准成功更具信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机位置控制在仿真中表现良好，但在实际部署中难以兼顾快速逼近、近目标行为可控及跨平台可信度。作者旨在提出一种更具迁移性和鲁棒性的控制架构，并建立分阶段评估方法以揭示基准测试到实际部署的性能鸿沟。

Method: 提出分层终端控制架构，包含三个核心组件：平滑逼近生成、持续偏置补偿、监督式近目标终端调节。采用三阶段评估：Phase I在PyBullet+随机风环境下进行快速结构选择；Phase II将核心架构迁移至PX4/Gazebo级联飞行栈闭环中，暴露延迟敏感性和过渡-悬停耦合问题；最终在Vicon追踪的Tello硬件上完成两级硬件验证。

Result: Phase I中裸控制器达到0.024m平均后期风误差；Phase II中基于迁移导向规则（强调无基准先验、跨场景平衡、可部署监督逻辑）选出最终控制器；Grace事后分析显示部分残留故障集对完成语义敏感而非航点丢失行为。

Conclusion: 将主控制器设计与基准特定优化相分离，并在更逼真的闭环评估下仍保持设计可辩护性，能使基准测试成功更具信息量和实际参考价值。

Abstract: Disturbance-robust UAV position control is easy to demonstrate in benign simulations but much harder to make fast in approach, well behaved near the target, and credible beyond a single benchmark. This letter presents a layered terminal-control architecture for multi-waypoint UAV position regulation together with a staged evaluation across PyBullet, PX4/Gazebo, and hardware. Phase I uses a PyBullet benchmark with stochastic wind for rapid structural selection, identifying a controller core that separates smooth approach generation, persistent-bias compensation, and supervised near-target terminal regulation. Phase II carries only that main architecture into a more demanding PX4/Gazebo closed loop, where the outer-loop controller acts through a cascaded flight stack with delay-sensitive settling and stronger transit-to-hover coupling. This step exposes which benchmark gains survive autopilot-mediated dynamics and which refinements collapse once the loop becomes more deployment-like. In Phase I, the bare controller attains 0.024 m mean late-stage wind error. In Phase II, the final controller is selected using a transfer-oriented rule emphasizing absence of benchmark priors, cross-scenario balance, and deployable supervisory logic. Strict is used as the primary reporting reference; the supplementary retrospective Grace analysis shows that part of the residual failure set is sensitive to completion semantics rather than gross waypoint-miss behaviour. The evaluation is completed on one Vicon-tracked Tello stack through a two-level hardware study. Taken together, the results suggest that benchmark success becomes more informative when the main controller design is separated from benchmark-specific refinement and remains defensible under harder closed-loop evaluation.

</details>
