To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [72] [BOWConnect: Parallel Bayesian Optimization over Windows with Learned Local Cost Maps for Sample-Efficient Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27292)
*Sourav Raxit,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Jose Fuentes,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BOWConnect是一种双向并行运动学动力学运动规划器，通过集成贝叶斯优化窗口（BOW）学习驱动函数、双向并行树探索和空间哈希机制，在10个基准环境及真实地面车辆/四旋翼飞行器上实现了100%成功率、最快规划时间和无碰撞实时规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的运动规划方法存在三个根本性局限：高维状态空间的样本低效性、动态约束下成本启发式不可靠、狭窄通道环境中性能差。

Method: 将贝叶斯优化窗口（BOW）作为学习型转向函数集成到并行树探索框架中，使每个工作线程学习局部成本图和约束以引导采样；采用双向架构并行生长前向和后向树，配合空间哈希加速连接查询和边界值求解器生成动力学一致的桥接轨迹。

Result: 在10个基准环境中实现100%成功率，在复杂场景（包括狭窄通道和非凸空间）中达到最快或接近最快的规划时间，在真实地面车辆和四旋翼飞行器上实现无碰撞实时规划。

Conclusion: BOWConnect通过将学习的转向函数与双向并行框架相结合，有效克服了传统采样方法的三大局限性，在仿真和真实部署中均表现出色。

Abstract: This paper presents BOWConnect, a bidirectional parallel kinodynamic motion planner that addresses three fundamental limitations of existing sampling-based methods: sample inefficiency in high-dimensional state spaces, unreliable cost heuristics under dynamic constraints, and poor performance in narrow passage environments. Unlike classical planners that rely on random control sampling and geometric distance heuristics, BOWConnect integrates Bayesian Optimization over Windows (BOW) as a learning-based steering function within a parallel tree-based exploration framework, enabling each worker to learn local cost maps and constraints to guide sampling toward dynamically feasible and collision-free controls. A bidirectional architecture simultaneously grows forward and backward trees from the start and goal regions in parallel threads, with a spatial hashing mechanism enabling fast connection queries and a boundary value problem solver generating kinodynamically consistent bridge trajectories. Extensive evaluations across ten benchmark environments demonstrate that BOWConnect achieves 100\% success while delivering the fastest or near-fastest planning time in complex scenarios, including narrow passages and non-convex spaces where state-of-the-art planners fail or degrade substantially. Real-world deployment on a ground vehicle and a quadrotor confirms real-time planning with no collisions. Videos of real-world and simulated experiments, high-resolution versions of the figures, and the open-source code are available at https://bow-connect.github.io/.

</details>


### [73] [LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27295)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yilei Zhong,Bing Cheng,Yiming Wang,Jiting Liu,Yang Tian,Junchi Yan,Feiran Wu,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Yuqian Fu,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LA4VLA提出了一种不依赖视觉观测的语言-动作预训练框架，通过从专家演示中解耦出语言-动作对（LA4-33K数据集），让VLA模型在预训练阶段学习语言条件化的动作先验，从而减少对视觉捷径的依赖。实验表明，混合LA-VLA预训练在仿真和真实任务上分别比无预训练基线提升17.8%和45.0%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在预训练时，密集的视觉-动作监督信号会压制稀疏的语言-动作信号，导致策略倾向于利用视觉捷径（visual shortcuts）而非真正学习语言如何调节动作执行，从而对视觉变化敏感。作者希望通过解耦语言-动作信号来让模型获得语言条件化的动作先验。

Method: 1) 将专家演示轨迹分解为原子动作段，并为每段配对一个底层动作描述，构建LA4-33K数据集（33K语言-动作片段）。2) 提出LA4VLA-1B（1B参数轻量VLA模型）。3) 探索三种语言-动作监督融入VLA学习的范式：仅LA预训练、顺序LA-to-VLA预训练、混合LA-VLA预训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实任务中，LA预训练策略始终优于匹配的VLA预训练策略。混合LA-VLA预训练使LA4VLA-1B在仿真任务中平均成功率提升17.8个百分点，在真实任务中提升45.0个百分点。

Conclusion: LA4VLA是一种有效且互补的预训练策略，能够构建更强、更鲁棒的VLA策略。语言-动作先验可以捕获跨任务和场景的可复用操作技能，减少对场景特定视觉线索的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [74] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct提出了一种通过共享接触与滑移的物理表征，将真实压电麦克风振动信号与仿真强化学习相结合的框架，使灵巧手策略能够利用触觉反馈进行训练和迁移，在五类接触密集任务中优于纯本体感知与点云基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖快速、局部且常被视觉遮挡的接触事件，压电麦克风虽能高带宽感知振动信号，但现有方法难以端到端地从仿真噪声模拟到真实策略迁移，亟需一种可泛化的触觉表征桥接真实传感与仿真训练。

Method: 1) 在真实灵巧手中嵌入压电麦克风，遥操作采集振动数据；2) 在标定数字孪生中回放数据，自动标注逐指接触与滑移标签；3) 训练触觉估计器从真实麦克风波形预测接触/滑移；4) 在仿真中基于相同表征（直接从仿真接触计算）训练操作策略，解耦触觉反馈与原始音频模拟。

Result: 在重抓取、手中重定向和插入等五类任务中，VibeAct在仿真中一致优于本体感知+点云基线，在需要持续反应控制的任务上增益最大；连续滑移幅度通道是最具信息量的观测量；学到的策略成功迁移到真实灵巧手-臂平台。

Conclusion: 通过共享物理表征（接触/滑移）桥接真实触觉传感与仿真强化学习是可行且有效的，该方法避免了模拟原始音频的困难，同时保留了快速触觉反馈的优势，为灵巧手触觉策略的sim-to-real迁移提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [75] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的无人机竞速零样本泛化框架，通过结合任务感知切换与物理信息化赛道生成器，在真实世界多种未见赛道上实现7.4倍泛化性能提升，同时保持竞争力竞速速度，并成功应用于无显式状态估计的端到端视觉控制场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL无人机竞速策略在特定环境中表现良好，但无法泛化到未见配置；现有提升泛化能力的方法会显著降低飞行速度，性能与泛化之间存在严重权衡。

Method: 提出结合(1)基于学习进度的任务感知切换机制与(2)物理信息化的程序化赛道生成器的框架，训练出快速且鲁棒的通用策略，无需测试时自适应。

Result: 在真实世界多种未见赛道上实现零样本泛化，相比最先进方法泛化性能提升7.4倍，同时保持竞争性竞速速度。在无显式状态估计的端到端视觉控制中，所有先前方法均失败而本方法成功泛化。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了敏捷飞行中零样本泛化的难题，在不牺牲速度的前提下大幅提升泛化能力，为RL在高速自主飞行中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [76] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种持续学习框架，通过结合物理先验和神经残差的动力学模型，以及循环编码器推断隐条件，使机器人能够在部署中利用经验改进策略，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中比在线残差重拟合快约5倍恢复扰动，并分别降低65.7%和53.3%的悬停和跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器人面临动态变化（如风力、载荷、电池等），但大多数基于学习的控制器训练一次后即部署，无法利用部署经验进一步提升性能。

Method: 1) 结合解析物理先验与神经残差构建条件感知动力学模型；2) 循环编码器从近期交互推断当前隐条件，该估计同时调节残差模型和策略；3) 通过可微分仿真使用从潜模型采样的多样化学习动力学进行策略学习；4) 部署时用在线推断的条件替代采样条件，通过识别而非残差重拟合恢复重复出现的动态。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，策略约1秒内恢复重复出现的扰动，比在线残差重拟合快约5倍；在大的扰动下，悬停和跟踪误差分别比最先进的在线自适应方法降低65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过识别而非重拟合的方式处理重复出现的动态变化，显著提升了机器人在不可观测扰动下的策略性能，验证了在仿真和真实世界中的有效性。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [77] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RouterVLA 提出利用部署前评估数据来监督策略选择，通过结果不相交的交叉拟合方法，在 34,752 条 LIBERO-Plus 记录上将成功率从 0.4686 提升至 0.6149（+14.64pp），并揭示评分复用会膨胀收益 1.87 倍，证明了委托（commissioning）本身具有路由价值，而额外标量评分器能力并不创造该价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究能否复用部署前的评估 rollout 数据来监督策略选择，从而提升机器人团队中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的整体系统性能。

Method: 提出 RouterVLA 方法，采用结果不相交的交叉拟合（outcome-disjoint cross-fitting）：用记录的探测数据构建每个冻结专家的档案，再用独立的试驾评分所选专家，确保评分数据不进入专家档案。通过透明探针-成功规则和可学习评分器进行对比实验。

Result: 在 34,752 条 LIBERO-Plus 记录上，透明探针-成功规则将留出成功率从 0.4686 提升至 0.6149（+14.64pp）。基于标量档案的可学习评分器与这一规则统计上无显著差异。复用评分试驾会膨胀测量收益 1.87 倍。

Conclusion: 委托（commissioning）本身携带路由价值，额外标量评分器能力并不创造该价值；可信的账本式路由需要结果分离；模型扩展提升个体策略性能，而委托感知路由提升系统整体性能。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [78] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REGEN利用世界行动模型生成伪重放轨迹，使机器人能够在持续模仿学习中不存储原始人类示范的情况下回顾先前学过的任务，相比顺序微调减少了高达50%的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的持续学习方法需要存储原始人类示范来重放以防灾难性遗忘，但存储大量示范数据存在隐私和存储成本问题。本文旨在探索如何利用世界行动模型的生成能力，在不存储原始演示的情况下实现有效的持续模仿学习。

Method: 提出REGEN框架，利用世界行动模型（WAM）的生成能力，递归地查询WAM合成伪重放轨迹，这些轨迹仅以先前的任务指令和当前任务的观察为条件。在持续适应过程中，机器人通过合成轨迹进行排练，而无需访问原始人类示范。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作实验中，REGEN相比顺序微调减少了高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能接近需要访问真实重放数据的特权经验重放方法。分析揭示了长时域视觉退化和动作-观察不一致是限制生成重放性能的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 世界行动模型可作为无需存储演示的持续机器人学习的有前景的基础，REGEN框架有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，但长时域生成质量和动作-观察一致性仍有待改进。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [79] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个完全开源的行为克隆操作栈，包含最大开源遥操作数据集ABC-130K（3500小时、13万+片段、195个任务），并提供硬件、训练基础设施、仿真管线以及400小时仿真遥操作数据。通过共训练方案实现仿真与现实评估相关性，探索了DiT和VLA模型的架构选择与训练策略，在真实机器人上完成折纸盒、从钱包取信用卡等灵巧操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆领域缺乏大规模、标准化、可复现的开源平台，现有数据集规模有限且难以对比不同模型架构和训练决策的效果，研究者难以公平起步。ABC旨在提供完整可复现的工具包，使社区能够平等地学习和推进行为克隆。

Method: 构建ABC-130K数据集（195个任务、3500小时、13万+片段），开源硬件平台、训练基础设施和仿真管线；收集400小时仿真遥操作数据，设计共训练方案使仿真评估与真实评估相关；系统对比Diffusion Transformers (DiT)和Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型的架构选择与训练策略。

Result: ABC-130K是迄今最大开源遥操作数据集；共训练方案实现了仿真与真实评估的相关性，可作为真实评估的可靠代理；训练的策略成功执行了折纸盒、从钱包取信用卡等灵巧操作任务；通过对比实验为DiT和VLA模型的设计与训练决策提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: ABC为行为克隆研究提供了完整的开源基础设施，降低了研究门槛，使社区能够在大规模标准化平台上公平地比较和推进各种方法，学会行为克隆的"ABC"。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Orchestrating Black-Box Schema Converters: An Empirical Study of Automated, Quality-Ranked Conversion Across Heterogeneous Schema Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26180)
*Felix Neubauer,Giridhar Chinnikkaramadom Govindan,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模式转换编排方法，将异构的模式语言转换器视为有向图中的黑盒节点，通过路径发现、执行、排序和完整溯源来处理转换任务。实现为开源工具Schema Conversion Orchestrator，在60个真实模式转换任务中成功解决了43个，其余失败定位了当前转换器生态的具体缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统常需要在多种模式语言（如JSON Schema、XSD、SHACL）中维护同一数据模型，但现有转换器难以寻找、分布零散、质量参差不齐且常丢失信息，导致模型演化的构建和维护负担沉重。

Method: 将模式语言建模为节点，转换器（视为黑盒）建模为有向边，转换任务转化为图的路径发现问题。路径经过发现、执行、排序和报告，每个步骤附带完整溯源信息，失败时尝试替代路径。基于此实现开源Schema Conversion Orchestrator，并集成到MetaConfigurator中。

Result: 在60个跨5种模式语言的真实模式转换任务上评估，编排方法为43个任务提供了可用结果，剩余17个失败任务精确标定了当前转换器生态的具体缺口。

Conclusion: 编排异构的、有损的转换器能够自动化、可复现且质量可排序地完成大部分模式转换任务，同时为工具开发者和转换质量评估提供了改进方向。

Abstract: Modern software systems routinely need the same data model in several schema languages: a model may exist as JSON Schema for a web API, as XSD for data exchange, and as SHACL for a knowledge graph. Keeping these representations consistent as the model evolves is a recurring construction and maintenance burden, because converters between schema languages are hard to find, scattered across ecosystems, of uneven quality, and frequently lossy. We study, empirically, to what extent such imperfect, heterogeneous converters can be orchestrated into automated, reproducible, and quality-ranked conversions, and where the current converter landscape reaches its limits. Our approach models schema languages as nodes and converters, treated as black boxes, as directed edges, so that conversions become paths that are discovered, executed, ranked, and reported with full per-step provenance, with failures handled by trying alternatives. We realize it as the open-source Schema Conversion Orchestrator, integrate it into MetaConfigurator, and evaluate it on 60 conversion tasks built from real-world schemas across five schema languages, using agent-assisted, human-reviewed quality annotations. Orchestration surfaces a usable result for 43 of 60 tasks; the remaining failures localize concrete gaps in the converter landscape. We discuss implications for tool builders and for measuring conversion quality.

</details>


### [81] [Augmentation with Dilution: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Human Contributor Ecosystems After AI Coding Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26289)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究AI编码代理对开源项目中人类贡献者生态系统的影响。采用交错双重差分法分析11,097个GitHub仓库数据，发现AI代理并未显著改变人类贡献者绝对数量，但显著降低了人类贡献者密度（-1.9%），新参与者相对份额下降3.7个百分点，审查深度增加5.3%，呈现"增强伴随稀释"的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将人类贡献者视为静态背景，缺乏对AI编码代理采纳后人类参与者生态系统（数量、组成、行为）如何变化的系统理解。

Method: 基于2023年1月至2026年5月11,097个GitHub仓库数据集，采用交错双重差分设计（Staggered DiD）及Sun and Abraham估计量进行因果推断。

Result: AI代理采纳未显著改变人类贡献者绝对数量（ATT=0.014, p=0.224），但显著降低人类贡献者密度（ATT=-0.019, p=0.002）；新参与者相对份额下降3.7个百分点（ATT=-0.037, p<0.001）；审查深度增加5.3%（ATT=+0.0168, p<0.001）；效应随项目规模、编程语言和项目成熟度系统变化。

Conclusion: AI代理并非替代人类贡献者，而是系统性地重塑了开源生态系统的参与结构，表现为"增强伴随稀释"模式——将负担从代码生产阶段转移到审查阶段。

Abstract: AI coding agents are penetrating open-source software development at an unprecedented pace, yet existing research predominantly treats human contributors as a static backdrop rather than as the subject of inquiry. This paper presents the first large-scale empirical study that takes the human contributor ecosystem as its dependent variable, examining how the number, composition, and behavior of human participants change following AI coding agent adoption in open-source projects. Using a staggered difference-in-differences design on a dataset of 11,097 GitHub repositories spanning January 2023 to May 2026, we provide causal evidence via the Sun and Abraham estimator. Our results show that AI agent adoption does not significantly change the absolute number of human contributors (ATT = 0.014, p = 0.224), but significantly reduces human contributor density (ATT = -0.019, p = 0.002), indicating that the relative share of human participation declines as AI-generated pull requests accumulate. The relative participation share of newcomers declines significantly by 3.7 percentage points (ATT = -0.037, p < 0.001), with the effect emerging immediately after adoption and remaining stable throughout the observation window. Review depth increases significantly by 5.3% (ATT = +0.0168, p < 0.001), indicating that AI agents shift burden from the code production stage to the review stage. Moderator analysis reveals that these effects vary systematically with project size, programming language, and project maturity. Together, these findings present a pattern of augmentation with dilution: AI agents are not displacing human contributors, but are systematically reshaping the participation structure of open-source ecosystems.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Safety-Aware Mutation Testing for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26456)
*Donghwan Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向安全意识的变异测试方法（SAMT），通过在ADS模块间消息中注入时间约束故障来模拟真实交互失败，并基于STPA等安全工程框架推导变异规则，以系统性地评估测试充分性、指导场景生成和系统修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仿真的ADS测试缺乏何时停止生成测试场景的系统性标准；已有覆盖度指标未能捕获导致大多数ADS事故的组件交互故障；传统变异测试直接应用于ADS各模块（代码级和深度学习模型级）不足以反映真实的交互安全问题。

Method: 提出Safety-Aware Mutation Testing (SAMT)范式转变：在ADS模块间交换的消息中注入时间约束故障（temporally bounded faults），而非在单个组件内部注入人工故障；从自上而下的安全工程框架（如STPA）推导变异生成规则，将系统思维融入变异测试流程。

Result: SAMT可提供严格的测试充分性评估机制，支持自动化场景生成并指导ADS修复。本文同时指出若干关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: SAMT通过关注模块间交互故障（而非单组件故障），将安全工程思想引入变异测试，为ADS测试充分性评估提供了更系统、更贴近实际事故成因的新方向。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is essential for ensuring the safety of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS), yet the community lacks a systematic criterion for determining when we can safely stop additional test scenario generation. Existing coverage metrics typically focus on individual component reliability or treat the ADS as a black box, failing to capture certain component interactions that cause most ADS accidents. While traditional mutation testing provides a falsifiable measure of test adequacy, directly porting code- and deep learning model-level mutations to the corresponding modules of ADS is insufficient.
  In this vision paper, we propose a paradigm shift toward Safety-Aware Mutation Testing (SAMT). Unlike traditional mutation testing, which creates mutants (i.e., faulty versions of the software under test) by injecting artificial faults into individual components, SAMT systematically injects temporally bounded faults into the messages exchanged between ADS modules to simulate realistic interaction failures. To ensure these mutants represent genuine hazards, we propose deriving mutant generation rules directly from top-down safety engineering frameworks, such as System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). By embedding systems thinking into the mutation testing pipeline, SAMT provides a rigorous mechanism for evaluating test adequacy, enabling automated scenario generation, and guiding ADS repair. We also outline critical open challenges.

</details>


### [83] [An Empirical Study of LLM-Generated Specifications for VeriFast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26490)
*Wen Fan,Minh Tran,Sanya Dod,Xin Hu,Marilyn Rego,Danning Xie,Jenna DiVincenzo,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大语言模型在生成分离逻辑验证器VeriFast规范时的表现，测试了303个C函数、8种提示方法、10个LLM和3种输入类型。结果表明LLM能较好地保持功能行为（>91%），但验证成功率仅31.4%，且94%的错误源于LLM对VeriFast领域知识的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离逻辑验证器在验证堆操作程序方面表现出色，但需要大量复杂辅助规范来描述堆结构，人工编写成本高昂。现有LLM生成规范的工作主要针对非SL验证器，针对SL验证器生成规范的研究存在空白。

Method: 设计两阶段实验，使用8种提示方法、10个LLM和3种输入类型，对303个C函数进行VeriFast规范生成。采用定量和定性分析评估LLM生成的代码和规范的功能行为、可验证性和错误类型。

Result: 功能行为保持率超91%，但验证成功率仅31.4%。Gemini 2.5 Pro和提供形式化契约的方法成功率更高。94%的错误源于LLM对VeriFast等SL验证器领域知识的错误理解。

Conclusion: LLM在保持功能行为方面表现良好，但在SL验证器领域知识方面存在显著短板。这些发现为优化LLM生成SL验证器规范提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Static verification tools can assure industrial scale software, but require significant human labor to write specifications. This is particularly true of static verifiers based on separation logic (SL verifiers), which excel at verifying heapmanipulating programs, but require many complex auxiliary specifications to reason about heap structure. Recent work applies large language models (LLMs) to generate code, tests, and proofs, including specifications for verifiers, but mostly targeting non-SL verifiers. To address this gap, this paper thoroughly evaluates how well LLMs perform when prompted to generate specifications for verifying 303 C functions with the SL verifier VeriFast. We explored eight prompting approaches, ten LLMs, and three input types in two stages. Quantitative and qualitative analyses are used to assess the LLM-generated code and specifications for functional behavior, verifiability and errors. The results show that LLMs preserve functional behavior in source code and specifications (both over 91%), but achieve modest verification success (31.4%). Using Gemini 2.5 Pro and providing formal contracts lead to higher success rates in our setting. Moreover, most errors (94%) come from LLMs' mistakes in the domainspecific knowledge of SL verifiers such as VeriFast. These findings provide guidance for optimizing LLM-generated specifications for SL verifiers.

</details>


### [84] [Evaluation-Strategy Gap in Fault Diagnosis of Deep Learning Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26492)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度学习程序训练失败诊断技术在不同程序间的性能差距。通过构建包含38个真实DL程序的5,542条故障注入训练轨迹的DynFault语料库，发现现有诊断技术在程序内评估与跨程序评估间存在0.190的平衡准确率差距，并指出该差距源于特征中的程序级结构。曲率特征对未见程序的故障检测有效，而优化器和激活特征仅对已见程序有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习故障诊断技术多采用程序内交叉验证进行评估，但在部署时可能面临从未见过的程序，这种评估方式可能高估实际性能。因此需要量化不同评估设置间的性能差异，并找出导致性能差距的根本原因。

Method: 构建DynFault语料库（5,542条故障注入训练轨迹，来自38个真实DL程序），对比程序内评估与跨程序（留出完整程序）评估的性能差距，分析两类运行时特征（曲率特征和优化器特征）在不同设置下的表现。

Result: 存在0.190的平衡准确率差距；曲率特征对未见程序的故障检测有效，优化器和激活特征仅在已见程序上有帮助；差距源于特征中的程序级结构。

Conclusion: 跨程序评估是必要的，曲率特征具有更好的泛化能力，而优化器和激活特征存在过拟合到特定程序的问题。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) programs can fail during training for many reasons, and diagnosing the cause is a costly and time-consuming maintenance task. Techniques for diagnosing such failures are commonly assessed using within-program cross-validation, which may be inadequate for deployment settings involving previously unseen programs. It is therefore necessary to assess how performance differs across these settings and to identify the causes of any performance gap in established fault diagnosis techniques for DL. We investigate this gap using DynFault, a corpus of 5,542 fault-injected training traces from 38 real-world DL programs. We found a gap of 0.190 in balanced accuracy for existing fault diagnosis techniques between within-program evaluation and holding out whole programs. We also found the gap comes from program-level structure in the features, which led us to examine two runtime feature sets, curvature features and optimizer features, and their behavior on unseen programs. We found that curvature features are useful for instability detection on unseen programs, while optimizer and activation features help only on programs seen during training.

</details>


### [85] [Same Scrutiny, More Time: Eye Tracking Insights into Reviewing LLM-Labelled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26505)
*Ranim Khojah,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Mazen Mohamad,Julian Frattini,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过Wizard-of-Oz实验研究发现，当代码被明确标注为LLM生成时，开发者的代码审查彻底性没有改变，但他们会花更多时间关注这些代码，并调整审查策略（如基于逻辑正确性评估或利用提示词指导审查）。研究揭示了审查者意图与实际行为之间的差距，为LLM工具设计和AI政策制定提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码日益普及，但其仍存在错误和幻觉问题，强调代码审查的重要性。然而，开发者在审查LLM生成代码时的实际行为尚不明确，特别是当代码被明确标注为LLM生成时，开发者的信任度和审查行为如何变化仍缺乏研究。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz实验设计，在代码审查任务中将代码明确标注为LLM生成；通过眼动追踪收集行为数据，通过退出访谈收集主观反馈；结合贝叶斯数据分析与定性分析方法对数据进行分析。

Result: 代码审查的彻底性（thoroughness）未发生显著变化；但参与者在LLM标注的代码上花费了更多注视时间，说明标注本身影响了注意力分配；审查者针对LLM标注代码调整了策略，包括基于特定标准（如逻辑正确性）进行评估，或利用提示词指导审查过程。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了审查者意图与实际审查行为之间的差距，表明仅靠标注不足以改变审查彻底性；建议LLM工具设计应包含标注并纳入提示词作为软件制品；软件公司需重新审视AI政策，以更好地支持开发者审查LLM生成代码。

Abstract: Modern software development increasingly involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate code. Despite their rapid advancement, LLMs remain prone to errors and hallucinations, emphasizing the importance of careful code inspection. However, in practice, developers' trust in LLM-generated code and their willingness to review it thoroughly may differ from these recommendations. How developers actually behave when reviewing LLM-generated code remains largely unexplored. In this study, we conduct a Wizard-of-Oz experiment to examine how software engineers behave when code is explicitly labeled as LLM-generated during a code review task. We collect both behavioral data and participant feedback through eye-tracking and exit interviews. Combining Bayesian data analysis with qualitative analysis, we found that while the thoroughness of code review did not change for participants, they spent more time fixating on LLM-labelled code, indicating that the label itself influences attention. Practitioners also adapted their review strategy for LLM-labelled code by assessing the code based on specific criteria (e.g., logical correctness), or using the prompt to guide their review. These findings inform LLM-based tool design on labelling while incorporating the prompt as a software artifact. Our study reveals a gap between reviewers' intentions and actual reviewing behaviour, highlighting the need for software companies to revisit their AI policies (particularly regarding LLM-assisted development) to better support developers in reviewing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [86] [ConcoLixir: Reactive LLM Discovery Oracles for Python Concolic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26545)
*Dong Chen,Chih-Duo Hong,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConcoLixir是一个将LLM作为发现预言机（discovery oracle）集成到Python混合执行测试中的扩展，通过在种子生成、求解失败后提供具体输入以及覆盖停滞时目标未覆盖代码三个环节引入LLM，在不替换符号求解器的情况下提升覆盖率。在合成、真实世界和库目标上，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点，完整评估仅花费1.63美元API费用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python混合执行测试中存在三个固有限制：库调用导致符号变量降级为具体值；正则表达式、校验和、解析器等语义操作难以通过约束求解处理；探索在已覆盖路径上停滞不前。现有方法无法有效突破这些语义屏障和库边界。

Method: 提出ConcoLixir，将LLM作为反应式发现预言机集成到混合执行流程中，在三个关键点介入：(1) 生成初始种子输入；(2) 当约束求解失败时提出具体候选输入；(3) 当覆盖率停滞时定向生成未覆盖代码的输入。所有候选输入都通过混合执行实际执行，仅基于观测到的覆盖率和收集的路径约束指导后续探索，LLM不替换求解器也不作为正确性预言机。

Result: 在合成目标、真实世界目标和库目标上，相比无LLM预言机的基线混合执行测试器，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点。提升效果在语义屏障和库边界附近最为显著，完整评估的API费用仅1.63美元。

Conclusion: 有界的LLM发现能力可以补充符号推理而无需替换它。将LLM作为轻量级发现预言机以低成本方式突破了传统混合执行测试在语义操作和库调用上的瓶颈。

Abstract: Concolic testing combines concrete execution with symbolic constraint solving, but Python programs expose recurring limits. Library calls can cause symbolic variables to downgrade to concrete values. Regular expressions, checksums, parsers, and other semantic operations can be hard to solve, and exploration can plateau on already covered paths. We present ConcoLixir, a reactive LLM extension for Python concolic execution. The LLM acts as a discovery oracle, not a replacement for the solver or a correctness oracle. It generates initial seeds, proposes concrete inputs after solver failures, and targets uncovered code when coverage stalls. Each candidate is executed concolically, and only observed coverage and collected path constraints guide later exploration. Across synthetic, real-world, and library targets, ConcoLixir improves mean line coverage over the baseline concolic tester without an LLM oracle by 8.6, 15.1, and 17.0 percentage points. The gains are strongest near semantic barriers and library boundaries, and the full evaluation costs \$1.63 in API charges. These results show that bounded LLM discovery can complement symbolic reasoning without replacing it.

</details>


### [87] [Quantum Mutant Equivalence via Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26604)
*José Campos,Andriy Miranskyy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于转译器的量子软件等价变异体识别方法（TBE），通过比较原始电路和变异电路在相同配置下转译后的OpenQASM代码来判断语义等价性。在348,299个存活变异体中，TBE成功识别出29,536个等价变异体（32.1%），实现了100%的精确率和82%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试中的等价变异体问题在量子软件领域尚未解决。大量存活变异体无法确定是因为测试不足还是语义等价，导致测试评估失真和资源浪费。

Method: 提出Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE)方法：将原始量子电路和变异电路在相同转译配置下进行转译，然后比较二者生成的OpenQASM代码是否一致。若一致则判定为等价变异体。

Result: 在348,299个存活变异体中，92,011个为等价变异体；TBE成功识别其中29,536个（32.1%），精确率100%，准确率82%。

Conclusion: TBE是一种轻量级、高精度的量子软件等价变异体识别方法，能够有效缓解量子变异测试中的等价变异体问题，但仍有提升空间（覆盖32.1%的等价变异体）。

Abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test suite quality by introducing artificial faults (mutants) and checking whether tests detect (kill) them. A central challenge is the equivalent mutant problem: some mutants are syntactically different from the original program but semantically identical to it and therefore cannot be killed by any test. If left unidentified, such mutants waste testing effort and distort mutation scores. In quantum software, mutation testing is increasingly used, but the equivalent mutant problem remains unsolved. A recent study generated more than 700,000 quantum circuit mutants and found that roughly half survived the available tests, making it unclear whether these survivors reflect weak tests or semantic equivalence. We propose Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE), a lightweight approach that identifies equivalent quantum mutants by transpiling original and mutated circuits under the same configuration and comparing their resulting OpenQASM code. We evaluate TBE on 348,299 surviving mutants, 92,011 of which are equivalent; TBE identifies 29,536 of them (32.1%) as equivalent while achieving 100% precision and 82% accuracy.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27258)
*Daniel Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文批判了面向对象编程的核心原则（将系统功能按问题域中的个体进行划分），指出该原则导致了功能碎片化和混淆，并提出应放弃面向对象，采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向对象编程的核心原则（按问题域个体划分功能）已主导软件工程五十余年，但其导致了功能碎片化和混淆等根本性问题，后续改进方案未能解决这些由该原则本身引发的必然后果，因此需要从根本上重新审视。

Method: 通过理论分析，指出面向对象的分区原则既不自然也不直接，揭示了后续方法学试图解决的功能碎片化和混淆问题实际上是该原则的必然结果，并提出放弃面向对象、采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方案。

Result: 识别出面向对象的核心原则是导致软件工程中功能碎片化和混淆问题的根本原因，后续改进方法未能从根本上解决这些问题。

Conclusion: 解决功能碎片化和混淆问题的更简单路径是放弃面向对象，采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法。

Abstract: A core principle of object orientation -- that the functionality of a system can be partitioned amongst objects that correspond to individuals in the problem domain -- has influenced how software has been specified, designed and implemented for more than fifty years. Later developments in software engineering sought to build on this principle. But in fact this partitioning is neither natural nor straightforward, and the problems that these later developments sought to mitigate -- the fragmentation and conflation of functionality -- were often, in fact, the inevitable consequences of this founding principle. An easier path to addressing these problems therefore starts by going back, abandoning object orientation, and replacing it with an alternative approach that decouples the individuals of the problem domain from the modules that partition functionality.

</details>


### [89] [Knowledge-Based Pull Requests: A Trusted Workflow for Agent-Mediated Knowledge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26721)
*Xinyu Zhang,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出知识驱动拉取请求（KPR）工作流，将外部协作者的代码、测试和交互轨迹视为知识源而非直接合并候选，通过项目内可信代理重新生成代码，从而分离"知识是否应进入项目"与"特定实现是否应合并"两个决策。通过7个合并PR的仿真实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理正在改变软件协作瓶颈：代码日益廉价，但理解意图、协商范围和治理长期项目责任仍然成本高昂。传统PR将两个关键决策（知识是否应进入项目、特定实现是否应合并）混为一谈，导致跨信任边界（开源、企业、供应商、承包商、客户驱动）的协作效率低下。

Method: 1) KPR工作流：外部协作方提交本地代码、测试和清洗后的代理交互轨迹作为知识源；2) 代理将这些来源提炼为人工确认的知识包，并呈现为设计备忘录、风险检查表、测试计划或实施简报等审阅形式；3) 项目拥有的内部可信编码代理在接收项目环境中（含仓库上下文、工程规范、测试和安全策略）重新生成候选代码；4) 提出候选工件模式、成本核算视图、协作网关架构。

Result: 在7个已合并公开PR上的受控仿真实验表明：KPR包可以从真实PR材料中实例化，并在描述消融、差异消融和合成投毒补丁条件下经受压力测试。

Conclusion: KPR是一个可经验检验的工作流，其价值取决于可审计的提取、转换和项目侧重新生成是否能降低理解和重构高上下文外部变更的成本。论文将KPR定位为需要进一步实验验证的工作流。

Abstract: AI coding agents are changing the bottleneck in software collaboration: code is increasingly cheap, while understanding intent, negotiating scope, and governing long-term project responsibility remain costly. This paper proposes \emph{Knowledge-Based Pull Requests} (KPR), a trusted workflow for agent-mediated software collaboration across trust boundaries, including open source, enterprise, vendor, contractor, and customer-driven settings. In KPR, an external collaborator's local code, tests, and cleaned agent interaction trace are treated as knowledge sources rather than as the default merge candidate. Agents distill these sources into a human-confirmed knowledge package and render it into reviewer-facing forms such as design memos, risk checklists, test plans, or implementation briefs. A project-owned inner trusted coding agent then regenerates candidate code inside the receiving project's environment under repository context, engineering conventions, tests, and security policy. KPR therefore separates two decisions that traditional pull requests often collapse: whether the knowledge should enter the project, and whether a particular implementation should be merged. We contribute the KPR workflow, a candidate artifact schema, a cost-accounting view, a collaboration gateway architecture, a minimal controlled simulation pilot over seven merged public pull requests, and an evaluation agenda. The pilot shows that KPR packages can be instantiated from real PR material and stress-tested under description ablation, diff ablation, and synthetic poisoned-patch conditions. We position KPR as an empirically testable workflow: its value depends on whether auditable extraction, transformation, and project-side regeneration reduce the cost of understanding and reworking high-context external changes.

</details>


### [90] [A Deterministic Control Plane for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26924)
*Padmaraj Madatha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文发现AI代理配置文件在GitHub上大量重复传播（10.1%路径SHA-256完全相同）、很少被修改（58%仅一次提交）、几乎不声明权限边界（<1%），并提出一个确定性控制平面Rel(AI)Build来管理这一层：内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、分层权限、阶段状态机、跨IDE编译、Jaccard相似度检测提示漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码工具（LLM coding harnesses）赋予代理广泛的文件与shell访问权限，但指导这些代理行为的配置层（规则文件、代理定义、IDE特定的markdown）缺乏管理，存在安全隐患。

Method: 1) 对10,008个GitHub仓库进行大规模流行度研究（n=6,145个代理配置文件）；2) 提出Rel(AI)Build确定性控制平面，包括SHA-256内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、分层权限、攻击衍生黑名单、阶段状态机、跨7个IDE编译、Jaccard相似度检测提示漂移。

Result: 配置传播：10.1%的跟踪路径在独立仓库间SHA-256完全重复（fork调整、阈值无关），75.5%的克隆对跨组织边界；配置很少修订：58%单次提交，年龄标准化后0.4 vs 0.6次提交/月（对比CI/CD工作流）；权限声明极少：<1%代理配置 vs 33% Actions工作流（n=31真阳性）。合规测试验证各机制强制执行其声称的不变条件。

Conclusion: AI代理配置层需要确定性的、工具无关的治理，不应委托给进一步的LLM编排。开发者成果留待未来工作。

Abstract: LLM coding harnesses grant agents broad file and shell access, yet the configuration layer that steers them -- rules files, agent definitions, IDE-specific markdown -- is largely unmanaged. A prevalence study of 10,008 public GitHub repositories (n=6,145 agent config files) finds that agent configurations propagate as undeclared shared components: 10.1% of tracked paths are SHA-256 exact duplicates across independent repositories (fork-adjusted, threshold-independent), with 75.5% of clone pairs crossing organisational boundaries. Two further patterns are indicative: configurations are rarely revised (58% single-commit; 0.4 vs 0.6 commits/month age-normalised against CI/CD workflows), and rarely declare permission boundaries (<1% of agent configs vs 33% of Actions workflows, n=31 true positives).
  We propose a deterministic control plane above the harness that maps one-to-one to these gaps. Rel(AI)Build treats agent definitions as a managed supply chain (SHA-256 content addressing, HMAC-stamped lockfiles, hash-chained audit logs); enforces tiered permissions and attack-derived blocklists before LLM invocation; gates feature work through a phase state machine with requirement-to-file-to-test traceability; compiles a single canonical definition to seven IDE targets; and detects prompt drift via Jaccard similarity. Conformance tests on injected violations confirm each mechanism enforces its stated invariant; developer outcomes remain future work. Governance of this layer must be deterministic and tool-agnostic -- not delegated to further LLM orchestration.

</details>


### [91] [Are LLMs Ready for Anti-Pattern Detection in Microservice Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26927)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Amalfitano,Porfirio Tramontana,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）通过基于提示的分析流程检测微服务架构中反模式的能力，并与静态分析工具MARS进行对比。结果表明LLMs在多种反模式检测上表现有竞争力，尤其擅长局部、异构或语义丰富的证据场景，但在需要显式结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上仍不如静态分析工具，因此LLMs可作为传统分析器的补充而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统中频繁出现架构反模式，影响可维护性、可演化性和运营质量。现有检测方法依赖静态分析和手工规则，存在工具依赖性强、检测逻辑固化、难以适应异构代码库等问题，亟需探索更灵活通用的检测方案。

Method: 采用基于提示的分析流程，利用LLMs对静态仓库工件进行分析，在包含16种架构反模式的微服务基准数据集上评估了三款通用LLMs，并与最新静态分析工具MARS在精确率和召回率上进行了统一对比。

Result: LLMs在多种反模式检测上达到有竞争力的性能，尤其在证据局部化、异构或语义丰富的反模式上表现突出；但在需要显式结构信息或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上，LLMs存在明显局限，静态分析工具仍更可靠。

Conclusion: LLMs目前尚不能替代传统静态分析工具，但已展现出作为架构评估中辅助手段的潜力，可作为传统分析器的有益补充。

Abstract: Microservice systems are prone to recurrent architectural anti-patterns (APs) that hinder maintainability, evolvability, and operational quality. Most existing AP detection approaches rely on static analysis and handcrafted rules, which can be effective but are often tool-dependent, limited to explicitly encoded detection logic, and difficult to adapt to heterogeneous repositories. In this paper, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) are ready to support architectural anti-pattern detection in microservice architectures through a prompt-based analysis pipeline over static repository artifacts. We evaluate three general-purpose LLMs on a curated benchmark of microservice repositories annotated with 16 architectural anti-patterns, and compare their performance against the state-of-the-art static-analysis tool MARS using a uniform evaluation protocol based on precision and recall. Our results show that LLMs can provide useful support for anti-pattern detection, achieving competitive performance on several anti-patterns, especially when the relevant evidence is local, heterogeneous, or semantically rich. At the same time, they exhibit clear limitations on anti-patterns that require explicit structural or cross-service dependency evidence, where static analysis remains more reliable. These findings suggest that LLMs are not yet a replacement for traditional analyzers, but already represent a promising complementary aid for architectural assessment in microservice systems.

</details>


### [92] [CLIR: Liveness-Driven and Structure-Aware Fuzzing for the Cranelift Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26977)
*Shangtong Cao,Tianlei Song,Qiuping Yi,Tianyu Chen,Guoai Xu,Ningyu He,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CLIR是一个针对Cranelift编译器后端的差分测试框架，通过语法保持的分层生成策略、活跃度引导的指令精炼机制和诊断引导的跨架构适配方案，在72小时内检测到24个跨所有目标架构的bug，其中21个已确认、9个已修复，检测bug数量比现有方法提升8-24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cranelift作为新兴的高性能编译器后端（支持x86-64、AArch64、s390x、RISCV64），其测试面临三大独特挑战：（1）在SSA形式严格约束下构造有效IR；（2）生成足够计算密度的指令序列以压测后端组件；（3）在异构架构间实现广泛的测试覆盖与高效的根因分析平衡。

Method: 提出CLIR差分测试框架，包含三个核心机制：（1）语法保持的分层生成策略，保证SSA有效性；（2）活跃度引导的指令精炼机制，最大化计算密度；（3）诊断引导的跨架构适配方案，实现异构后端的高效根因分析。

Result: CLIR在检测独特bug数量上分别比cranelift-fuzzgen、wasm-smith和WASMaker高出8倍、24倍和8倍（RustSmith未发现bug）。72小时内检测到24个跨所有目标架构的bug，21个已确认，9个已修复。

Conclusion: CLIR框架有效解决了Cranelift编译器后端测试中的SSA有效性、计算密度和跨架构根因分析三大挑战，显著优于现有测试方法，证明了其在实际编译器测试中的高效性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern compilers are complex software systems that must correctly translate high-level programming languages into machine code across multiple architectures. Cranelift, a fast and modern compiler backend originally developed for WebAssembly and recently adopted as an experimental backend for Rust, has gained increasing importance due to its superior compilation speed compared to LLVM and comprehensive multi-architecture support, including x86-64, AArch64, s390x, and RISCV64. However, despite decades of development in compiler testing, testing Cranelift still presents unique challenges, including (1) constructing valid IR under the strict enforcement of SSA form, (2) generating sequences with sufficient computational density to stress backend components, and (3) balancing broad backend coverage with efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous architectures.
  To address these challenges, we propose CLIR, a differential testing framework that integrates a syntax-preserving hierarchical generation strategy to guarantee SSA validity, a liveness-guided instruction refinement mechanism to maximize computational density, and a diagnosis-guided cross-architecture adaptation scheme to facilitate efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous backends. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that CLIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, detecting 8x, 24x, and 8x more unique bugs than cranelift-fuzzgen, wasm-smith, and WASMaker, respectively, while RustSmith uncovered no bugs. Consequently, within 72 hours of testing, CLIR discovered 24 bugs spanning all target architectures, with 21 confirmed and 9 fixed.

</details>


### [93] [To Run or Not to Run: Analyzing the Cost-Effectiveness of Code Execution in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26978)
*Zhihao Lin,Junhua Zhu,Mingyi Zhou,Xin Wang,Zhensu Sun,Renyu Yang,David Lo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（分析7,745条智能体轨迹和3,000次修复尝试）发现：代码执行在LLM程序修复智能体中被广泛使用（平均每任务8.8次测试运行），但限制执行对修复成功率影响极小（商业智能体仅下降1.25个百分点，统计上不显著），却能大幅节省成本。执行收益呈集中分布而非均匀分布，表明当前智能体不加区分地使用执行，应将其视为具有明确成本效益权衡的资源而非默认能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于"生成-运行-修订"范式的执行驱动方法已成为LLM程序修复智能体的标准实践，但代码执行对这些智能体的实际影响尚未被充分探索，尤其执行的高时间成本和计算成本是否值得尚未明确。

Method: 两阶段实证研究：第一阶段分析SWE-bench排行榜上7,745条智能体执行轨迹以刻画执行行为规模特征；第二阶段在200个SWE-bench实例、3个智能体（Claude Code、Codex和开源OpenCode）和4种执行范式下评估3,000次端到端修复尝试，细粒度比较性能和成本。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 所有智能体和模型均使用执行，平均每任务8.8次测试运行，行为差异大（2-19次/任务），后期执行成功率高于早期；(2) 限制执行对修复成功率影响极小，禁止执行与无限制之间的成功率差距仅1.25个百分点且统计不显著，但大幅节省token和耗时；(3) 执行收益集中而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 当前智能体不加区分地使用执行，在收益甚微的实例上也付出了成本。执行应被视为具有明确成本效益权衡的资源，而非默认启用的功能。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for program repair are increasingly built on a "generate-run-revise" paradigm, iteratively executing tests to evaluate and refine patches. This execution-based approach has become standard practice in state-of-the-art systems. However, executions can be time-consuming and expensive, yet their impact on these agents remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a two-stage empirical study over execution behavior in LLM-based program repair. To characterize execution behavior at scale, we first analyze 7,745 agent traces from SWE-bench leaderboard submissions. Second, we evaluate 3,000 end-to-end repair attempts across 200 SWE-bench instances and three agents (Claude Code, Codex, and the open-source OpenCode) under four execution paradigms, which allows for a fine-grained comparison of performance and cost. Our analysis reveals three key observations: (1) Code execution is used across all agents and models analyzed, with an average of 8.8 test runs per task. Execution behavior varies substantially across agents and models, with frequency ranging from 2 to 19 per task, and late-stage executions consistently achieve higher success rates than early-stage ones. (2) Execution restrictions have little effect on repair success: on commercial agents with SOTA models the resolve-rate gap between Prohibited and Unrestricted is only 1.25 percentage points and not statistically significant, while Prohibited saves substantial token and wall-clock cost. (3) Execution benefit is concentrated rather than uniform. These patterns suggest that current agents apply execution indiscriminately, paying its cost on instances where it provides little benefit. Execution, therefore, should be treated as a resource with an explicit cost-benefit tradeoff, not a default capability.

</details>


### [94] [How Much Static Structure Do Code Agents Need? A Study of Deterministic Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26979)
*Zhihao Lin,Mingyi Zhou,Yizhuo Yang,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻量级静态分析（如调用图、继承层次等结构关系）作为确定性锚点注入LLM代码智能体的效果，发现结构注释能够约束概率探索、提高导航可重复性，并提出了"确定性锚定效应"——静态结构并非让智能体更"聪明"，而是使其导航更规范、更可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的代码智能体依赖关键词搜索来导航代码仓库，但忽略了调用图、继承层次、配置依赖等结构关系，导致导航具有随机性且难以跨运行复现。本文旨在探究轻量级静态分析能否为这些智能体提供确定性锚点，使导航更可预测。

Method: 以OpenAI的Codex为强基线，系统地注入不同粒度的结构注释（调用/继承拓扑等），测量其对定位精度、轨迹行为和运行间稳定性的影响。在中等规模仓库上进行实验，分析锚定效应的三个维度：有效性、规模敏感性和稳定性。

Result: (1) 锚定有效：轻量级调用/继承拓扑使函数级定位提升2.2个百分点（Func@5），轨迹缩短1.6个交互轮次；(2) 锚定具有规模敏感性：最优粒度和方向性取决于仓库特征，稠密语义呈现收益递减，Hub密集项目受益于仅反向链接；(3) 锚定提升稳定性：链接跟随率从0.15-0.18提升至0.21-0.24，运行间方差减半，单次运行可靠性提升3.4个百分点（Pass@1），代价是约10%的额外输入token。

Conclusion: 建议实践中默认在中等项目使用轻量级拓扑，大型仓库裁剪前向边，隐式依赖场景保留稠密标签。

Abstract: LLM-based code agents navigate repositories through keyword search but miss the structural relationships, such as call graphs, inheritance hierarchies, and configuration dependencies, that define how software actually works. This makes agent navigation stochastic and difficult to reproduce across runs. We investigate whether lightweight static analysis can provide deterministic anchors for these agents: stable structural facts injected as plain-text comments that constrain probabilistic exploration and make navigation more predictable. Starting from a strong baseline, Codex from OpenAI, we systematically inject varying granularities of structural annotations and measure their effects on localization, trajectory behavior, and run-to-run stability. Our study identifies what we call the deterministic anchoring effect: static structure helps less by making agents "smarter" and more by making their navigation disciplined and reproducible. Three observations support this finding: (1) Anchoring works: lightweight call/inheritance topology improves function-level localization (+2.2pp Func@5) and shortens trajectories (-1.6 interaction rounds); (2) Anchoring is scale-sensitive: the optimal granularity and directionality depend on repository characteristics, where denser semantics show diminishing returns and hub-heavy projects benefit from inverse-only links that expose "who-calls-me" without forward edges; (3) Anchoring stabilizes: tags raise link-following rate from 0.15-0.18 to 0.21-0.24, roughly halve run-to-run variance, and improve single-run reliability (Pass@1 +3.4 pp) on medium-scale repositories, at the cost of roughly 10% more input tokens. These observations suggest practical guidelines: default to lightweight topology on medium projects, prune forward edges in large repositories, and reserve dense tags for implicit-dependency cases.

</details>


### [95] [Cleaning Logs for Downstream Tasks (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27000)
*Zahra G. Yazdi,Van-Hoang Le,Nyyti Saarimäki,Donghwan Shin,Domenico Bianculli,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志模板依赖关系的任务无关日志清洗方法，旨在识别并移除与系统执行行为无关的游离消息，以提高下游分析任务（模型推断和异常检测）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统日志中包含大量冗余和无关信息（游离消息），这些信息会负面影响下游分析任务（如模型推断和异常检测）的性能，因此需要有效的日志清洗方法。

Method: 提出LogPurifier方法，基于日志消息模板之间的依赖关系，实现任务无关的日志清洗，识别并移除与系统功能行为无关的游离消息。

Result: 计划通过受控实验设计，评估LogPurifier对两个下游任务（模型推断和异常检测）在有效性和效率方面的影响。

Conclusion: LogPurifier作为一种任务无关的日志清洗方法，有望通过移除游离消息来提升下游分析任务的性能，实验评估将验证其效果。

Abstract: Background: Software systems generate logs during execution to record critical events and runtime information for troubleshooting and monitoring. However, in practice, logs often contain significant amounts of redundant and irrelevant information, which can negatively impact the performance of downstream analysis tasks, such as model inference and anomaly detection. Objective: The objective of this study is to clean log data by identifying and removing free-standing messages -- messages that are not relevant to the execution behaviors of interest and are interleaved with messages capturing the system's functional behavior. Method: To address this objective, we propose LogPurifier, a task-agnostic log-cleaning approach based on dependency relationships between log message templates. The paper presents a plan for an empirical evaluation using a controlled experimental design to assess the impact of LogPurifier on the effectiveness and efficiency of two downstream tasks: model inference and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [96] [The Spec Growth Engine: Spec-Anchored, Code-Coupled, Drift-Enforced Architecture for AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27045)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Spec Growth Engine框架，通过机器可读的规范图、Spine上下文组装器、垂直切片增长协议和漂移门机制，解决AI编码代理中的上下文爆炸和规范-代码静默漂移两大结构性故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理面临两个现有规范驱动方法未能充分解决的结构性故障：(1)上下文爆炸——代理需同时推理整个仓库，上下文窗口填满后输出质量下降；(2)规范-代码静默漂移——代码演化而规范未更新，差异在修复成本高昂前不可见。

Method: 提出Spec Growth Engine框架，包含：(1)机器可读的规范图，节点携带显式的契约/设计分离；(2)Spine上下文组装器，将代理上下文限定到所有权路径；(3)垂直切片增长协议，强制最难优先排序；(4)漂移门，将规范-代码差异作为阻塞性合并条件。

Result: 该设计综合了Parnas信息隐藏、C4、ADR、Walking Skeleton、Reflexion Models、Fitness Functions等成熟的软件工程原则，形成轻量级、代码耦合、机器强制的整体方案，无需RUP或MDA等重量级框架的开销。

Conclusion: Spec Growth Engine通过合成现有软件工程原则，以轻量级方式解决了AI驱动软件开发中上下文爆炸和规范-代码漂移两大核心问题，实现了代码耦合的机器强制规范管理。

Abstract: AI coding agents dramatically accelerate implementation speed but introduce two structural failure modes that existing spec-driven approaches do not fully solve: (1) context explosion -- the agent must reason over an entire repository at once, degrading output quality as the context window fills; and (2) silent spec-code drift -- code evolves, the specification does not, and the divergence becomes invisible until it is costly to repair.
  We present the Spec Growth Engine, a lightweight framework that addresses both failure modes through a machine-readable spec graph whose nodes carry explicit contract/design separation, a Spine context assembler that scopes agent context to an ownership path, a vertical-slice growth protocol that enforces hardest-first ordering, and a drift gate that makes spec-code divergence a blocking merge condition.
  The design synthesises well-established software engineering principles (Parnas information hiding, C4, ADRs, Walking Skeleton, Reflexion Models, Fitness Functions) into a lean, code-coupled, machine-enforced whole -- without the overhead of heavy-weight frameworks such as RUP or MDA.

</details>


### [97] [ATGBuilder: Feature-Assisted Graph Learning for Activity Transition Graph Construction with Seed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27080)
*Chenhui Cui,Zixiang Xian,Danyu Li,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ATGBuilder，一种基于特征辅助图学习的方法，用于种子监督下的活动转换图（ATG）构建。该方法利用大语言模型（LLM）将UI布局信息总结为功能摘要，并将widget触发信息显式建模为边属性，通过辅助重构目标进行训练。实验表明，ATGBuilder在多个基准测试上显著优于现有方法，并能有效提升自动化GUI探索工具的导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATG构建方法存在局限性：静态分析可能遗漏可行转换或提取不可行转换，动态探索可能生成不完整的转换图。虽然已有基于学习的方法将ATG构建视为种子监督的链接预测任务，但对activity-layout和widget-trigger信息的利用仍不充分。

Method: ATGBuilder包含三个关键设计：(1) 使用LLM将UI布局元数据总结为紧凑的文本功能摘要；(2) 将widget触发信息显式建模为边属性；(3) 在模型训练中引入辅助的widget属性重构目标。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ATGBuilder显著优于现有最先进方法。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。此外，通过提供更好的导航引导，ATGBuilder能够有效改进自动化GUI探索工具。

Conclusion: ATGBuilder通过融合LLM生成的布局功能摘要和显式的widget触发信息建模，在种子监督的ATG构建任务上取得了显著性能提升，并能实际应用于增强GUI自动化探索工具。

Abstract: Android applications are organized around activities that provide visual Graphical User Interface (GUI) containers that host the UI and handle user interaction events. Activity Transition Graphs (ATGs) have been widely used to model apps' GUI navigation. However, the construction of high-quality ATGs is challenging: ATGs based on static analysis may miss acceptable transitions and may extract infeasible ones; while dynamically explored ATGs can yield incomplete transitions. Recent learning-based approaches can treat ATG construction as a seed-supervised link-prediction task. However, the use of activity-layout and widget-trigger information for ATG construction remains limited. We propose ATGBuilder, a feature-assisted graph-learning approach for seed-supervised ATG construction. ATGBuilder uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to summarize UI activity metadata from layouts into compact textual functionality summaries. ATGBuilder explicitly models widget-trigger information into the edge attribute: It then uses an auxiliary widget-attribute reconstruction objective on this information during model training. ATGBuilder's performance was evaluated across a series of ablations on the frontmatter corpus, and an experiment on benchmark using manually-checked ground-truth ATGs. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ATGBuilder significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We further demonstrate its effectiveness by improving automated GUI exploration tools through better navigation guidance.

</details>


### [98] [On the Reproducibility of Quantum Software Defect Datasets: A Case Study of Bugs4Q](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27124)
*Haruto Ohto,Yuta Ishimoto,Shinsuke Matsumoto,Shinji Kusumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究复现了Zhu等人关于软件缺陷数据集可复现性的工作，将其扩展到量子软件领域。通过对Bugs4Q数据集的77,700次量子程序执行分析发现，其可复现性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%降至v2.3.1的16.2%，93.6%的失败源于依赖问题。与先前工作不同的是，多数故障仅靠调整依赖版本无法解决，需修改源代码。研究还构建了Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本，将可复现性恢复至78.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明经典软件缺陷数据集（如Defects4J）随时间推移存在可复现性问题，但尚不清楚该结论是否能推广到量子软件缺陷数据集。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对Zhu等人先前工作的复现研究，使用Bugs4Q量子程序缺陷数据集，在Qiskit多个版本上执行77,700次量子程序运行，分析可复现性变化并进行根因人工检查。

Result: Bugs4Q可复现性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%下降至v2.3.1的16.2%；93.6%的故障为依赖相关；与经典软件不同，多数故障需修改源代码而非仅调整依赖版本；构建的Bugs4Q-Robust将可复现性恢复至78.4%。

Conclusion: 量子软件缺陷数据集同样面临随时间推移可复现性衰退的问题，且在快速演进的量子软件生态中，依赖问题更深层，需要持续的维护和源代码级别的修复。

Abstract: The reproducibility of software defect datasets is essential for obtaining reliable and comparable research results. Zhu et al. have shown that defect datasets such as Defects4J suffer from reproduction failures (i.e., reported bugs become non-reproducible) as time passes since their creation. However, it remains unclear whether these findings generalize to quantum software defect datasets. We therefore conduct a replication study of the prior work using Bugs4Q, a widely used dataset of real-world bugs in quantum programs. Our analysis includes 77,700 quantum program executions of 37 Bugs4Q artifacts across 21 core-library versions. The experimental results showed that the reproducibility of Bugs4Q dropped from 62.2% on Qiskit v0.20.1 to 16.2% on v2.3.1, the latest version as of April 1, 2026. A manual inspection of the root causes further indicated that 93.6% of the failures were dependency-related. While these findings are consistent with those of the prior work, we also observed differences. In particular, most reproduction failures in Bugs4Q cannot be resolved merely by adjusting dependency versions; instead, they require source-code modifications such as migrating import paths and API invocations. Based on this observation, we curated Bugs4Q-Robust, a patched version of Bugs4Q to restore reproducibility. Bugs4Q-Robust increases reproducibility from 16.2% to 78.4% on Qiskit v2.3.1. Our findings highlight the importance of continuous dataset maintenance in the rapidly evolving quantum software ecosystem.

</details>


### [99] [Smaller Models, Unexpected Costs: Trade-offs in LLM Quantization for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27205)
*Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz,Giordano d'Aloisio,Max Hort,Luca Traini,Antinisca Di Marco,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM量化在自动化程序修复（APR）任务中的影响，分析了13种量化配置在6个代表性LLM上的表现。结果表明：量化模型与原模型修复的问题集合重叠度低但修复数量相当；量化虽能减少高达85%的内存占用，但会增加推理时间和能耗；Pareto分析显示48%的配置被严格支配；模型架构和任务复杂度对效果-内存-能效之间的权衡有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化技术虽能有效减少LLM的内存占用，但现有研究仅用基准分数概括其整体影响，掩盖了模型行为变化和非功能性开销。因此需要在一个复杂软件工程任务（APR）上进行更全面的实证评估。

Method: 选取6个代表性LLM，在2个APR基准（HumanEval-Java和Defects4J）上评估13种量化配置（不同位宽、方法、目标组件（权重和KV缓存）），进行全面的实证对比分析。

Result: 1) 基模型与量化模型修复的问题集合不同，重叠度低但修复数量可比；2) 量化最多减少85%内存占用，但推理时间和能耗增加；3) Pareto分析表明48%的量化配置被严格支配；4) 不存在统一的更优量化方法，模型架构和任务复杂度决定权衡关系。

Conclusion: 量化策略的选择需综合考虑模型架构和任务特性，在修复效果、内存占用和能效之间进行权衡，不存在普适的最优量化配置。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) are powerful toolsand have been increasingly adopted for complex software engineering tasks. As the number of parameters increases, results can often be improved, but this also imposes substantialmemory requirements. While quantization effectively reduces thememory footprint, its overall impact is often summarized onlyby benchmark scores, which mask changes in model behaviorand non-functional overheads. In this work, we conduct anempirical evaluation of LLM quantization using AutomatedProgram Repair (APR), a complex task in software engineering.We analyze 13 quantization configurations spanning differentbit-widths, methods, and target components (weights and KVcache) across six representative LLMs, evaluated on two APRbenchmarks (HumanEval-Java and Defects4J).
  Our findings reveal that base and quantized models can provide different sets of repaired problems with little overlap, whileretaining a comparable number of repaired problems. Althoughquantization successfully reduces memory footprints by up to85%, it increases both inference time and energy consumption,which we attribute to suboptimal hardware utilization. OurPareto trade-off analysis shows that 48% of the configurationsevaluated are strictly dominated by alternatives. Rather thanidentifying a superior quantization method, our findings highlightthat the trade-offs between effectiveness, memory footprint,and energy efficiency are sensitive to the underlying modelarchitecture and the complexity of the task.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Axon: A Synthesizing Superoptimizer for Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26344)
*Akash Kothari,Shaowei Zhu,Daniel Kroening,Chungha Sung*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Axon是一个面向AI加速器的张量程序合成超优化器，通过程序合成自动生成目标指令、探索语义等价的程序变体以选择最佳性能内核，支持代数变换、SMT语义验证、指令降级、分块配置探索和算子融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为AI加速器编写高性能内核需要在分块、指令选择、数据布局和算子融合方面具备深厚专业知识，给程序员带来巨大负担，因此需要自动化方法来生成和优化张量程序。

Method: 提出Axon框架：(1) 使用程序合成从语义规范自动生成目标指令；(2) 通过计算图传播算子发现代数变换，并用SMT在无界张量上保证语义等价性，无需手工编写重写规则；(3) 将张量操作降级到目标ISA指令，结合硬件描述探索分块配置；(4) 融合算子和指令以最小化内存流量。

Result: 能够自动生成和优化张量程序内核，通过语义等价变换和分块配置探索来选择性能最佳的实现，同时降低内存访问开销。

Conclusion: Axon通过程序合成和形式化验证相结合的方式，自动化了AI加速器高性能内核的开发过程，减轻了程序员在分块、指令选择、数据布局和算子融合等方面的负担。

Abstract: Writing high performance kernels for AI accelerators requires deep expertise in tiling, instruction selection, data layout, and operator fusion placing a significant burden on programmers. In this paper, we focus on tile based AI accelerator programs and present Axon, a synthesizing superoptimizer for tensor programs: it uses program synthesis to automatically generate target instructions from semantics specifications, and explores semantically equivalent program variants to select the best performing kernel empirically. Axon discovers algebraic transformations by propagating operators through computation graphs and uses SMT over unbounded tensors to guarantee that all transformations preserve semantics without requiring hand crafted rewrite rules. It then lowers tensor operations to target ISA instructions, explores tiling configurations constrained by hardware descriptions, and fuses operators and instructions to minimize memory traffic.

</details>


### [101] [A Typestate Approach to Purpose-aware Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26386)
*Joan Montas,Samuel Dodson,Anitha Gollamudi,Matteo Cimini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出PurPL语言，利用类型状态（typestate）机制在编译时验证敏感数据使用是否符合其目的合规性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用需要验证敏感数据是否按预期目的使用，但现有文献在编译时保证方面成果有限，缺乏有效的编译时保障机制。

Method: 开发了面向对象语言PurPL，其核心是类型状态系统：数据类型的状态被定义为数据可用目的的集合，该集合可在运行时动态增减（添加或移除目的），形成自动机风格的状态转换。实现了类型检查器并进行实验评估。

Result: 通过示例展示了PurPL的概览，给出了形式化类型系统，并实现了类型检查器，在多种处理敏感数据的编程场景上进行了类型检查实验。

Conclusion: PurPL通过类型状态机制为敏感数据的目的合规性提供了编译时保障，填补了该领域在静态验证方面的空白。

Abstract: Real-world applications often require verification that sensitive data is being used for their intended purpose. However, existing literature offers limited results regarding compile-time guarantees in this domain. In this paper, we explore the use of typestate to reason about the purpose of data. In typestate, types have a state, which can transition to other states in the style of automata. In our approach, the state of the type of sensitive data is defined as the set of purposes for which the data can be used. This set can grow or shrink at runtime as purposes can be added or removed during execution.
  In this paper, we have developed PurPL, an object-oriented programming language that features a typestate system that is capable of reasoning about purposes and data usage compliance according to them.
  We give an overview of PurPL through examples and present a formal type system. We have also implemented PurPL's type checker, and we report on our experiments with type checking various programming scenarios that handle sensitive data.

</details>


### [102] [Compiler-Driven Approximation Tuning for Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26547)
*Xavier Routh,Abdul Rafae Noor,Akash Kothari,Zheyu Li,Mahbod Afarin,Tajana Rosing,Vikram Adve*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出ApproxHDC框架，用于在超维计算(HDC)工作负载中自动识别和应用领域特定的近似优化，通过扩展HPVM-HDC编译器基础设施，支持CPU、GPU以及模拟ReRAM和PCM加速器等多种硬件后端，并利用高效搜索分析在指数级近似空间中识别软硬件层面的高影响配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律的物理和经济极限逼近，需要领域特定的方法来加速机器学习工作负载。超维计算(HDC)作为一种新兴范式，以硬件效率为首要目标设计，具有噪声和近似容忍特性，但如何系统化地识别和应用近似优化以充分利用其潜力仍是一个挑战。

Method: ApproxHDC框架通过扩展HPVM-HDC编译器基础设施，实现对CPU、GPU及模拟ReRAM和PCM加速器的可重定向编译；采用高效搜索和分析方法，在指数级规模的近似配置空间中识别高影响的软硬件协同近似方案。

Result: ApproxHDC能够在保持最小精度损失的前提下实现显著的性能提升，有效利用HDC算法固有的噪声和近似容忍特性，在异构硬件平台上自动发现最优近似配置组合。

Conclusion: ApproxHDC展示了通过自动化领域特定近似优化，可以充分发挥超维计算在异构硬件平台上的效率和性能潜力，为后摩尔时代机器学习加速提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: As Moore's law reaches its physical and economic limits, domain-specific approaches are increasingly employed to accelerate machine learning workloads. Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents one such emerging paradigm, offering an alternative to conventional deep learning techniques. Rooted in cognitive models of computation, HDC is designed bottom-up with hardware efficiency as a first-class objective. HDC workloads map naturally to heterogeneous hardware platforms, including CPUs, GPUs, and FPGAs, as well as emerging in-memory computing technologies such as Resistive RAM (ReRAM) and Phase-Change Memory (PCM). HDC algorithms are intrinsically tolerant to noise and approximation, enabling substantial performance gains with minimal accuracy loss. In this work, we introduce ApproxHDC, a framework for automated identification and application of domain-specific approximations in HDC workloads. ApproxHDC extends the HPVM-HDC compiler infrastructure to enable retargetable compilation across diverse hardware backends, including CPUs, GPUs, and simulated ReRAM and PCM-based accelerators. The space of possible approximations is exponentially large; ApproxHDC employs efficient search and analysis to navigate it and identify high-impact configurations spanning both software and hardware levels.

</details>


### [103] [Mostly Automatic Translation of Language Interpreters from C to Safe Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27122)
*Bo Wang,Brandon Paulsen,Joey Dodds,Daniel Kroening,Umang Mathur,Prateek Saxena*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Reboot是一种将C语言解释器程序大部分自动翻译为安全Rust代码的技术，通过特征分解和多智能体架构，成功翻译了6个解释器（6k-23k行C代码），仅需1-11次人工干预，消除内存漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将C程序翻译为安全Rust面临类型约束、所有权和借用规则等显著差异的挑战。解释器程序尤其重要，因为它们常处理不可信输入并遭受内存相关漏洞。现有翻译方法在处理大型真实世界程序时效果有限。

Method: Reboot采用两种核心思路：1) 特征分解：按程序特征将翻译分解为一系列里程碑，每个里程碑都是完整可测试的程序，从最简版本开始逐步恢复特征，每个里程碑经验证后再继续；2) 多智能体架构：通过自动化验证和反馈协调不可靠的编码智能体，使长流程翻译工作保持正轨，最小化人工干预。

Result: 翻译了6个解释器（6k-23k行C代码），每个仅需1-11次人工干预。所有翻译100%通过提供的测试套件，在从未暴露的验证测试上达到62%-92%通过率。安全案例研究显示，mujs中的堆缓冲区溢出和释放后使用等漏洞在安全Rust翻译中被消除。消融实验表明特征分解使验证测试通过率提升6%-20%。

Conclusion: Reboot证明了通过特征分解和多智能体架构相结合，可以将真实世界解释器程序从C自动翻译为安全Rust，消除内存漏洞，且翻译质量高、人工干预少。

Abstract: Translating C programs to safe Rust is challenging owing to significant differences in typing constraints, ownership, and borrowing rules. Interpreter programs are particularly important targets for such translation, as they often handle untrusted inputs and suffer from memory-related vulnerabilities. We present Reboot, a mostly-automatic technique that translates real-world interpreter programs from C to safe Rust. Using Reboot, we have translated six interpreters ranging from 6k to 23k lines of C code to safe Rust, with each translation requiring only 1 to 11 brief user interventions. All translations pass 100% of the provided test suites, and achieve 62%--92% pass rates on separately created validation tests that were never exposed to the system. A security case study on mujs shows that memory vulnerabilities such as heap buffer overflows and use-after-free present in C are eliminated in the safe Rust translation. Two ideas underpin Reboot. First, feature reduction decomposes the translation by program features, creating a sequence of milestones where each is a complete, testable program; the translation starts from the simplest version and incrementally restores features, with each milestone validated before proceeding. Second, a multi-agent architecture orchestrates inherently unreliable coding agents through automated validation and feedback, keeping long-running translation workflows on track with minimal human involvement. An ablation study confirms that feature reduction improves translation correctness compared to using multi-agent translation alone, with 6%--20% improvements in pass rates on validation test suites.

</details>


### [104] [Welterweight Go: Boxing, Structural Subtyping, and Generics (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27138)
*Raymond Hu,Julien Lange,Bernardo Toninho,Philip Wadler,Robert Griesemer,Keith Randall*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了WG（Welterweight Go）作为Go语言的核心模型，涵盖了底层类型、类型联合、类型集以及泛型方法等关键特性，并设计了LWG低级语言来建模Go的运行时机制（原始结构体值与携带RTTI的接口值的区分）。通过从WG到LWG的类型导向编译，展示了如何在不依赖静态单态化、保持分离编译兼容性且无需运行时代码生成的情况下，实现上述特性，其编译策略采用运行时类型转换和适配器方法来处理结构子类型、泛型与运行时基础设施之间的复杂交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化工作未能覆盖Go语言的关键特性，如底层类型、类型联合、类型集和泛型方法，且Go独特的结构子类型、泛型与非统一运行时表示之间的交互缺乏系统性的形式化建模与实现方案。

Method: 提出WG核心模型以捕获Go的关键语言特性，设计LWG低级语言建模运行时机制，并给出从WG到LWG的类型导向编译策略，采用运行时类型转换和适配器方法而非静态单态化。

Result: 所提出的编译策略能够实现泛型方法等特性，同时保持与分离编译的兼容性，且无需运行时代码生成。

Conclusion: 基于运行时类型转换和适配器方法的编译策略可以有效处理结构子类型、泛型与Go运行时基础设施之间的复杂交互，为Go语言的形式化建模和实现提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Go's unique combination of structural subtyping between generics and types with non-uniform runtime representations presents significant challenges for formalising the language. We introduce WG (Welterweight Go), a core model of Go that captures key features excluded by prior work, including underlying types, type unions and type sets, and proposed new features, such as generic methods. We also develop LWG, a lower-level language that models Go's runtime mechanisms, notably the distinction between raw struct values and interface values that carry runtime type information (RTTI). We give a type-directed compilation from WG to LWG that demonstrates how the proposed features can be implemented while observing important design and implementation goals for Go: compatibility with separate compilation, and no runtime code generation. Unlike existing approaches based on static monomorphisation, our compilation strategy uses runtime type conversions and adaptor methods to handle the complex interactions between structural subtyping, generics, and Go's runtime infrastructure.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为替代模型，用于学习由非线性偏微分方程（如Cahn-Hilliard方程）描述的微结构演化，在长时程预测中保持稳定准确，并符合Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解非线性偏微分方程计算成本高，而深度神经网络替代模型可作为高效替代方案。本文旨在开发一种能准确预测相分离微结构演化的替代模型。

Method: 提出基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络（physics-guided CNN with attention），以二元混合物的Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的相分离过程为研究对象，训练模型预测完整时间演化过程。

Result: 模型在临界和非临界混合物下均能保持长时程预测的稳定性和准确性，在演化过程中守恒混合物组成，准确捕捉畴尺寸增长，且与Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律一致。

Conclusion: 该框架对具有守恒动力学的系统建模有效，并可推广至其他复杂动力系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [106] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,William F. Shen,Daniel Burkhardt,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nurbek Tastan,Lorenzo Sani,Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi,Ambroise Odonnat,Zeyu Cao,Bill Marino,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出红皇后哥德尔机（RQGM），一种在非平稳效用下进行递归自我改进的进化框架，通过固定时期内的搜索与时期边界上的效用更新相结合，在编码、科学论文撰写/评审和奥赛级证明任务上超越先前SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体的搜索方法假设评估标准是平稳的（固定验证器、基准或标注数据集），忽略了物种与环境共同进化的核心特征。作者旨在将评估纳入改进循环，使搜索面向进化评估器、对抗目标和动态效用，超越静态基准。

Method: 提出RQGM框架，将自我改进组织为多个时期：每个时期内使用固定评估标准确保改进保证，在时期边界上更新效用函数（如引入对抗目标、进化评估器）。在编码任务中引入智能体作为评审的代码审查信号；在论文撰写中采用协同进化的写作与评审机制；在证明任务中使用对抗目标优化评审器。

Result: 编码任务：测试通过率超越SOTA，节省1.35x-1.72x token；论文撰写：协同进化作家达到1.78x-1.86x更高接受率；论文评审：基线评审对AI生成论文过度接受（高达人类1.91x），RQGM通过对抗目标纠正此偏差；证明分级：协同进化的分级器达到9%更高准确率。

Conclusion: RQGM通过引入非平稳效用进化，使自我改进智能体能够适应动态环境，在多个领域取得性能提升，并纠正了AI论文评审中的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens.
