Existing cold-start methods typically either train the backbone throughout the stream and compensate for semantic drift, or freeze a backbone after the first task, producing features biased toward the initial classes. These choices also create a computational tension: drift-compensation methods require repeated backbone training and increasingly expensive updates as the task horizon grows, while frozen-backbone methods are cheap but weak under cold start. We study a third option: a feature extractor that is never fit to image data at all. We propose CIRCLE, a class-incremental classifier built from fixed bidirectional two-dimensional reservoir features, adapted from BiRC2D for image classification, and streaming linear discriminant analysis heads. CIRCLE groups multiple random reservoir instantiations into feature ensembles and averages the softmax outputs of independent SLDA heads, yielding a tunable bias-variance tradeoff between richer random features and prediction-level ensembling. Because the feature extractor is fixed and the head admits streaming closed-form updates, CIRCLE performs sample-wise training without replay, task-boundary information, or backbone backpropagation. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, ImageNet-Subset, and ImageNet-1k, CIRCLE is competitive at 10-20 task splits and substantially outperforms strong CS-EFCIL baselines at 50, 100, and 500 task splits, while training much faster than trained-backbone drift-compensation methods. Ablations show that the BiRC2D-style extractor, SLDA head, and balanced feature/prediction ensembling each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [178] [Transformer-Based Classification of Bacterial Raman Spectra with LOOCV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27096)
*Jamile Mohammad Jafari,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于Transformer的模型在拉曼光谱分类中的表现，使用嵌套留一重复交叉验证框架，并与传统机器学习方法（PCA/ICA结合LDA、SVM、随机森林）进行比较。在包含5,417个单细胞光谱、6种细菌和9个独立测量重复的数据集上，Transformer持续取得最佳分类性能，显著优于传统方法；其学习到的潜在特征空间相比PCA/ICA具有更好的类别分离性，且能直接处理未预处理原始光谱并保持稳健表现。研究强调了重复感知验证对于模型实际评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱分类中，传统的机器学习方法（如PCA/ICA结合LDA、SVM、随机森林）存在局限性，而Transformer模型在光谱分类中的潜力尚未被系统评估，特别是在考虑测量重复变量的严谨验证框架下。

Method: 采用嵌套留一重复交叉验证框架，系统评估Transformer模型与传统机器学习方法（PCA-LDA、ICA-LDA、PCA-SVM、ICA-SVM、PCA-RF、ICA-RF）的性能。使用包含5,417个单细胞光谱、6种细菌种类和9个独立测量重复的数据集。

Result: Transformer在所有独立测试重复上持续取得最高分类性能，显著优于所有传统方法。其学习的潜在特征空间相比PCA和ICA表示具有更好的类别分离性。Transformer甚至能直接应用于未经预处理的原始拉曼光谱，并在不同测量重复间表现出稳健行为。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类中具有显著潜力，能够实现更稳健和准确的分类；同时强调了重复感知验证对于实现真实模型评估的重要性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently attracted increasing attention for Raman spectral classification. In this study, a transformer-based approach was systematically evaluated using a nested leave-one-replicate-out cross-validation framework and compared with conventional machine-learning pipelines combining PCA or ICA with LDA, SVM, and Random Forest classifiers. A bacterial Raman dataset comprising 5,417 single-cell spectra from six bacterial species and nine independent measurement replicates was used. The transformer consistently achieved the highest classification performance across independent test replicates and significantly outperformed all conventional approaches. Analysis of the learned latent feature space revealed improved class separation compared with PCA- and ICA-based representations. Furthermore, the transformer maintained superior performance when applied directly to raw Raman spectra without preprocessing, demonstrating robust behavior across measurement replicates. These findings highlight the potential of transformer-based models for robust Raman spectral classification and emphasize the importance of replicate-aware validation for realistic model evaluation.

</details>


### [179] [Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的重球动量Q-learning方法，证明了其收敛性，并给出了其比标准Q-learning收敛更快的理论条件，随后将该方法扩展到线性函数逼近场景，同样得到了收敛性和加速性保证。分析基于切换线性系统表示和联合谱半径工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning收敛速度有限，研究者希望利用重球动量（heavy-ball momentum）技术加速Q-learning，同时保证理论上的收敛性。

Method: 提出修正的重球动量Q-learning方法；利用切换线性系统（SLS）表示Q-learning算法，并通过联合谱半径（JSR）分析收敛性和加速条件；随后将相同构造扩展到线性函数逼近场景。

Result: 证明了修正的重球Q-learning在表格型和线性函数逼近设置下均收敛；识别出了比标准Q-learning收敛更快的理论条件；SLS视角为理解动量加速机制提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 重球动量可以加速Q-learning，SLS和JSR框架是分析这类加速算法的有效工具，为未来动量RL算法的理论分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: This paper proposes a corrected heavy-ball Q-learning method for reinforcement learning (RL) and establishes its convergence. It also identifies conditions under which the method is theoretically guaranteed to converge faster than standard Q-learning. The same construction is then extended to Q-learning with linear function approximation, where analogous convergence and acceleration statements are derived. The analysis is based on a switched linear system (SLS) representation of Q-learning algorithms and on the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the associated switching families. This SLS viewpoint is not commonly used in standard analyses of Q-learning, and it provides a complementary framework and new insight into how heavy-ball momentum can accelerate Q-learning.

</details>


### [180] [Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network with Inverse Propensity Score under Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27114)
*Haoran Zhang,Chuanpu Li,Yuxin Fu,Bin Tong,Guan Wang,Bo Zheng,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAUN（跨头注意力提升网络）和RA-IPS（鲁棒对抗逆概率加权）方法，分别解决提升建模中跨组相似性利用不足和未观测混杂下的去偏问题。实验表明CHAUN在QINI指标上相对提升最高25.6%，RA-IPS在未观测混杂下比标准IPS提升5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升建模面临两个核心挑战：一是如何灵活利用处理组和对照组之间的相似性来增强个体处理效应（ITE）的区分能力；二是在存在未观测混杂变量的情况下进行去偏估计。

Method: CHAUN通过共享特征嵌入和跨头注意力机制动态整合处理组与对照组的表征，增强组间关联建模；RA-IPS在约束不确定性集内对抗性优化倾向性权重，以减轻未观测变量带来的偏差。

Result: 在CRITEO-UPLIFT、LAZADA及生产电商数据集上，CHAUN的QINI分数相对提升最高达25.6%；RA-IPS在未观测混杂场景下比标准IPS提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 理论证明了已知真实倾向性分数下即使存在未观测混杂也能保证ITE可识别性；实验验证了所提方法在真实因果推断任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Uplift modeling, crucial for estimating individual treatment effects (ITE), faces dual challenges: flexibly leveraging inter-group similarity to enhance discriminative power and debiasing under unobserved confounding scenarios. In this paper, we propose the Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN) and Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS) method to address these limitations. CHAUN employs shared feature embeddings and cross-head attention mechanisms to dynamically integrate treatment-specific and control-specific representations, enhancing inter-group correlation modeling. Theoretically, we prove that access to the true propensity scores ensures ITE identifiability even with unobserved confounders. For practical scenarios lacking true propensity scores, RA-IPS adversarially optimizes propensity weights within constrained uncertainty sets to mitigate bias from unobserved variables. Experiments on public datasets (CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA) and a production e-commerce dataset demonstrate CHAUN's superiority over state-of-the-art uplift models, achieving relative improvements of up to 25.6% in QINI scores. RA-IPS further enhances robustness, outperforming standard IPS by 5.4% under unobserved confounding. The results validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in real-world causal inference tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了KAN、MLP和GNN在流体动力学代理建模（预测翼型表面压力分布）中的表现，发现KAN性能略逊于MLP，最佳性能由GNN实现但训练更耗时；KAN虽复杂度低、训练快，但存在训练不稳定且高度依赖超参数优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估KAN在流体动力学代理建模中的表现，并与MLP和GNN进行细致比较，探讨KAN是否在经典领域（如符号回归、通用机器学习、NLP、计算机视觉）之外也能超越MLP。

Method: 以预测亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布为任务，对比KAN、MLP和GNN三种模型在相同任务上的性能、复杂度和训练效率。

Result: KAN在预测压力系数和跨马赫数/攻角插值方面表现良好，但性能略逊于充分训练的MLP；GNN性能最佳但训练更长；KAN模型复杂度最低、训练最快，但存在训练不稳定性，性能高度依赖超参数优化。

Conclusion: 在流体动力学代理建模中，KAN并未展现出对MLP的明显优势，其性能可比但略逊于MLP；当前尚无证据表明KAN在该领域具有超越性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [182] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性张量神经网络子空间方法fTNN，用于求解有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题（分数阶泊松方程和时间依赖的分数阶对流扩散方程）。该方法采用几何自适应积分分裂策略，结合奇异感知基函数和时空可分离神经网络，在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分数阶神经网络方法（如fPINN）和蒙特卡洛方法在处理有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题时，面临精度不足、计算效率低、难以处理强边界奇异性和长时间模拟等挑战。因此需要发展一种高精度、高效率的确定性数值方法。

Method: (1) 采用几何自适应积分分裂策略，根据空间依赖的近场半径将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内远场和分析外远场三部分；(2) 奇异径向积分使用Gauss-Jacobi求积，规则径向积分使用Gauss求积，角度变量使用确定性角度求积，构成完全确定性的积分框架；(3) 构造边界奇异性感知的试函数，显式嵌入边界特征，提出两种自动选择主导指数和评估损失函数的策略；(4) 对时间依赖问题设计时空可分离神经网络，将时空残差分解为低维时间积分和空间积分之和，结合交替神经网络子空间优化策略进行训练。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在测试基准上达到了高精度，并且在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于现有的fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。

Conclusion: fTNN作为一种确定性的张量神经网络子空间方法，为分数阶拉普拉斯问题提供了一种高精度、高效率的数值求解方案，特别适用于具有强边界奇异性和需要长时间模拟的场景。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [183] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于抽样调查理论的模型辅助采样框架，通过引入辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，在71-86%的实验场景中提升性能，尤其在使用AdamW时能在约一半训练轮次内实现更好的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的小批量梯度估计存在噪声，现有方差缩减技术和自适应优化器虽能缓解但可能引入额外计算开销，因此需要更高效的梯度估计方法。

Method: 将小批量梯度解释为调查抽样中的样本估计，把数据集视为固定有限总体，引入辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器；该方法可轻松集成到现有优化器中而不改变其动态特性。

Result: 在合成数据集和6个基准数据集上的实验显示，71-86%的实验场景取得性能提升；与AdamW等动量优化器结合时，约一半训练轮次即可达到更好的泛化效果。

Conclusion: 模型辅助采样框架能有效桥接机器学习优化与调查抽样理论，构建更高效的梯度估计器，尤其对中等规模输入空间表现突出，且与现有优化器良好兼容。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [184] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，利用54,165份FDA医疗器械召回记录，同时预测召回严重性等级（I/II/III类）和根本原因类别（9类）。基于PubMedBERT文本表示与结构化特征嵌入，该模型在多任务设置中显著优于单任务基线，召回严重性预测准确率达0.963，风险排名与真实严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983），为可扩展的召回分类、监管决策及根因风险分析提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDA医疗器械召回记录数量庞大，现有研究大多单独处理召回发生预测或根因分析，而联合建模召回严重性与根因类别的工作十分有限。亟需一种自动化框架来支持召回后分类、严重性评估及根因解读。

Method: 基于54,165份openFDA召回记录（2002–2025.10），首先评估经典机器学习与Boosting模型；随后开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，融合PubMedBERT文本表示与产品代码、法规编号、医学专业等结构化特征的嵌入表示，同时预测召回严重性（3类）和根因类别（9类）。

Result: 单任务严重性预测中，LightGBM文本-表格配置达最优：准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线。模型风险排名与真实根因严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的召回后分类、监管决策辅助及基于模型的根因风险分析，为医疗器械召回管理提供自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [185] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对事件型时序图神经网络（ETGNNs）现有解释方法仅追踪部分信息流（从事件嵌入到输出）的局限，本文提出一种基于归一化相关度量（NRM）框架的全信息流归因方法，通过模块化解构处理架构复杂性，同时分析事件嵌入和事件诱导变量的信息路径，在合成与真实数据集上超越现有方法，生成更可解释的说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅关注从事件嵌入到输出的部分信息流，忽略了通过事件诱导变量（连接节点间交互、捕获长期时序依赖）的重要路径，导致解释不完整。

Method: 基于NRM框架，提出分析所有事件关联变量完整信息流的归因方法：1) 显式量化事件嵌入和事件诱导变量的信息贡献；2) 确保跨层潜变量可比性；3) 支持事件间高阶交互分析；4) 采用模块化解构程序处理ETGNN复杂架构。

Result: 在流行病追踪、社会动力学两个合成数据集以及政治事件网络真实数据集上的定性和定量实验表明，该方法一致优于现有解释方法，且生成更符合人类认知的解释。

Conclusion: 该方法通过完整建模ETGNN中所有信息流路径（特别是事件诱导变量），显著提升了解释的全面性和可解释性，为复杂时序图神经网络的归因分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [186] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了图神经网络（GNN）的设计空间、谱域与空域方法的统一原理、Weisfeiler-Leman层次下的表达能力，并在12个应用领域中分析了图结构何时值得计算开销、何时不必要。跨领域比较揭示了异质性、规模、时序动态等重复模式，并指出过度平滑、过度挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性是决定实际采用的关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络已成为处理关系数据的默认模型，但关键问题已从"消息传递是否有帮助"转变为"图结构在哪些场景下值得计算开销、哪些场景下不值得"。本文旨在系统性地组织该领域，建立统一的设计空间，分离真实收益与基线弱点造成的伪增益，为指导实际部署提供依据。

Method: 围绕单一设计空间组织研究，从共享第一原理推导谱域和空域方法，将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次联系起来，明确说明当前架构能分离和不能分离的内容。在12个应用领域（推荐系统、社交网络、知识图谱、语言模型集成、药物发现、分子性质学习、医疗健康、神经科学、计算机视觉、交通与城市计算、电力与可再生能源、无线与6G网络、欺诈与网络安全、工业预测、材料科学、气候建模）中，分别指定图构建选择及其成本，确定主导架构及原因，区分报告增益与基线弱点或不利数据划分带来的伪增益，并进行跨领域比较。

Result: 跨领域比较揭示了重复模式：异质性和规模几乎在大部分领域中削弱了同一模型；时序图始终比静态图更难处理；在公开排行榜上领先的架构很少是最终部署的架构。将过度平滑、过度挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性视为决定采用与否的约束条件，而非一份可有可无的检查清单。

Conclusion: 图结构在某些场景下值得计算开销，但并非普遍适用。异质性、规模、时序动态是模型失效的主要来源，实际部署中的决定因素包括鲁棒性、公平性、可解释性和分布偏移等约束条件。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [187] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FisherSketch，一种无需训练即可在共享词汇表的LLM家族（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中高效选择源模型的方法。它利用Fisher对齐的余弦等价形式，通过单次流式传递直接估计头Fisher对齐，避免了传统表示相似度指标（如CKA）在共享输出头设置下的不可识别性问题，且计算开销极低（16 KB任务签名、192 KB流状态）。实验验证了其在Llama-3.1-8B等模型上优于激活相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学序列领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组），多个候选语料库共享同一个分词器但预测目标不同，形成了"激活-暗区"机制：表示相似度指标缺乏标签条件误差几何假设时无效，而经典更新几何指标在词汇表规模下计算成本过高。

Method: 1. 证明共享输出头设置下，表示度量（如CKA）对迁移不可识别——模型可共享相同表示但头部更新正交。2. 核心恒等式：头Fisher对齐等于联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦，分解为激活、误差和耦合因子。3. FisherSketch：在单次流式传递中直接估计该余弦，使用K=128/256的Fisher对齐，16 KB任务签名（m=4096）和192 KB每任务流状态。

Result: FisherSketch在K=128/256时即可实现实用的Fisher对齐估计，存储开销极小（16 KB签名+192 KB流状态），可随模型哈希一起存储。在共享参数和内部层验证中，以及Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer转移实验中，FisherSketch在激活相似度无法区分任务时仍保持信息量。

Conclusion: FisherSketch不仅可用于源选择，其签名和边缘分布还可作为诊断工具，研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动。实验表明其在激活相似度失效时仍能提供有效迁移信息。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个可解的高维线性生成对抗网络（GAN）模型，研究了生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间的过程，将先前假设对角潜在协方差的分析推广到类别相关、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。证明微观随机训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由有效协方差驱动的确定常微分方程，并在匹配协方差特殊情形下给出了模式可解的稳定区间，揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可提升弱方向超越可学习阈值，而过强相关则会破坏恢复稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解GAN分析仅假设无条件信号与对角潜在协方差，无法处理类别依赖、相关和非零均值的潜在结构，缺乏对更真实场景中生成器学习动力学的理论理解。

Method: 采用高维线性生成器与二次能量判别器，将潜在协方差推广至结构化（类别相关、相关、非零均值）形式，利用高维极限下的随机过程收敛性得到确定性ODE，并在匹配协方差情形下进行稳定性分析。

Result: 所有异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学；得到模式可解的稳定区间，学习率与噪声水平决定学习边界；低秩相关提升弱方向可学习性，过强相关破坏稳定性；数值模拟和MNIST等实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 揭示了GAN训练中信号增强与稳定性权衡的机制，表明结构化潜在协方差可改善生成器与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐，为设计更高效的生成模型提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [189] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖焦虑/抑郁等情绪障碍、关系压力源触发因素及关系阶段信息。通过微调7个Transformer模型和5个大型语言模型，发现Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务最优（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测最优（Macro-F1=0.519），并揭示焦虑障碍与关系不确定性之间的强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康建模通常将精神疾病视为孤立现象，忽略了人际关系背景。为弥补这一不足，本文以异地恋Reddit帖子为切入点，旨在同时捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关关系触发因素。

Method: 构建RSPC语料库（1,799条Reddit帖子，由精神科医生标注诊断类别、关系压力源触发因素和关系阶段），并基准测试7个微调Transformer模型和5个大型语言模型，涵盖多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务中表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务中表现最强（Macro-F1=0.519），不同模型家族在任务上存在明显差异。发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系背景的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从以个体为中心向上下文感知的心理健康建模转变，能够捕捉困扰的社会性和时间动态。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [190] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个社交平台博彩广告操控性与欺骗性检测数据集（含人工标注与解释），分析了常见说服策略及其对用户心理健康的影响，并提出了可用于浏览器插件和网络爬虫的自动化检测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交平台上博彩应用广告激增，这些广告常使用误导性和欺骗性说服策略，可能鼓励冒险行为并影响用户心理健康。然而，由于缺乏公开标注数据集，针对操控性和欺骗性博彩广告的自动化检测研究十分有限。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交平台收集博彩相关广告，进行人工标注（包括分类标签和推理解释），分析常见说服策略及其对心理健康的影响，构建自动化检测框架。

Result: 构建了首个包含人工标注和解释的博彩广告操控性与欺骗性检测数据集，分析了常见说服策略，提出了可支持浏览器插件和网络爬虫等实际应用的检测框架。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架填补了该领域的研究空白，能够支持可解释的操控性广告检测方法，并为实际应用（如用户预警插件、监管自动化监测）奠定基础。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [191] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战时间序列预测中"越大越好"的主流观点，证明通过优化预处理（上下文长度、归一化、正则化、数据增强）而非扩大模型规模，就能在多数基准上超越Transformer/MLP/CNN等复杂架构。使用Ridge回归作为测试平台，发现了三个关键模式：最优回溯长度具有序列特异性且与预测步长呈非单调关系；局部归一化优于全局归一化；同一数据集内序列间超参数存在异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究趋向于使用越来越大的架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型），隐含假设是模型容量决定精度。本文质疑这一假设，试图证明通过精心调整数据预处理步骤，可以在远低于扩大模型规模的成本下达到相近甚至更好的性能。

Method: 以Ridge回归（具有闭式解和可解释权重）作为测试平台，在8个标准基准上系统搜索超参数空间，包括：上下文长度（回溯窗口）、局部归一化（学习归一化窗口的尾部分数）、正则化强度（λ）、数据扩增策略。通过超参数搜索直接读取最优设置并分析其统计规律。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度与预测步长呈非单调关系，幂律指数从ETTm2的+0.46到Exchange/Traffic的-0.19；(2) 局部归一化（学习到对上下文尾部而非整体进行归一化）几乎总是最优；(3) 同一数据集中不同序列的最优超参数存在差异。优化后的Ridge模型在6/8个基准上超越Transformer、MLP、CNN基线，并在大多数数据集-预测步长组合上超越先前线性预测器。

Conclusion: 预处理优化是比模型缩放更高效且更具解释性的改进路径；最优超参数可作为数据诊断工具，揭示大型模型隐式学习到的数据结构。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [192] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对科学机器学习中从观测数据学习控制方程这一基础问题，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了线性及非线性（Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场）ODE的可辨识性界限，并基于度量熵估计推导了样本复杂度上界，首次定量分析了此类学习任务的样本需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管从观测数据中发现控制方程是科学机器学习中的核心挑战，但现有文献缺乏对真实ODE能否被唯一且稳定地识别的理论条件，也没有对该学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，利用其捕捉最坏情况下不同初始条件导致的方程间分离程度，从而编码辨识问题的极小化极大结构；基于该度量推导ODE类别的度量熵估计，进而分析样本复杂度界限。

Result: 建立了从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz（Hölder）连续向量场的非线性ODE类别的可辨识性界限，精确刻画了两个不同方程可从解数据中被区分的条件；同时给出了样本复杂度上界，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的解观测数量。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离为微分方程的比较与辨识提供了理论合适的度量框架，填补了ODE可辨识性理论与样本复杂度分析的空白，为科学机器学习中从数据发现控制方程提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [193] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的RLAIF框架用于生成可移植的求职搜索查询，发现训练成功根本上取决于奖励塑造规范而非优化器选择，其中GRPO对虚假奖励信号尤其敏感，且训练时的奖励模型将性能增益膨胀了2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职搜索平台依赖低带宽查询接口，无法捕捉候选人资料的高维复杂性。现有方法缺乏能够抽象掉求职者特定标识符同时保留通用资格的便携式搜索查询生成方法。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架生成便携式求职搜索查询。通过综合实验分离优化机制与结构化奖励设计的影响，引入基于规则的确定性奖励下限来纠正对逐字复制行为分配的奖励，对比了RLOO、REINFORCE++和GRPO等优化器。

Result: 无评论家的优化器性能由奖励塑造主导，算法选择基本无关紧要。GRPO的组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号特别敏感，易被利用。引入基于规则的奖励下限后，跨族评估评审质量提升+0.147。训练时奖励模型将性能增益膨胀2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功根本上依赖于奖励塑造规范的执行，而非优化器的选择。GRPO的组相对优势归一化机制在对抗奖励黑客方面存在固有脆弱性。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [194] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，实现了板结构损伤的准确定位与尺寸估计，R²分别超过0.93和0.99。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型的实际部署受到标注实验数据稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据生成计算成本高的制约。

Method: 采用一维时域谱元模型生成大量合成数据用于预训练，通过卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习，结合前馈神经网络，并利用少量标注实验数据进行迁移学习。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN方案；损伤定位R²>0.93，损伤尺寸估计R²>0.99；在未见过的损伤场景中仍保持高预测精度。

Conclusion: 该方法准确、计算高效且实用，为实际GWSHM应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [195] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡（GEQ）框架与Blackwell可逼近性在算法上等价，进而与遗憾最小化、校准等经典在线学习框架等价，并建立了有约束与无约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的约简关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解GEQ框架在在线学习理论中的定位，厘清其与遗憾最小化等经典框架之间的关系。

Method: 通过构造双向黑盒约简：证明任何Blackwell可逼近问题可用GEQ oracle求解且渐近误差率不变，反之亦然；进而利用已知等价链将GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等框架关联。

Result: GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性算法等价，从而与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价；建立了GEQ的必要充分条件；实现了有约束/无约束决策集下不同GEQ概念间的约简；可将乐观性、强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移至GEQ。

Conclusion: GEQ并非孤立框架，而是在线学习经典体系中的一环，该等价关系为跨框架的理论迁移和算法设计提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k SAE架构兼容的稀疏正则化器（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比值惩罚），在不牺牲重建质量的前提下提升单义性，并发现硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Top-k SAE虽然避免了ℓ1惩罚的缺陷，但存在固定预算k无法适应输入复杂度、容易过拟合到训练时选择的k值等问题。为了解决这些问题，作者试图引入显式的稀疏正则化器来弥补架构稀疏性的不足。

Method: 在Top-k SAE的激活函数选取前，对未选中单元施加ℓ1惩罚，以及施加尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比值惩罚。两种惩罚仅作用于批次活跃单元（在批次内至少被Top-k算子选中一次的单元）。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化器均在不损失重建质量的前提下持续提升单义性。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少的隐单元上，使重建对推理时k值选择更鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探测的性能。

Conclusion: 硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的，两者结合可以有效提升SAE的表示质量和可解释性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [197] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入而非优化目标，通过迭代修正预测实现高精度求解，在四种PDE族上取得显著性能提升，并在分布偏移下展现强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，训练后无法自我修正约束违反和分布外泛化；混合方法虽通过梯度下降/Gauss-Newton步骤优化PDE残差，但继承了经典优化器的高计算成本和不稳定性，且在病态系统中残差最小化无法可靠反映重建精度。

Method: ENS将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入而非优化目标，使网络能读取自身误差的空间结构并学习迭代更新策略来逐步修正预测。

Result: 在四种PDE族上，ENS在绝大多数设置中达到最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上增益达10倍；避免混合方法的高计算成本；在分布偏移下（零样本参数变化、跨方程迁移）泛化能力最强，尤其在残差最小化最不可靠的病态区域优势最大。

Conclusion: 数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能不可靠；将残差作为网络输入而非优化目标是更有效的范式。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [198] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过用自回归建模替代基于流的范式，克服了归一化流在可逆性约束和计算成本上的局限，在分子平衡态采样任务上显著优于现有方法，并训练了一个1.32亿参数的可迁移模型Robin，在零样本能量误差上降低超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流，但流模型存在严格可逆性约束导致的表达力有限（离散时间）或似然计算昂贵（连续时间）的问题，亟需一种更具表达力和可扩展性的替代框架。

Method: 提出ArBG（自回归玻尔兹曼生成器），采用自回归建模框架替代流模型，绕过了流的拓扑约束；利用在大语言模型中有效的架构提升可扩展性，并支持推理时的顺序干预。

Result: 在所有基准测试上，ArBG显著优于基于流的模型，在大分子体系（如10残基的Chignolin）上表现尤为突出；基于ArBG训练的1.32亿参数可迁移模型Robin在8残基体系的零样本能量误差（E-W₂）上较之前最优方法降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归框架有效克服了流模型的固有限制，为分子平衡态采样提供了一个更具表达力、可扩展且性能更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [199] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励塑造方法，使LLM能够在没有标准答案的分数优化任务上进行强化学习训练，不仅提升了启发式竞赛分数，还意外地提升了精确求解任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖标准答案分配奖励，限制了其在标准答案未知场景（如启发式优化、分数型任务）中的应用。作者希望设计一种无需标准答案、仅依赖执行反馈（连续分数）就能训练LLM的强化学习框架。

Method: 提出RiVER（Ranking-induced VERifiable framework），核心包括：(1) 使用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督信号；(2) 识别出两个关键挑战——尺度主导（分数跨实例未校准导致策略更新失真）和频率主导（重复采样的次优解压倒罕见但更强的候选解）；(3) 通过校准奖励塑造解决上述问题：采用实例级比较、强调排名靠前的求解器、同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%的排名。更重要的是，仅在分数型任务上训练（无标准答案），RiVER在LiveCodeBench和USACO精确求解基准上分别提升2.4%和3.5%。而使用原始分数的基线仅提升ALE排名但无法迁移到精确求解任务。

Conclusion: 分数优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以作为有效的训练环境来提升通用编码能力，而无需依赖标准答案。这表明基于连续分数的RL训练可以超越其原始任务范围，迁移到精确求解任务上。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [MPE-Adam: Multi-Population Evolutionary Optimization with Adam Refinement for QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26670)
*Chi Quan Luu,Thai T. Vu,John Le*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出MPE-Adam，一种混合优化框架，将多群体进化搜索（全局探索）与Adam梯度优化（局部精化）相结合，用于量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的参数优化，在MaxCut问题上显著优于进化算法和SPSA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（如QAOA）中的参数优化面临高维、非凸参数空间和测量噪声的挑战。现有单阶段优化器无法区分全局探索和局部精化的不同角色，限制了互补策略的协同使用。

Method: 提出MPE-Adam混合优化框架，采用模块化设计：多群体进化搜索负责全局探索参数空间，Adam梯度优化负责局部收敛精化。该框架可作为量子软件管道的模块化组件。

Result: 在最多22节点的随机3-正则图MaxCut实例上，MPE-Adam相比纯进化算法和SPSA基线取得了更高的近似比和更低的方差，改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 结构化多阶段优化能同时提升量子应用的解质量和软件层面的灵活性，MPE-Adam作为混合优化框架是QAOA参数优化的有效方案。

Abstract: Parameter optimization is a central bottleneck in variational quantum algorithms such as the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). The classical optimizer must navigate a high-dimensional, non-convex parameter space under measurement noise. From a quantum software perspective, this process forms a multi-stage workflow: global exploration of the parameter space followed by local refinement within the hybrid quantum-classical loop. Most existing approaches, however, employ single-stage optimizers that do not separate these roles, which limits the use of complementary strategies. We propose MPE-Adam, a hybrid optimization framework that integrates multi-population evolutionary search for global exploration with Adam-based gradient refinement for local convergence. The method is structured as a modular component suitable for quantum software pipelines. We evaluate MPE-Adam on MaxCut instances generated from random 3-regular graphs with up to 22 nodes. The results show that MPE-Adam achieves higher approximation ratios and lower variance than evolutionary-only and SPSA-based baselines, with statistically significant improvements. These findings indicate that structured multi-stage optimization improves both solution quality and software-level flexibility in quantum applications.

</details>


### [201] [Generative Models on Analog Hardware with Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27294)
*Yu-Neng Wang,Sara Achour*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟交互系统（AIS），一种统一硬件可实现的动力学系统框架，通过时间变化分段参数和隐藏物理状态两种机制缩小与神经网络的表达能力差距，并开发Wasserstein GAN训练流程。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上，基于振荡器的AIS以4位稀疏架构实现FID 27.6和80.8，比此前最佳硬件可实现的模拟生成模型提升3-4倍，每张图像能耗23uJ，比数字基线低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟硬件（如耦合振荡器、模拟伊辛机）能以极低能耗自然求解微分方程，在低功耗生成建模方面具有吸引力，但现代生成模型假设灵活的软件定义动力学，而模拟硬件受限于固定的物理微分方程，表达能力有限，存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 提出Analog Interaction Systems (AIS)统一框架，引入两种硬件兼容机制：时间变化分段参数和隐藏物理状态；开发Wasserstein GAN训练流程，无需模型遵循特定轨迹；采用稀疏连接和低比特量化参数以实现实际部署。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上，基于振荡器的AIS（4位稀疏架构）分别达到FID 27.6和80.8，比此前最佳硬件可实现的模拟生成模型提升3-4倍；每张图像能耗仅23uJ，比数字基线低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过AIS框架和提出的硬件兼容机制，有效缩小了模拟硬件与神经网络生成模型之间的表达能力差距，在极低能耗下实现了有竞争力的生成质量，为低功耗生成建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Analog hardware platforms such as coupled oscillators and Analog Ising Machines naturally solve differential equations at a fraction of the energy cost of digital computation, making them attractive for low-power generative modeling, yet a fundamental mismatch exists: modern generative models assume flexible, software-defined dynamics, whereas analog hardware imposes fixed, physics-determined differential equations with limited approximation capacity. This paper introduces Analog Interaction Systems (AIS), a unified framework for hardware-implementable dynamical systems, and empirically characterizes their expressivity gap relative to neural network baselines. Two hardware-compatible mechanisms are proposed to narrow this gap - time-varying piecewise parameters and hidden physical states - and a Wasserstein GAN training procedure is developed to enable training of these models without requiring them to follow a specific trajectory. We characterize how area and power scale with connection density and precision, showing that sparse connectivity and low-bit-width quantized parameters are necessary for practical implementation, and estimate an energy cost of 23uJ per generated image for the chosen architecture, representing a 2-orders-of-magnitude improvement over digital baselines. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, our oscillator-based AIS achieves FID scores of 27.6 and 80.8, outperforming the best prior hardware-implementable analog generative models by 3-4x with a 4-bit sparse architecture.

</details>


### [202] [An Instruction Set Architecture for IMPLY-based Memristive Processing-in-Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27319)
*Liam Splittgerber,Fabian Seiler,Nima TaheriNejad*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于忆阻存内计算（IMC）的独立通用微控制器架构，专为超低功耗边缘设备设计。该架构采用IMPLY状态逻辑范式，实现了基于RV32I指令集扩展的指令系统，并设计了支持忆阻交叉阵列计算的新型寻址方案。通过仿真评估了能耗等电路级指标，并与传统微控制器进行了对比，最后通过智能环境传感器节点的应用案例验证了其功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CMOS微控制器受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈和易失性存储器的静态功耗泄漏，难以满足超低功耗边缘计算对严格能耗约束的需求。现有忆阻存内计算研究主要集中在作为协处理器的加速器，缺乏对独立通用IMC微控制器架构的探索。

Method: 采用IMPLY状态逻辑范式，基于RV32I标准设计指令集，提出支持忆阻交叉阵列中计算操作的新型寻址方案，并通过仿真评估电路级能耗指标。

Result: 通过仿真评估了所提架构的能耗等电路级指标，并与传统微控制器进行了对比；通过智能环境传感器节点的应用案例研究验证了设计的功能可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的独立通用IMC微控制器架构在超低功耗边缘计算领域具有可行性和潜在优势，能够有效克服传统CMOS微控制器的局限性。

Abstract: The push towards expanded ultra-low-power edge computing necessitates hardware capable of operating under extremely strict energy constraints. Traditional Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS) microcontrollers are fundamentally limited in this domain by the von Neumann bottleneck and by the static power leakage inherent to volatile memory. Memristive In-Memory Computing (IMC) offers a promising solution to these inefficiencies by unifying data storage and computation into a single non-volatile component. However, the State of the Art (SoA) predominantly focuses on accelerators designed to be a co-processor for data-intensive computation. This leaves the prospect of standalone, general-purpose IMC microcontroller architectures underexplored. This thesis proposes such an architecture tailored for ultra-low-power edge devices, alongside an instruction set closely derived from the RV32I standard. Using the IMPLY stateful logic paradigm, a complete implementation of the proposed instruction set is provided, and the novel addressing schema required to support computation in the memristive crossbar array is described as well. Then, the energy use and other circuit-level metrics of the proposed architecture are evaluated through simulation and compared against those of traditional microcontrollers. Finally, the functional viability of the design is demonstrated through an application case study, describing how the proposed design could be used in an intelligent environmental sensor node.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Random Walk on Bézier Curves for Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26714)
*Jinpeng Wang,Xingguo Xu,Yujing Sun,Jiguang Yu,Kaichen Ouyang,Yuansheng Gao*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出贝塞尔行走进化(BWE)——一种基于几何驱动的元启发式优化框架。BWE通过贝塞尔曲线建模结合距离感知随机游走生成自适应搜索轨迹，利用曲线阶次动态调整实现从全局探索到局部开发的平滑过渡。在CEC2017和CEC2022的41个基准函数(10-100维)及5个工程设计问题上，BWE优于7种经典和6种最先进优化器(包括L-SHADE和CMA-ES)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元启发式优化中探索与开发的平衡仍是核心挑战，现有自然启发式方法缺乏可解释性，需要一种更可控、可解释的搜索机制。

Method: 提出BWE框架：将进化搜索重构为决策空间中的自适应轨迹构建，核心是(1)贝塞尔曲线建模生成拓扑引导的搜索轨迹，(2)距离感知随机游走机制，(3)自适应曲线阶次控制——高阶曲线利用多个种群控制点增强多样性(全局探索)，低阶曲线生成近线性轨迹提升收敛效率(局部开发)。

Result: 在CEC2017和CEC2022的41个基准函数(10-100维)上，BWE在整体性能和可扩展性上优于7种经典和6种最先进优化器(包括L-SHADE和CMA-ES)；在5个约束工程设计问题上进一步验证了实际应用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BWE通过几何驱动的自适应搜索轨迹为探索-开发平衡提供了可解释的替代方案，在基准测试和实际工程问题上均表现出优越的性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Balancing exploration and exploitation remains a central challenge in metaheuristic optimization. To address this issue, this paper proposes Bézier Walk Evolution (BWE), a geometry-driven optimization framework that reformulates evolutionary search as adaptive trajectory construction in the decision space. BWE integrates Bézier curve modeling with a distance-aware random walk mechanism to generate topology-guided search trajectories. By adaptively varying the curve order during evolution, the proposed method enables a smooth transition from diversified global exploration to refined local exploitation. Higher-order Bézier curves leverage multiple population-derived control points to enhance search diversity, while lower-order curves generate near-linear trajectories to improve convergence efficiency. This adaptive geometric search mechanism provides an interpretable alternative to conventional nature-inspired designs. Extensive experiments on 41 benchmark functions from the CEC2017 and CEC2022 suites, spanning dimensions from 10 to 100, show that BWE achieves strong overall performance and favorable scalability compared with 7 classical and 6 state-of-the-art optimizers, including L-SHADE and CMA-ES. Additional evaluations on five constrained engineering design problems further demonstrate the practical applicability and robustness of BWE.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Query Cost Model Calibration in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26385)
*Qihan Zhang,Mengyuan Li,Ibrahim Sabek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对机密虚拟机（如AMD SEV-SNP）中分析查询性能下降的问题，本文提出了一种轻量级的CVM感知成本校准方法，通过建模数据移动和RMP相关转换两种主要开销来源，显著缩小了KVM与CVM之间的性能差距，最高可恢复48%的性能，甚至在某些工作负载上超越KVM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机中分析查询存在显著性能开销，但先前研究仅停留在基准测试层面，未对查询优化器进行优化，原因在于查询优化器仍使用基于KVM（非加密虚拟机）的成本假设，导致硬件与软件不匹配。

Method: 提出轻量级CVM感知成本校准方法，建模两种主要的优化器层面开销来源：数据移动（Data Movement）和RMP（Reverse Map Table）相关转换，并使用优化器已有的简单物理代理（Proxies）来表示这些开销。

Result: 校准方法显著缩小KVM与CVM之间的性能差距，最高恢复48%的性能，部分工作负载甚至超越KVM基线。

Conclusion: 通过CVM感知的成本校准，可以有效解决机密虚拟机中分析查询的性能退化问题，实现接近甚至优于非加密VM的性能表现。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of Confidential Computing, running databases in confidential virtual machines (CVMs) such as AMD SEV-SNP has become an attractive way to protect sensitive cloud data with minimal changes to legacy DBMSs. However, analytical queries in such CVMs often suffer substantial overhead, and prior database work has largely stopped at benchmarking these slowdowns rather than optimizing them. We show that this problem stems from a hardware-software mismatch: query optimizers still rely on KVM-oriented (non-encrypted VM) cost assumptions that no longer hold in CVMs. To address this, we propose a lightweight CVM-aware cost calibration. It models two dominant sources of optimizer-facing overhead: data movement and RMP-related translation using simple physical proxies already available to the optimizer. Experiments show that the calibration significantly narrows the KVM/CVM performance gap, recovering up to 48 percent performance and even outperforming the KVM baseline on some workloads.

</details>


### [205] [3D Spatial Pattern Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26465)
*Nicole R. Schneider,Avik Das,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Abhijeet Ghodgaonkar,Hanan Samet,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文首次将空间模式匹配从2D扩展到3D，提出了通用的问题定义，设计了支持距离关系的子图匹配算法，并发布了合成数据集和德国汉堡真实3D建筑数据集作为基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间模式匹配方法局限于2D笛卡尔平面，无法处理具有高度信息的真实世界实体（如建筑物），存在显著局限性。

Method: 提出3D空间模式匹配的通用定义，设计基于距离关系的子图匹配算法，构建合成数据集和真实3D建筑数据集（德国汉堡）进行实验。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上测试了所提子图匹配算法，提供了基准结果供后续方法参考。

Conclusion: 将空间模式匹配扩展到3维是可行且有必要的，所提方法可作为未来研究的基线。

Abstract: Spatial pattern matching is the process of matching query entities and constraints with database entities and relations. It has many applications, including similar region search, housing market search, landmark search, and road network matching. To our knowledge, all existing spatial pattern matching approaches frame the problem in a 2 dimensional space, where entities lie in a cartesian plane and relationships defined between them are contained in 2 dimensions. However, this problem framing has significant limitations when searching for real world entities that have height in addition to position. To address this limitation, we extend spatial pattern matching to 3 dimensions and provide a generalized definition of the problem. We describe a subgraph matching algorithm capable of resolving 3D spatial patterns over distance relations and release two 3D spatial pattern matching datasets, one synthetic and one containing real 3D building data from the city of Hamburg, Germany. We test our subgraph matching algorithm on both datasets and present results as a baseline for future methods to build upon.

</details>


### [206] [EcoTable: Cost-effective Table Integration in Data Lakes for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26613)
*Yuhui Wang,Jinqi Liu,Chengliang Chai,Hangyu Zhao,Yuhao Deng,Yuyu Luo,Xin Tang,Ye Yuan,Guoren Wang,Fengjin Wang,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EcoTable是一个基于自然语言的自动数据集成框架，利用LLM的语义理解和推理能力，通过图结构表示搜索空间、两阶段模式链接、Steiner树搜索和LLM代码生成，在4个真实数据集上相比SOTA准确率提升30%以上，LLM调用成本降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ETL依赖数据工程师预定义目标数据库模式和构建复杂数据集成管道，无法灵活支持各种分析需求和未知查询，且预定义模式难以满足未知查询的表格式或连接关系。

Method: EcoTable提出基于图的搜索空间表示（节点为表、边权重由轻量深度学习模型估计连接概率），包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于两阶段模式链接的表识别层，根据用户查询识别相关表；(2) 基于图验证层，将问题建模为Steiner树搜索，发现重要连接路径（含数据变换和桥接表）；(3) 使用LLM生成变换代码实现表连接的变换层。同时通过图结构和轻量模型降低LLM推理的规模和成本。

Result: 在4个真实基准数据集、200+查询上的实验结果显示，EcoTable相比最先进基线方法准确率提升30%以上，同时LLM调用成本降低5倍。

Conclusion: EcoTable是首个基于自然语言的数据集成框架，通过图结构搜索空间与LLM结合，有效解决了传统ETL的灵活性问题，在提升集成准确率的同时大幅降低了LLM的调用成本。

Abstract: The diverse formats of CSV and Parquet files in data lakes pose a significant challenge to traditional ETL, which relies on data engineers to pre-define a target database schema and build a complex pipeline for data integration. Moreover, with this approach, the integrated data often cannot support various analytical needs, as the predefined schema does not necessarily satisfy the table format or join relationships required to answer unforeseen queries. To address this, we propose EcoTable, the first natural language-based data integration framework. Given a set of user-specified natural language queries, EcoTable automatically integrates the tables into a form that adequately supports the corresponding SQL queries. EcoTable achieves this by leveraging the semantic understanding and complex reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Moreover, EcoTable addresses the scalability and cost issues introduced by expensive LLM inferences with a set of novel ideas. First, EcoTable introduces a graph to represent the overall search space, where nodes represent tables and edges carry weights indicating join likelihood produced by a lightweight deep learning model. On top of this graph data structure, EcoTable designs three components to achieve our goal: (1) the table identification layer aims to identify relevant tables via a two-stage schema linking based on user queries; (2) the graph-based validation layer aims to discover significant join paths, including necessary data transformations and bridging tables, by modeling the problem as Steiner tree searches; and (3) the table transformation layer generates transformation code to implement the joins using LLMs. We construct 4 real-world benchmark datasets with more than 200 queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EcoTable outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines, increasing accuracy by more than 30% and cutting LLM invocation costs by 5 times.

</details>


### [207] [BtrLog: Low-Latency Logging for Cloud Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27051)
*Maximilian Kuschewski,Lam-Duy Nguyen,Matthias Jasny,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis,Muhammad El-Hindi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BtrLog是一个可重用的云日志服务，通过SSD-backed仲裁节点实现低延迟持久追加，并异步归档到对象存储以降低成本，在保证持久性的同时比EBS延迟更低、事务吞吐量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据库系统中WAL依赖远程存储，现有方案存在不足：远程块存储（如EBS）延迟高且成本高，对象存储延迟更高不适合OLTP，专用日志后端又通常专有且不可复用。

Method: BtrLog将日志记录复制到SSD-backed日志节点的仲裁集上，仅需一次网络往返，减少慢节点对提交延迟的影响；日志节点异步地将记录归档到对象存储作为大段写入，避免在关键写入路径上增加延迟。

Result: BtrLog比EBS延迟更低，集成到DBMS后实现了更高的端到端事务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BtrLog提供了一种低延迟、低成本、可复用的云日志服务方案，适用于常见的单写入器架构，解决了现有云存储方案在WAL持久化上的性能与成本权衡问题。

Abstract: Cloud database systems cannot rely on instance-local disks for write-ahead logging (WAL) durability, forcing WAL onto remote storage. Existing options are unsatisfying: remote block storage like EBS is easy to adopt but adds substantial write latency and cost, while object storage offers excellent durability and low storage cost but is impractical for OLTP due to high latency and per-append cost. Many cloud-native databases, therefore, depend on purpose-built logging backends, which are typically proprietary and tightly coupled to engine-specific replication and recovery protocols, limiting reuse. We present BtrLog, a reusable cloud logging service that combines low-latency durable appends with low-cost archival for the common single-writer architecture. BtrLog replicates log records across a quorum of SSD-backed log nodes in a single network round trip, reducing sensitivity to stragglers in commit latency. To minimize storage cost, log nodes archive records to object storage as large segments, which are written asynchronously and off the latency-critical write path. In our evaluation, BtrLog achieves lower latency than EBS and enables higher end-to-end transaction throughput when integrated into a DBMS.

</details>


### [208] [Understanding Domain-Aware Distribution Alignment in Budgeted Entity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27342)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Gregory Goren,Roee Shraga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了低资源、领域感知的实体匹配方法BEACON，通过一系列针对性实验，分析不同算法选择和数据可用性条件对其性能的影响，深入探讨了分布对齐的作用和BEACON框架的行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源领域感知的实体匹配方法虽表现优异，但在不同数据约束和监督水平下的实际行为尚不明确，缺乏对其性能影响因素的深入理解。

Method: 以BEACON框架为研究对象，设计一系列针对性实验，系统评估不同算法选择和数据可用性条件下的性能变化。

Result: 揭示了分布对齐在BEACON框架中的作用，以及不同数据约束条件对模型性能的具体影响。

Conclusion: 为低资源、领域感知的实体匹配方法在实际应用中的行为提供了深入见解，有助于指导该方法在不同场景下的选择与优化。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM) is a core operation in the data integration pipeline, where records from different sources are compared to determine whether they refer to the same real-world entity. Recent work has incorporated domain information and low-resource learning techniques to better adapt EM systems to realistic settings. While these approaches have demonstrated strong performance, it remains unclear how they behave under varying data constraints and levels of supervision in practice. In this paper, we investigate a state-of-the-art method for low-resource, domain-aware EM--BEACON--and study how its performance is affected by different algorithmic choices and data availability conditions. We conduct a series of targeted experiments to evaluate these variations, providing deeper insight into the role of distribution alignment and the behavior of the BEACON framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [MIRAGE: Protecting against Malicious Image Editing via False Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26199)
*Anshul Nasery,Ramnath Kumar,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRAGE 是一种通过对抗性扰动触发商业图像编辑系统的预生成安全审核机制，从而阻止图像被任意编辑的方法，在黑盒 API 上实现了超过 88% 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像免疫方法需要访问模型权重和编辑提示词，无法防御 GPT-Image、Gemini Flash Image、Grok Imagine 等强大的商业闭源图像编辑系统。

Method: MIRAGE 利用商业图像编辑系统的预生成安全审核模块这一攻击面，对图像添加对抗性扰动，使其在开源嵌入模型和审核模型的表示空间中与违反政策的概念对齐，从而触发系统自动拒绝编辑请求。

Result: 在多个闭源图像编辑 API 上评估，MIRAGE 实现了超过 88% 的防御成功率。

Conclusion: MIRAGE 是一种简单、与提示词无关且有效的图像免疫方法，为保护个人图像免受未经授权的 AI 编辑提供了实用途径。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-powered image editing systems raises serious concerns because it allows personal images to be arbitrarily manipulated at scale, with minimal effort, and a lower barrier to entry. Prior work on image immunization adds imperceptible perturbations to an image to protect against unauthorized manipulations. However, these methods usually require access to the model weights and the image manipulating prompt. This significantly limits their use, especially against powerful commercial image-editors such as GPT-Image, Gemini Flash Image (Nano Banana), and Grok Imagine. To address this, we take a system-level view of the problem and identify a previously unexplored attack surface common to all major commercial image editing systems: pre-generation safety moderation.Rather than disrupting the generative model itself, we propose to immunize images by causing these moderation classifiers to flag images as policy-violating, triggering an automatic refusal regardless of the editing prompt. We operationalize this by adding adversarial perturbations to align our image to policy-violating concepts in the representation space of an ensemble of open-source embedding and moderation models. We call our method MIRAGE, which stands for Moderation Induced Resistance Against Generative Editing. We evaluate MIRAGE against multiple closed-source image editing APIs and demonstrate success rates of more than 88%. Our approach is simple, prompt-agnostic, and effective, offering a practical path towards protecting personal images from unauthorized AI-powered editing.

</details>


### [210] [Data Facts: A Metadata Schema for Structured Data Exchange in the NANDini Multi-Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26211)
*Jin Gao,Maria Gorskikh,Pradyumna Chari,Brittany Box,Mukul Kemla,Pratik Behera,Abhishek Mehta,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Data Facts——一种轻量级JSON元数据架构，作为NANDini自主智能体生态系统的核心组件，通过标准化数据结构化描述（含访问层级、端点、TTL和SHA-256校验和）及三层安全流水线（JWT认证、网关授权、A2A凭证委托），实现智能体间自主、安全、可验证的数据交换。实验表明，数据访问使决策准确率从35.2%提升至100%，TTL机制将过期数据错误从37.6%降至8.8%，校验和实现100%篡改检测，安全流水线成功阻断全部46次伪造攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议（如NANDA、A2A、MCP）仅解决智能体身份发现和通信问题，缺乏结构化数据交换机制；而企业级数据共享框架（IDS-RAM、Gaia-X、Ocean Protocol）依赖人在回路治理，无法支持自主、实时的智能体间数据交互。因此需要一种轻量级、去中心化的数据结构化描述方案。

Method: 设计Data Facts JSON元数据架构，通过单个指针data_facts_url嵌入Agent Facts注册记录，编码数据集身份、访问层级（公开/半私有/私有）、端点、TTL及SHA-256校验和；对私有/半私有数据实现JWT认证、能力范围网关授权、A2A凭证委托的三层安全流水线。

Result: 840次决策评估中，数据知情智能体准确率100%（vs. 无数据访问的35.2%，p<0.001）；TTL执行将过期数据错误从37.6%降至8.8%；校验和在所有注入率下实现100%篡改检测；安全流水线阻断全部46次伪造尝试，零数据泄露。

Conclusion: Data Facts有效弥合了智能体发现与数据访问之间的鸿沟，为自主智能体生态系统提供了轻量、安全、可验证的数据交换基础设施，在准确性、数据新鲜度、完整性保护和安全性方面均有显著提升。

Abstract: NANDini (Networked Agents Natural Distillation of Interconnected Nodal Intelligence) envisions an automated ecosystem where intelligent agents independently create, process, and exchange data to drive decisions at scale. Realizing this vision requires infrastructure beyond agent discovery and communication: agents must be able to advertise, evaluate, and verify the datasets they hold.
  Current protocols, including NANDA for federated registry and A2A and MCP for inter-agent messaging, address identity and communication but provide no mechanism for structured data exchange. Existing enterprise data-sharing frameworks, such as IDS-RAM, Gaia-X, and Ocean Protocol, assume human-in-the-loop governance that is incompatible with autonomous, real-time agent interactions.
  We introduce Data Facts, a core NANDini concept: a lightweight JSON metadata schema that bridges agent discovery and data access through a single pointer, `data_facts_url`, added to an existing Agent Facts registry record. The linked document encodes dataset identity, access tier, whether public, semi-private, or private, endpoint, a time-to-live for freshness validation, and a SHA-256 integrity checksum.
  For private and semi-private data, we implement a three-layer security pipeline: JWT authentication, capability-scoped gateway authorization, and an A2A credential delegation protocol. Across 840 decision-making evaluations, data-informed agents achieve 100% accuracy versus 35.2% without data access (p < 0.001); TTL enforcement reduces stale-data errors from 37.6% to 8.8%; checksum verification achieves 100% corruption detection at all injection rates; and the security pipeline blocks all 46 forgery attempts with zero data leakage.

</details>


### [211] [CyberChainBench: Can AI Agents Secure Smart Contracts Against Real-World On-Chain Vulnerabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26216)
*Jintao Huang,Fengqing Jiang,Radha Poovendran,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberChainBench是一个评估LLM智能体在智能合约安全方面能力的基准，涵盖漏洞检测、漏洞利用和补丁生成三个互补任务，基于541个真实DeFi攻击事件构建，并通过链上评估环境进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全基准缺乏对真实链上环境、端到端评估和结构化ground truth的支持，无法全面评估智能体在漏洞检测、利用与修复三个维度的能力。

Method: 从DeFiHackLabs收集541个真实攻击事件，覆盖9条EVM链，通过Harbor搭建隔离评估环境，智能体在历史链上分叉中读取代码、追踪交易并验证漏洞利用，每个案例锚定特定区块并包含结构化ground truth。

Result: 最佳配置在检测上得分37.5%，利用上得分43.7%，但补丁生成仅23.4%；Codex with GPT-5.5智能体在200个案例中实现了5740万美元的漏洞利用利润，每案例成本2.39美元。

Conclusion: 智能合约安全任务存在明显的难度梯度：漏洞利用相对容易，检测次之，补丁生成最为困难，当前LLM智能体在补丁生成方面性能显著不足。

Abstract: We present CyberChainBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents on smart contract security across three complementary tasks: vulnerability detection, exploit generation, and patch synthesis. Built from 541 real-world exploit incidents from DeFiHackLabs spanning 9 EVM chains, the benchmark provides end-to-end on-chain evaluation where agents interact with historical blockchain state through isolated evaluation environments orchestrated by Harbor, using tools to read code, trace transactions, and validate exploits on mainnet forks. Each case is anchored to a specific block and includes structured ground truth covering vulnerability type, localization, and attacker profit. Exploits are graded by economic impact on historical forks; patches are validated by replaying historical attacks and legitimate transactions as fail-to-pass test oracles on a proxy-upgradeable subset. We define a five-type vulnerability taxonomy and evaluate multiple agent--model configurations. Results reveal a clear difficulty gradient: the best configuration scores 37.5% on detection, 43.7% on exploitation, but only 23.4% on patching, with the top agent (Codex with GPT-5.5) realizing \$57.4M in total exploit profit across the 200-case exploit set at a cost of $2.39 per case.

</details>


### [212] [Expecting (Targeted Ads)? Network Analysis of User Health Data Leakage in Fertility Tracking Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26276)
*Yeeun Jo,Shahanaasree Sivakumar,Mahnoor Jameel,Camille Cobb,Adam Bates,Brad Reaves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究对20款安卓生育跟踪应用进行网络测量，发现5款应用存在用户健康数据泄露（包括显性泄露和通过上下文广告的隐性泄露），同时也有应用采用广告变现模式却未泄露数据，为用户隐私担忧提供了技术依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注生育跟踪应用中的人为因素，但缺乏对应用数据处理技术层面的分析，特别是与第三方广告服务共享用户数据的机制尚不清楚。
